# DREDGE English Yarn dialogue, attributed

**Source:** user-supplied `localization-string-tables-english(en)_assets_all.bundle`, `Yarn_en` table. **Corpus:** 2,607 string entries. **Ordering:** original string-table row order within each group, not validated runtime scene order. The companion `catalog.json` identifies addressable assets but does not by itself resolve untagged speech or Yarn branch sequence.

A `*_NAME_KEY:` prefix is explicit speaker evidence and is removed only from the readable text; the original line is retained in the CSV and JSON. Untagged lines are conservatively grouped as narrator, player option, action, system/editor, or unattributed. An **inferred** or **unknown** assignment is not a confirmed in-game speaker. This export also retains non-dialogue Yarn narration and choices to avoid silently dropping strings. Color markup `[C0]` etc. is preserved. Characters introduced as unknown are not silently merged into their revealed scene identities.

## Collector (107 strings)

**row 0046; string ID 7217358294814738; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> So, will you allow me to fit your vessel with dredging equipment? I'll need a few hours.

**row 0047; string ID 7217358294814741; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> An excellent decision. I'll prepare the equipment immediately.

**row 0048; string ID 7217358294814742; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I would encourage you to [C0]ask around the towns about shipwrecks or other strange occurrences[/C0] - anything that might be worth investigating.

**row 0049; string ID 7217358294814743; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm. Return when you change your mind.

**row 0090; string ID 7657931879288840; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Well? You have something, don't you? Show me.

**row 0092; string ID 7657931879288842; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Is this all? Where is the lock?

**row 0094; string ID 7657931879288844; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> It seems...the pieces we're looking for have travelled further than I anticipated. Much further, I suspect.

**row 0095; string ID 7657931879288847; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Now go. We have more relics to uncover.

**row 0163; string ID 29671733545996288; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Do not bother me with common clutter. I hear that the [C0]Trader[/C0] in [C0]Little Marrow[/C0] deals with such trivialities.

**row 0169; string ID 42729481881964546; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Return when you have something.

**row 0299; string ID 66638564575744010; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> In that case, allow me to aid your travels.

**row 0312; string ID 66638564575744023; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> And you have something for me.

**row 0314; string ID 66638564575744026; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> These patterns are...unmistakeable...even after all this time. This [C3]Handkerchief[/C3] comes from an old ship I've been searching for. It sank many years ago.

**row 0315; string ID 66638564575744027; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> It carried items of great importance. As you can imagine, retrieving them is proving to be challenging. That's where you come in.

**row 0316; string ID 66638564575744028; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> My proposition is this: I will outfit your vessel with equipment that will let you [C3]dredge the wrecks and dark depths[/C3].

**row 0317; string ID 66638564575744029; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Find me these [C3]lost relics[/C3] and I will [C1]reward you in ways beyond all imagining[/C1].

**row 0318; string ID 66638564575744030; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> You need not give up your fishing career. I'll merely need a few hours with your vessel to make the necessary alterations. What do you say?

**row 0482; string ID 73989035164618752; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> So you've returned. I wasn't sure you were coming back.

**row 0484; string ID 73989035210756097; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> You found this at the cliffs, didn't you?

**row 0486; string ID 73989035210756099; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, excellent.

**row 0488; string ID 73989035210756101; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Curious.

**row 0490; string ID 73989035210756104; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Now, there are yet more relics to find. Keep looking.

**row 0491; string ID 73989035210756105; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> This should make future return journeys a little more swift. Allow me.

**row 0495; string ID 73989035210756113; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> And with that, our collection is complete...I must admit, I am surprised. I did not think you had the determination for such a job.

**row 0496; string ID 74259460765323264; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> What is it?

**row 0497; string ID 74259460782100480; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else?

**row 0501; string ID 74259460786294784; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]You should ask around the towns in The Marrows[/C0]. Somebody is sure to know something.

**row 0502; string ID 74259460786294785; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> My...intuition tells me that the current may have carried some debris to the [C0]South-East[/C0], towards [C0]Gale Cliffs[/C0]. Make that your priority.

**row 0503; string ID 74259460786294786; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> The reef at [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0] may have caught and accumulated some wreckage. Search around that area.

**row 0504; string ID 74259460786294787; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I can imagine all manner of detritus swallowed by the silty muck of [C0]Twisted Strand's[/C0] channels. It's worth investigating.

**row 0505; string ID 74259460786294788; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I loathe to suggest it, but the abyssal plain on the approach to [C0]Devil's Spine[/C0] is a likely candidate. See what you can find.

**row 0507; string ID 74259460786294791; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> He approaches! Drawing closer with...an incessant ticking. You have the pocket watch.

**row 0508; string ID 74259460786294792; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> The relics we're looking for are: a [C3]ring[/C3], a [C3]necklace[/C3], a [C3]watch[/C3], and a [C3]music box[/C3] and [C3]key[/C3].

**row 0509; string ID 76154025294237696; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I'll mark a location on your [C0]Map[/C0] where you can begin your search.

**row 0510; string ID 76154025311014912; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I've already marked a location on your [C0]Map[/C0] where you can begin your search.

**row 0665; string ID 86974217865842688; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We're certainly making excellent progress now. And - I know what you're thinking.

**row 0666; string ID 86974217865842689; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> An item like this would fetch a fortune at any auction. But for us...its value transcends material wealth.

**row 0669; string ID 86974217865842693; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We must keep searching. I've been thinking more on where we might find another...

**row 0918; string ID 116750453726957573; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> ...

**row 0920; string ID 116750453726957575; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> ...

**row 0929; string ID 116750453726957584; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Regardless. You are due a reward.

**row 0930; string ID 116750453726957585; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Quite some time. I used to be a fisherman, as was my father before. But two decades ago I took up residence here. I needed to focus on...other endeavours.

**row 0931; string ID 116750453726957586; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I found that this island has a certain strength to it - those dark basalt columns stretching downwards into the deep.

**row 0932; string ID 116750453726957587; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Why, collecting them of course. An investment for times to come.

**row 0933; string ID 116750453726957588; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I largely don't...but I read the seas. I see the swells that give shape to the formless. I hear the waves that whisper secrets to their witnesses.

**row 0934; string ID 116750453726957589; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> And I speak to the currents that hold a memory of all that they claim.

**row 0935; string ID 116750453726957590; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Yes.

**row 0938; string ID 116750453726957593; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> An old friend.

**row 0939; string ID 116750453726957594; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I am what was fractured from you on that day you dredged the depths. I am what could be. I am your breathless self.

**row 0940; string ID 116750453726957595; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Yes. She will rise from the deep. The book describes it.

**row 0941; string ID 116750453726957596; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We almost have all of the pieces. Just one more.

**row 0942; string ID 116750453726957597; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Which book? This one here?

**row 0944; string ID 116750453726957599; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> There's really not much to be said of "Histories of The Gray Isles". It's quite a chore.

**row 0947; string ID 116750453726957602; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Gladly.

**row 0948; string ID 116750453726957603; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Play dumb? Your hypocrisy is wearying. I suppose we must go through this rigmarole yet again.

**row 0950; string ID 116750453726957605; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Go ahead. Ask us.

**row 0954; string ID 116750453726957609; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Don't you remember? You were there.

**row 0956; string ID 116750453726957611; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> So was she.

**row 0960; string ID 116750453726957615; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> You wanted to forget. Begged me.

**row 0962; string ID 116750453726957618; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> This is what you asked for.

**row 0966; string ID 116750453726957622; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> You won't change a thing. The book is ours.

**row 0970; string ID 116750453726957626; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> What can you possibly hope to achieve?

**row 0974; string ID 116750453726957630; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> ...

**row 0975; string ID 116750453726957631; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> You're pathetic.

**row 0977; string ID 116750453726957633; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Understand this, plainly, before you sink us into despair. I know how to bring her back. It's all right there on the pages; and now you've seen its power first hand.

**row 0978; string ID 116750453726957634; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We wield the power to release her from her slumber! We can undo everything...undo what you did!

**row 0979; string ID 116750453726957635; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> The book holds power from the deep. I merely...extended its powers towards you. Is there one in particular you wish to know more about?

**row 0980; string ID 116750453726957636; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Are there other powers you wish to know more about?

**row 0986; string ID 116750453726957642; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> The words of haste are instant and cruel. They will impart a terrible speed to your vessel.

**row 0987; string ID 116750453726957643; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Be warned, however - it may put a great deal of stress on your engines.

**row 0988; string ID 116750453726957644; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Use of these words will induce a trance-like state. While in this state time will pass in the blink of an eye. When it is over, you will find yourself here.

**row 0989; string ID 116750453726957645; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> As always, I caution against overusing this. Selectively and strategically utilize it when you need it most.

**row 0990; string ID 116750453726957646; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> The banishing words will provide you a temporary protection from...most things. If spoken swiftly enough, they can be used defensively.

**row 0991; string ID 116750453726957647; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Choose your moment carefully. The more they are spoken, the more they are...understood.

**row 0992; string ID 116750453726957648; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> This phrase instantly culls the closest shoal of fish, readying them for swift reaping. It can be heard from a great distance, so you need not be close to your victims.

**row 0993; string ID 116750453726957649; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> But the population is unlikely to recover from such vile trauma. Use it sparingly.

**row 1033; string ID 117037914600427520; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> An excellent find. It demands pride of place in my collection.

**row 1035; string ID 117037914600427522; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> And I suppose you'll be demanding recompense as well...

**row 1038; string ID 117037914600427525; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We're...so close. One more remains.

**row 1043; string ID 117385907199463427; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> The ocean no longer wishes to...cooperate. As the waves are unsafe for me, you will be my surrogate.

**row 1045; string ID 117385907199463429; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> It's surprising, isn't it? The attachments we hold towards simple objects. But they are anything but simple. Seemingly mute articles can speak in different ways.

**row 1046; string ID 117385907199463430; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> If I showed you a childhood toy, you would be awash with forgotten memories.

**row 1047; string ID 117385907199463431; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> They hold the power to recall what was once lost.

**row 1048; string ID 117385907199463432; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> That stuttering old fool...yes, I knew him. He'd join us on the water sometimes, but was more hindrance than help.

**row 1049; string ID 117385907199463433; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> He never seemed entirely with it. I can't imagine he has fared too well since I last saw him.

**row 1051; string ID 117385907199463435; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Why, of course. As human as you are.

**row 1437; string ID 148983051060432896; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Though it has been some decades since I last laid eyes upon this...the sound of its mechanisms has grown no less unpleasant.

**row 1438; string ID 148983051060432897; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Nevertheless. A reward is owed.

**row 1439; string ID 148983051060432898; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Now...with these [C3]five relics[/C3] in our possession...only one thing remains.

**row 1440; string ID 148983051060432899; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We will see her again soon. It will all be worth it. You know this.

**row 1441; string ID 148983051060432900; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> I suspect you know by now.

**row 1444; string ID 148983051060432903; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> It will become apparent soon enough.

**row 1445; string ID 148983051060432904; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We must make one final voyage. This time, I will be with you.

**row 1454; string ID 148983051060432913; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Now. Our destination is the open expanse to the [C0]West[/C0] of [C0]Greater Marrow[/C0]. It's marked on the [C0]Map[/C0].

**row 1455; string ID 148983051060432914; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> We [C2]must not tarry[/C2] on the open waters, not with such a significant cargo. Make haste!

**row 1457; string ID 148983051060432916; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> So be it. Return when you are prepared.

**row 1460; string ID 149004694776369152; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> There's no time for this. We must get back out to sea immediately. [C0]West[/C0] of [C0]Greater Marrow[/C0] - that's where we must go.

**row 1481; string ID 149408775223300115; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> The relics are coming with us. We should wait for the fog to thicken. With luck, the seas will remain unaware of our intentions...

**row 1483; string ID 149408775240077312; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> This is the place.

**row 1484; string ID 149408775240077313; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> This is where she was taken from us...and where we can at last bring her back.

**row 1485; string ID 149408775240077314; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Only here. And only because of what we've achieved.

**row 1487; string ID 149408775240077316; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> To release a lost one, frozen in time...

**row 1489; string ID 149408775240077318; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> ...and tethered by chains in the deep...

**row 1491; string ID 149408775240077320; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> ...bind them to this world once more.

**row 1493; string ID 149408775240077322; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> Open the door to the starry heavens...

**row 1495; string ID 149408775240077324; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> ...and lay the weary world to rest.

**row 1568; string ID 154122023411568640; source label COLLECTOR_NAME_KEY**

> But all the same, they must be found. I never said this would be easy. I suppose we'll see if you have what it takes, soon enough.


## Collector (unidentified in scene) (6 strings)

**row 0305; string ID 66638564575744016; source label COLLECTOR_UNKNOWN_DOCK_NAME_KEY**

> I know what you took to the Fishmonger.

**row 0306; string ID 66638564575744017; source label COLLECTOR_UNKNOWN_DOCK_NAME_KEY**

> And I'll be clear - I know he extracted an artifact from it. I need to inspect it. But not here.

**row 0307; string ID 66638564575744018; source label COLLECTOR_UNKNOWN_DOCK_NAME_KEY**

> Meet me on [C3]Blackstone Isle[/C3]. Head out of this bay and cut [C1]South[/C1]. I have a business proposition that you'll want to hear.

**row 0308; string ID 66638564575744019; source label COLLECTOR_UNKNOWN_DOCK_NAME_KEY**

> I'll be waiting.

**row 0310; string ID 66638564575744021; source label COLLECTOR_UNKNOWN_HOUSE_NAME_KEY**

> You came. Good. Allow me to introduce myself.

**row 0311; string ID 66638564575744022; source label COLLECTOR_UNKNOWN_HOUSE_NAME_KEY**

> I am a collector - of many things; art and artifacts, treasures and truths...and curios that occupy the periphery of desire.

## Narrator / scene text (401 strings)

**row 0002; string ID 5797928897679360; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You enter a squalid shack on the fringe of the marketplace. The familiar smell of fish fills the air. Flies buzz haphazardly around a downcast man behind the counter.

**row 0012; string ID 5797928922845193; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The morning light fills your eyes, and you try to sit upright. You're lying on the cold, wet dock where you collapsed the night before.

**row 0018; string ID 5797928922845199; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You step onto the dock at Greater Marrow. The Mayor is waiting nearby.

**row 0026; string ID 5832393539784704; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Mayor is standing outside the Fishmonger's store.

**row 0029; string ID 5877743080247296; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> As you walk into the yard, you see the [C0]Shipwright[/C0] making repairs to a damaged hull. She looks up at you briefly before turning back to her work.

**row 0033; string ID 5877743084441603; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She shrugs and gestures towards the hull she's currently attempting to repair. A number of wooden boards - all well above the water line - are splintered and scratched.

**row 0038; string ID 6872154509479938; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Mayor hands you a small, damp package bound in string. It's dripping slightly.

**row 0043; string ID 7217358294814721; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You hand the [C3]grotesque fish[/C3] to the Fishmonger. He grins, raising the fish up to his face before inhaling a deep, stuttering breath.

**row 0050; string ID 7258692242182144; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You enter a brightly lit shop. It's packed with antiques, the shelves full of jewellery and other baubles. An old man peers at you over silver spectacles.

**row 0055; string ID 7275844126969859; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You explain that you've sold an engraved belt buckle to The Trader.

**row 0060; string ID 7581114120081408; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You approach a woman sitting on the dockside, staring out to sea. She turns to you.

**row 0075; string ID 7581114124275729; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You've delivered all the necessary materials. You should let the Builder know.

**row 0082; string ID 7657931858317312; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A hunched woman approaches you from the steps to the lighthouse. She stops some distance away and looks at you with concern and visible apprehension.

**row 0088; string ID 7657931879288838; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She turns and shuffles back along the path that leads to the light.

**row 0089; string ID 7657931879288839; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You enter the Collector's house. He's standing at the back of the room, still holding the silver and crimson book. He seems impatient.

**row 0091; string ID 7657931879288841; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You hand him the key. The cold metal shimmers a [C3]strange shine[/C3] as he holds it. The shape of the key - the teeth, the head - now somehow seem changed, smaller. Has it always been this way?

**row 0096; string ID 11998932718653440; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Mayor is walking quickly towards you. He seems to be in a good mood, with a spring in his step.

**row 0190; string ID 51156735305633792; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The lighthouse towers above. Waves crash endlessly against the perilous rocks below.

**row 0192; string ID 51156735309828097; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You ponder the intrinsic power of this structure, a vast column of stone shouldering the responsibility of countless lives.

**row 0195; string ID 51156735309828100; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A floating buoy. It serves to mark safe passage past dangerous rocks and obscured hazards.

**row 0199; string ID 51156735309828104; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The islands to the North and South are...unfamilar, but are probably marked on your map.

**row 0242; string ID 53986564623224850; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He turns to you. For someone who has just been saved, he seems gloomy.

**row 0244; string ID 53986564623224852; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He removes a [C1]Signet Ring[/C1] from his left hand and presses it into yours. It's emblazoned with a symbol of a captain's wheel.

**row 0246; string ID 53986564623224854; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> On the shoreline, partially submerged in a rockpool, is the body of a man. The sun and sea have corrupted his features - it's hard to make out much more than that.

**row 0247; string ID 53986564623224855; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With a grim reverence, you turn the wheel and continue on your way.

**row 0248; string ID 55076055871983616; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You approach a hooded figure. Swathed in dark blue robes, their face is barely visible.

**row 0274; string ID 57660747079028736; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A short man is shouting orders at a handful of workers disembarking from a boat nearby. The man notices you.

**row 0283; string ID 58722875024265219; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He tears a small hole in the paper and peeks inside. You can't say for sure, but it feels as though he's shielding it from your view.

**row 0300; string ID 66638564575744011; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He turns a few pages in his book and mutters something low under his breath.

**row 0301; string ID 66638564575744012; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He runs his fingers firmly along the length of the fish, as though feeling for something. He pushes from one side of its stomach and a small shape can be seen against the bulging scales.

**row 0302; string ID 66638564575744013; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He slices the fish open. From its stomach he pulls a small piece of fabric and spreads it on the counter. It's a [C3]delicately patterned Handkerchief[/C3].

**row 0304; string ID 66638564575744015; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A man is looking at you through your cabin window. His face is mostly in shadow.

**row 0309; string ID 66638564575744020; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The man is standing in a doorway, watching you approach. He's holding an old book in one hand. It's bound in crimson, with silver ribbons.

**row 0313; string ID 66638564575744024; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You give the [C3]Handkerchief[/C3] to the Collector. He holds it up to the flickering light and studies it.

**row 0322; string ID 66658749214953472; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You don't have the [C3]Handkerchief[/C3] with you. The man seemed interested in it, so you should probably bring it to the meeting.

**row 0333; string ID 66696833423970312; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The man is clearly trying his hardest to remain composed, but tremors of grief rack his body.

**row 0341; string ID 66696833423970320; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You explain that you found the belt buckle, but lost it again.

**row 0344; string ID 66705580196171776; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You explain that you found a belt buckle, but it's currently in your storage.

**row 0357; string ID 68169881381572608; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She holds the lantern up so that it shines brightly onto your face.

**row 0367; string ID 71737740181557255; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Fishmonger wraps up the fish and hands you some money.

**row 0374; string ID 71737740181557262; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Fishmonger wraps up the fish and hands you some money.

**row 0378; string ID 71737740181557269; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Fishmonger wraps up the crabs and hands you some money.

**row 0389; string ID 71737740181557281; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Fishmonger takes the aberrated fish from you. He clutches it close to his chest, wringing his hands around its slimy body.

**row 0394; string ID 71737740181557286; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> In the stillness of the room, the Fishmonger's hands begin to tremble.

**row 0397; string ID 71737740181557289; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Fishmonger's door slams behind you. You hear the heavy scraping of a metal bar sliding across it.

**row 0398; string ID 71737740181557290; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Fishmonger's door is barred from the inside.

**row 0412; string ID 73248624187985927; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> Your brother forgives you. He's found you a place to stay.

**row 0429; string ID 73248624187985948; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Hermit climbs aboard with all of his belongings.

**row 0483; string ID 73989035210756096; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You drag the massive music box into the room, gouging the wooden floorboards beneath it. The Collector doesn't seem to mind. He's focused on the box.

**row 0492; string ID 73989035210756106; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He selects one of the silver ribbons trailing from the crimson-bound book, opening it to some unseen page.

**row 0494; string ID 73989035210756108; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> After a few seconds, you feel your stomach drop suddenly, as though thrown high by a wave. He closes the book and turns his attention back to you.

**row 0506; string ID 74259460786294789; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A burst of light fills your vision, and you stagger back briefly.

**row 0539; string ID 81572268593041422; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Hermit notices the old Whaler standing on the dock, waiting for him.

**row 0543; string ID 81572268593041426; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The two brothers walk away down the dock. One of them pauses for a moment and turns back to you.

**row 0546; string ID 81572268593041429; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> Your brother's sorry. Will you let him come home?

**row 0554; string ID 81892477604270080; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The old Whaler is waiting for you by the docks.

**row 0571; string ID 81906695908954112; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A pile of loose debris. It could be cleared to make a shortcut.

**row 0574; string ID 81906695908954115; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A pile of loose debris. Clearing this would take some strong explosives.

**row 0577; string ID 81906695908954118; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A pile of loose debris. In the gaps between the rocks you can see what appear to be large, white teeth.

**row 0582; string ID 81906695908954123; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The debris crumbles away. You should let the Whaler know.

**row 0583; string ID 83757368388685824; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You stand in the center of a campsite. The fire's embers are cool, but ash is still steadily blowing out with the wind.

**row 0589; string ID 83757368409657349; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A large flat rock sits atop the charred wood of the campfire. A handful of tiny fish bones can be seen poking out of the ashes.

**row 0590; string ID 83757368409657350; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You find no footprints, but unfamiliar symbols have been sketched in the dust nearby. Some have been erased with the hasty sweep of a foot.

**row 0591; string ID 83757368409657351; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The tent is a sheet of cloth suspended over some sticks. There is nothing inside, save for a smoothed hollow in the dirt where someone slept.

**row 0597; string ID 86592179656642560; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A slab of rock juts from the water, bearing crude symbols of [C0]curved fish[/C0]. At its base, a rough shape has been chiselled away.

**row 0598; string ID 86592179702779904; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A wave washes over the fish, sweeping them away. When the water recedes, you notice something has lodged against the rock.

**row 0617; string ID 86592179702779923; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She looks you up and down, and her voice cracks. She trails off.

**row 0654; string ID 86592179702779967; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Repulsion Machine[/C0] you installed is still connected to the Generator.

**row 0656; string ID 86592179702779969; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Researcher's Repulsion Machine[/C0] could be connected to the [C0]Outpost's Generator[/C0] here.

**row 0660; string ID 86592179702779973; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Repulsion Machine[/C0] hums and whirrs to life. A few seconds later, a bright light shoots from the top, and an alarm begins blaring.

**row 0661; string ID 86592179702779974; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Researcher[/C0] said this should keep the creature at bay - for a time, at least.

**row 0662; string ID 86592179702779976; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> After some searching, you find the [C0]Prototype Parts[/C0] that the Researcher needs to assemble the Sampling Device.

**row 0663; string ID 86592179702779977; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You stow the [C0]Prototype Parts[/C0] on board.

**row 0664; string ID 86974217815511040; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You walk in and place the jewel-encrusted ring on a table by the Collector. His eyes widen.

**row 0667; string ID 86974217865842690; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He opens the crimson book, its spine cracking audibly. Selecting a page carefully, he begins to read aloud. The words sound muffled, as though he's speaking through a thick haze.

**row 0668; string ID 86974217865842691; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You hear a growling, crackling in your ears. Suddenly, your vision flares and both of your ears pop. He closes the book.

**row 0677; string ID 88389352182435846; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Researcher is quiet for a moment and stares off to the side.

**row 0682; string ID 88852755371970560; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A slab of rock cuts above the waves. There are [C0]four large, powerful fish[/C0] shapes carved into its face, and four great fissures running through the rock's base.

**row 0683; string ID 88852755422302208; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You slide the four sharks into the fissures and they slip down out of sight. Moments later, you spot something rising towards the surface of the water.

**row 0686; string ID 89155828191514625; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The dog bounds over to the Researcher. She crouches down to pet the dog, grinning. The dog licks her chin in response.

**row 0692; string ID 89155828191514631; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> There is a skinny brown dog leaping around in the shallows. Occasionally it lunges at the water, snapping at some small, silvery fish. Its technique is ineffective.

**row 0727; string ID 89155828191514666; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You make your way back towards the boat. As you're wading out, you hear the sloshing of four legs cutting through the water. You stop and turn around.

**row 0730; string ID 89155828191514669; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You continue, this time with the dog alongside. Its tail is wagging so hard it beats the water into a froth behind it.

**row 0731; string ID 89155828191514670; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> When you get to the boat, you turn around to pull the dog on board.

**row 0733; string ID 89155828191514672; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You shout at the dog and hit the water with your hands. The dog scampers away, out of sight.

**row 0734; string ID 89155828191514673; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You climb back onto your boat, leaving the dog behind.

**row 0736; string ID 89155828191514675; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You leave the dog treading water beside the boat. After some time, it turns around and swims back to shore.

**row 0741; string ID 89479713950601216; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> There are some boxes scattered haphazardly on the shore.

**row 0744; string ID 89479713954795522; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A curious looking chest is half submerged in the sand.

**row 0753; string ID 104041768898711552; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Laboratory[/C0] is in ruins, with equipment and research papers strewn about the place.

**row 0757; string ID 104041768953237507; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> There are some strange looking [C0]Prototype Parts[/C0] on the table, but you can't see any use for them right now.

**row 0761; string ID 104041768953237511; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You look around on the floor. There are cracks in the base of the structure that grow to large gashes as you follow them along to the walls.

**row 0767; string ID 104048151782150147; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You kick the ashes. Your foot hits something hard at the bottom of the pile. You brush the remnants aside.

**row 0773; string ID 104062385798696963; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A once strong wooden hull lays broken against the shoreline. The stern is completely beached.

**row 0775; string ID 104062385798696965; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You pull a bit closer and manage to clamber into the back of the wreck. You look around.

**row 0777; string ID 104062385798696967; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A rotten mast lays down on this island, half hidden by the foliage. At its base, an old boat is cracked in two.

**row 0781; string ID 104322849489211392; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She reaches into her toolbox and pulls out a handful of components.

**row 0783; string ID 108775052987781120; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A large man is watching you through the billowing smoke of a campfire, idly carving away at a wooden stake. He stands as you pull ashore.

**row 0844; string ID 108775052987781184; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A large cage is suspended over the water. A small receptacle is floating underneath.

**row 0847; string ID 108775052987781187; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You load the [C0]Bait[/C0] into the trap. You should probably move away.

**row 0849; string ID 108775052987781189; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> Inside is the obliterated corpse of a [C3]Mind Sucker[/C3]. The Airman will be expecting proof of your success.

**row 0852; string ID 108775052987781192; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A mangled mess of wood and wire. The trap itself was re-usable, but not after being hit by a mortar.

**row 0854; string ID 108775052987781194; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You hand the tattered lump of flesh to the Airman. It's almost unrecognizable, but you both know it couldn't have belonged to anything else.

**row 0855; string ID 108775052987781195; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He pokes at it with his knife, testing the rubbery meat. He seems satisfied.

**row 0857; string ID 108775052987781197; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You drop the flesh onto the ground and he gives it a few slashes with the knife. It wobbles a little.

**row 0859; string ID 108775052987781199; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You drop the large, bloodied carcass in front of the Airman. His eyes fill with sadistic delight.

**row 0860; string ID 108775052987781200; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He bends down and plunges his knife into the soft flesh. It falls away before the blade, revealing a [C3]glint of metal[/C3].

**row 0861; string ID 108775052987781201; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With a sickening tear, he wrests it from where it was embedded in the flesh of the creature. He holds it aloft. You recognize it as a [C3]necklace[/C3], glistening in gore.

**row 0863; string ID 108775052987781203; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He stands back from the corpse. A melancholic look comes over his face.

**row 0878; string ID 109817482822471680; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A crooked boulder with a small, simple platform at its base. The [C0]twisted shape of a deformed fish[/C0] is barely recognizable, carved into the rockface.

**row 0879; string ID 109817482822471681; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The waves lap up to the platform where you laid the aberration. As the water makes contact with it, a buzzing sound fills the air.

**row 0891; string ID 111237613075222528; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You see a campsite. Some old fish are suspended on a spit over a fire that was never lit. A pile of fabric is gathered at the base of a large tree nearby.

**row 0897; string ID 111237613121359878; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A nest of rags lies at the base of a tree. Somebody slept here. The rags are covered in bark chips - there are symbols carved into the tree trunk above.

**row 0905; string ID 111237613121359889; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A book symbol sits above a wave. A downwards arrow points from the book, down through the wave.

**row 0906; string ID 111285490199744512; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The wreckage of an old fighter plane lies buried deep in the sand. You pull a little closer.

**row 0909; string ID 111285490254270464; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The fuselage of an old fighter plane sits amidst a mass of broken wings and wheels.

**row 0912; string ID 112418385421324288; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The previously still water bubbles up and over, obscuring the fish. A few moments later, when the water calms, something else is on the platform.

**row 0922; string ID 116750453726957577; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The words you read from that book...what did they do to me?

**row 0937; string ID 116750453726957592; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He looks at you. For a moment, a look of something like regret flashes in his eyes.

**row 0943; string ID 116750453726957598; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With a wry grin he pulls a dusty tome from the nearest shelf and glances at the cover.

**row 0949; string ID 116750453726957604; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C3]Book of The Deep[/C3] appears in his hands, sheathed in its dirty crimson jacket.

**row 0955; string ID 116750453726957610; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The air around the book begins to pulse rhythmically like a quickening heartbeat.

**row 0969; string ID 116750453726957625; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You take a step closer through the crackling room. The Collector lurches forwards in response.

**row 0973; string ID 116750453726957629; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You strike out with your right fist, punching hard into the jaw of the Collector. His face shatters and falls to the ground.

**row 0994; string ID 116750453731151872; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You undock next to an old rowboat. Somebody is here. He's hunched over by the campfire, muttering to himself. You cautiously approach.

**row 1007; string ID 116750453731151885; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He turns away, wringing his hands. He speaks to the fire.

**row 1009; string ID 116750453731151887; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The man makes crude explosion noises and motions wide with his hands, still talking to the fire, as though pantomiming a story to a child.

**row 1019; string ID 116750453731151897; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The man closes his eyes tight as he holds back tears. His voice falls to a whisper.

**row 1027; string ID 116750453731151905; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He rocks back and forth, staring through the flames.

**row 1032; string ID 117037914571067392; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You enter the house, carrying the ornate necklace. The Collector recognizes it almost immediately.

**row 1034; string ID 117037914600427521; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He sweeps aside some scraps on a dusty table, candle wax pooling in its corners. He places the necklace carefully in the clearing.

**row 1037; string ID 117037914600427524; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> As he finishes speaking you feel blood rushing back to your legs. A strange feeling of power grows in your chest.

**row 1052; string ID 127200836421079040; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A stone shrine. An upright slab bears a carving - [C0]two heavily plated creatures, locked in battle[/C0]. A lower slab holds two shallow grooves.

**row 1053; string ID 127200836446244864; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The rock splits with an almighty crack. It crumbles away, revealing its core.

**row 1054; string ID 127200836446244865; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A stone shrine. An upright slab bears a carving - [C0]two pale bottom-dwellers, hiding from their fate[/C0]. A lower slab holds two shallow grooves.

**row 1055; string ID 127200836446244866; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The rock splits with an almighty crack. It crumbles away, revealing its core.

**row 1056; string ID 127200836446244867; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A stone shrine. An upright slab bears a carving - [C3]two malformed fish, writhing under the sun[/C3]. A lower slab holds two shallow grooves.

**row 1057; string ID 127200836446244868; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The rock splits with an almighty crack. It crumbles away, revealing its core.

**row 1067; string ID 127200836446244878; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A man meets you as you dock, bracing himself against the wind and spray.

**row 1104; string ID 127200836446244915; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You follow him up the steps of the temple. At the top is a black stone altar. Stacked high upon the altar are layers of dark, damp wood.

**row 1105; string ID 127200836446244916; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He draws the antique pocket watch from his robe. It sways hypnotically in the breeze.

**row 1113; string ID 127200836446244924; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The wind picks up, swirling sea spray around the altar. A cold blue flame rises from the sodden wood at his feet.

**row 1119; string ID 127200836446244930; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The freezing blaze climbs his legs. His chanting continues, no pain detectable in his voice.

**row 1120; string ID 127200836446244931; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He lifts his arms skyward. Seconds later, the inferno engulfs him.

**row 1121; string ID 127200836446244932; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> It rages for a moment, then abruptly dies away. A cold mist pools around the floor of the temple briefly before the wind sweeps it into the sea.

**row 1126; string ID 127200836446244937; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You take the pocket watch - it's freezing cold. It vibrates in your palm, clock hands spinning wildly below the cracked face.

**row 1130; string ID 127200836446244941; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He pulls an antique pocket watch from inside his robe. [C3]Droplets of sea spray crackle and seethe in the air around it[/C3].

**row 1139; string ID 127200836446244950; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The statue holds a stone crucible. It's empty.

**row 1140; string ID 127200836446244951; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The statue holds a stone crucible. It's empty.

**row 1141; string ID 127200836446244952; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The statue holds a stone crucible. It's empty.

**row 1149; string ID 128651020652957696; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Fishmonger reaches under the counter and hands you a damp, battered book. A few shimmering fish scales are stuck to the cover.

**row 1150; string ID 128651020652957697; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She pulls a [C1]Book[/C1] from a toolbox nearby and hands it to you. A few of the pages are stuck together with grease.

**row 1153; string ID 128651020652957700; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He pulls a crumpled [C1]Book[/C1] from his back pocket and hands it to you. A number of the pages are folded over.

**row 1154; string ID 128651020652957701; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He pulls a [C1]Book[/C1] from a stack sitting on his desk and hands it to you. It's covered in dust, but otherwise seems in good condition.

**row 1160; string ID 128695983344373760; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You don't have any [C0]Packed Explosives[/C0]. Perhaps the [C0]Whaler[/C0] at [C0]Ingfell[/C0] has some more.

**row 1179; string ID 130128175236829184; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You disembark and take the package over to the Dockworker. He's standing motionless on the docks, staring down at the water.

**row 1180; string ID 130128175257800704; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You press the package into his hands - his fingers slowly gripping it is the closest thing to a response you get.

**row 1184; string ID 130128175261995011; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He takes the large package and examines it gently with his hands. He lifts it to one ear and listens for a while. He seems satisfied.

**row 1186; string ID 130128175261995013; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He turns and hurries off down the dock without another word to you - or compensation.

**row 1187; string ID 130128175261995014; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Dockworker[/C0] is standing motionless on the edge of the dock, staring down at the water. He seems paralyzed by fear at what he sees.

**row 1190; string ID 130128175261995017; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You stand with him for a while. Occasionally, a tremor shakes his body and his breathing becomes labored.

**row 1193; string ID 130128175261995020; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He strains, clenching his fists - eyes shut tight with the effort. A tear rolls down his cheek.

**row 1197; string ID 130128175261995024; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A [C3]drop of dark ooze[/C3] falls from one of his ears and lands on the dock. It slips between the boards and disappears into the water below.

**row 1199; string ID 130128175266189312; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You pull up alongside a boat at anchor. There are signs of life on board, but nobody appears to greet you.

**row 1201; string ID 130128175266189314; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> Almost immediately, a small slot in the door snaps open. A pair of frantic eyes peer out at you.

**row 1217; string ID 130128175266189330; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Courier pushes a lumpy brown package through the doorhole. It squelches slightly as you take it in your hands. [C3]Dark fluid oozes[/C3] from one corner.

**row 1230; string ID 131218274636713986; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Trader takes the [C0]Stone Tablet[/C0] from you and inspects it closely, turning it over in his hands.

**row 1236; string ID 131218274636713992; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Trader takes the [C0]Stone Tablet[/C0] from you and lays it on a table next to the others.

**row 1239; string ID 131218274636713995; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Trader takes the [C0]Stone Tablet[/C0] from you and places it alongside the others. He turns them over and arranges them together in a square.

**row 1240; string ID 131218274636713996; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> On the undersides of the tablets, new glyphs begin to form, casting an [C3]eerie light[/C3] onto the low ceiling.

**row 1242; string ID 131218274636713998; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He begins transcribing the glyphs onto an old scrap of parchment. Each stroke appears to demand excruciating concentration.

**row 1248; string ID 131218274645102592; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You climb the many steps leading up to the [C0]Ancient Lighthouse[/C0]. At its base is a solid stone door.

**row 1249; string ID 131218274645102593; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You can't see a handle, but there's a large socket carved into the face of the door.

**row 1251; string ID 131218274645102595; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Fused Tablet[/C0] clicks into place and the door rumbles before sliding aside.

**row 1254; string ID 131218274645102598; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You step through the doorway of the [C0]Ancient Lighthouse[/C0]. The stairway to the top has collapsed - this is as far as you're going to get.

**row 1267; string ID 131568044525932549; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An extremely rough and worn sheet of fabric is collapsed at the base of some wooden supports. There's nothing remarkable about it.

**row 1268; string ID 131568044525932550; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The symbols span the breadth of the camp, twisting this way and that. Some are grouped together into what must be words.

**row 1278; string ID 131568044525932565; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> In the dim light of the cave you can make out some paintings on both walls.

**row 1313; string ID 139135624497254402; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He flicks through a large log book. It appears to be a record of shipping manifests.

**row 1344; string ID 144664106983673856; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You can barely hear their rasping whisper above the wind and waves.

**row 1346; string ID 144664106987868161; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The hooded figure unfurls an old paper scroll. They point to a shape shown at the top. It appears to be a [C0]Blue Mackerel[/C0].

**row 1347; string ID 144664106987868162; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With expert precision they plunge their hands through the gullet of the fish and tear out the heart. They stuff it into their mouth unceremoniously.

**row 1349; string ID 144664106987868164; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They point to another shape on a second scroll. This one looks like a [C0]Tiger Mackerel[/C0].

**row 1352; string ID 144664106987868167; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They point to a fish on the final scroll. This one is a [C0]Snake Mackerel[/C0].

**row 1353; string ID 144664106987868168; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The fish has barely left your hands before splintered nails begin peeling at its scales. The heart is extracted and devoured.

**row 1356; string ID 144664106987868171; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They cross their arms and appear to fall into a deep sleep.

**row 1357; string ID 144664106987868173; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You watch from a distance for some time but see no movement. You turn around and leave quietly.

**row 1358; string ID 144958400722292736; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You approach a hooded figure. Dirty gold robes obscure all features except for a pair of withered and blackened hands.

**row 1362; string ID 144958400751652867; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The hooded figure holds out a scroll. They tap at a shape depicted. It looks like a [C0]Red Snapper[/C0].

**row 1363; string ID 144958400751652868; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They rip through the throat of the fish and tear out its heart. It's in their mouth before you have time to process what you just saw.

**row 1365; string ID 144958400751652870; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They point at another shape on a scroll. This one looks like a [C0]Fangtooth[/C0].

**row 1366; string ID 144958400751652871; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They bring this small fish up to their mouth and bite at where the heart is. Their cracked lips part to reveal a row of sharpened teeth.

**row 1368; string ID 144958400751652873; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They unfurl the final scroll. This one shows a [C0]Blue Crab[/C0].

**row 1369; string ID 144958400751652874; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They crack and bend the crab's legs apart and feast on the flesh inside.

**row 1372; string ID 144958400751652877; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They cross their arms and appear to fall into a deep sleep.

**row 1374; string ID 144958400751652879; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You watch from a distance for some time but see no movement. You turn around and leave quietly.

**row 1375; string ID 144958400751652880; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You approach a hooded figure. Deep violet robes mask their face completely.

**row 1377; string ID 144958400751652882; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The person appears to forget the rest of what they were about to say.

**row 1379; string ID 144958400751652884; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The hooded figure reveals a scroll. They gesture to a picture shown at the top. It's a [C0]Tarpon[/C0].

**row 1380; string ID 144958400751652885; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They hold the fish in front of their face, studying it with a tightening grip. After a few seconds, the fish collapses in on itself under the pressure.

**row 1381; string ID 144958400751652886; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure looks through the gory mess and picks out the heart, placing it onto their tongue.

**row 1383; string ID 144958400751652888; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They show you another scroll. This one shows a [C0]Horseshoe Crab[/C0].

**row 1384; string ID 144958400751652889; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure turns the crab over to expose its soft underbelly. They scoop out its innards with sharp fingernails, consuming them blindly.

**row 1386; string ID 144958400751652891; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure slowly reveals the final scroll. This one holds a picture of a [C0]Barreleye[/C0].

**row 1387; string ID 144958400751652892; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure begins to consume the fish head first, gnashing teeth searching for the heart of the thing. When the fish is halfway gone, they stop and throw it aside.

**row 1388; string ID 144958400751652893; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They take a [C1]Book[/C1] from inside their robe and give it to you.

**row 1390; string ID 144958400751652895; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They cross their arms and appear to fall into a deep sleep.

**row 1392; string ID 144958400751652897; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You watch from a distance for some time but see no movement. You turn around and leave quietly.

**row 1393; string ID 144958400751652898; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You approach a hooded figure. They are standing expectantly, vermillion red robes blowing in the wind.

**row 1395; string ID 144958400751652900; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The voice from the hood cuts through the humid air.

**row 1397; string ID 144958400751652902; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The hooded figure produces an old paper scroll. They point at a shape on it - a [C0]Cusk Eel[/C0].

**row 1398; string ID 144958400751652903; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure draws a ceremonial knife from their robe and slices into the fish, cutting out the heart. They place it on a stand in front of them.

**row 1401; string ID 144958400751652906; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The ceremonial knife comes out again, and the large heart is extracted. It's placed on a stand beside the first.

**row 1403; string ID 144958400751652908; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure points to a shape on the last scroll. This one is a [C0]Frilled Shark[/C0].

**row 1404; string ID 144958400751652909; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> They drag the shark's body around to find the right angle before plunging the knife in and pulling out the heart. They place it on a stand beside the others.

**row 1406; string ID 144958400751652911; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure kneels before the fish hearts and becomes still. You leave before they decide they need any more hearts.

**row 1408; string ID 144958400751652913; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You watch from a distance for some time but see no movement. You turn around and leave quietly.

**row 1409; string ID 145342053180690432; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An altar breaks through the waves at the base of the cliff. [C0]Three crab-like creatures[/C0] are carved into it. The largest is covered in rocks and other strange shapes.

**row 1410; string ID 145342053214244864; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The three crabs shudder, legs clicking into strange angles. Though long-dead, some unseen power commands their corpses into action.

**row 1411; string ID 145342053214244865; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The crabs scuttle over the altar, disappearing into cracks in the stone. Suddenly the rock splits in two, revealing a sunken chamber at its core.

**row 1419; string ID 147470449826254848; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You pull up to a wreck with a familiar looking hull. It's almost identical to the ship given to you by the Mayor - except this one has a huge breach in one side.

**row 1429; string ID 147470449851420681; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The cupboards have been completely and methodically emptied. All the drawers are missing.

**row 1431; string ID 147470449851420683; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You've already looked over the equipment pretty thoroughly.

**row 1432; string ID 147470449851420684; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The equipment is all still there, though surely exposure to the elements has fried the internals. You angle the overhead radio down to get a closer look.

**row 1433; string ID 147470449851420685; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A [C1]note[/C1] slips off the top. You put it in your pocket to read later.

**row 1435; string ID 147470449851420687; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You poke through the cargohold. A few items remain, not yet lost to the sea.

**row 1436; string ID 148983051026878464; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You take out the watch. Its once frozen hands are now inexplicably twitching in a rhythmic struggle, trying but failing to move forwards. You place it in front of the Collector.

**row 1488; string ID 149408775240077317; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He throws the [C3]pocket watch[/C3] overboard.

**row 1494; string ID 149408775240077323; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He slots the [C3]key[/C3] into the lock of the [C3]music box[/C3].

**row 1496; string ID 149408775240077325; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He throws the [C3]music box[/C3] overboard, its [C3]key[/C3] left unturned.

**row 1497; string ID 149408775240077326; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You open the book. Its pages are frosted with ice and a chill mist rises around your fingers. You begin to read aloud.

**row 1499; string ID 149408775240077328; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You throw the [C3]pocket watch[/C3] overboard.

**row 1505; string ID 149408775240077334; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You slot the [C3]key[/C3] into the lock of the [C3]music box[/C3].

**row 1507; string ID 149408775240077336; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You throw the [C3]music box[/C3] overboard, its [C3]key[/C3] left unturned.

**row 1510; string ID 149408775240077339; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The book struggles in your grasp, its weight somehow shifting from side to side. Silver ribbons flail pathetically against your fingers.

**row 1512; string ID 149408775240077341; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You hold it high above your head. A low groan rumbles from the book, echoed by another from the depths.

**row 1517; string ID 149674597082648576; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Lighthouse Keeper is guiding you out to sea - the lighthouse beam is focused on a dark patch of water [C0]West[/C0] of [C0]Greater Marrow[/C0].

**row 1540; string ID 152281662775021568; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An old man emerges from one of the nearby broken buildings and hobbles towards you.

**row 1542; string ID 153455563399942144; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A rotten chest sits atop a pile of rubble at the back of the cave.

**row 1545; string ID 153455563429302274; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A rotting chest peeks out from the skeleton of a wrecked ship.

**row 1548; string ID 153655601610805248; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A small structure sits on the island. Nearby is an abandoned campfire. What do you want to do?

**row 1553; string ID 153655601635971076; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An old stone structure is built around a statue. The statue has its arms raised towards the lighthouse at Devil's Spine.

**row 1555; string ID 153655601635971078; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The campfire itself is unremarkable, but you wonder who would choose an island such as this to make camp.

**row 1557; string ID 153655601640165376; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The stone columns of the temple are carved with ancient pictures and text. Which carving do you want to look at?

**row 1561; string ID 153655601640165380; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The carving shows a bubbling underwater vent. "The breath of salvation. It burns those that mean harm. Only the pure remain."

**row 1563; string ID 153655601640165382; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The carving shows a swarm of small fish surrounding a boat. "The ravenous children. Alone they are nothing. Together they summon her."

**row 1565; string ID 153655601640165384; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The carving is of a large fish with crossed-out eyes. "The unseeing mother. When the starving cannot eat, she approaches."

**row 1578; string ID 161626619830784007; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Mayor appears in front of you on the dock. His face swims before your eyes, and his words blur with the waves crashing around you.

**row 1581; string ID 161626619834978304; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Lighthouse Keeper emerges from the dark forest that flanks the town. She walks towards the dock with a confused look on her face.

**row 1587; string ID 161626619834978310; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Lighthouse Keeper is waiting for you on the dock, looking displeased.

**row 1596; string ID 161626619834978319; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Lighthouse Keeper approaches you on the dock wearing a questioning expression.

**row 1599; string ID 161626619834978322; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She shifts her weight and leans hard against her staff.

**row 1607; string ID 161626619834978330; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She turns from you, shaking her head and muttering. She heads back towards the lighthouse.

**row 1613; string ID 161626619843366917; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You notice his neck and overalls are covered in a dark fluid - the same dark fluid that was leaking from the package you delivered earlier.

**row 1615; string ID 161626619872727040; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A trembling head turns ever so slightly towards you. His breathing grows heavy in anger until you step backwards, leaving him standing there on the dock.

**row 1665; string ID 161672749440487424; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She hands you a [C1]Book[/C1]. The cover is dusty, but in otherwise good condition.

**row 1667; string ID 161672749440487426; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She takes a [C1]Book[/C1] from a nearby shelf and hands it to you. Flicking through it, you see page corners turned in and handwritten notes in the margins.

**row 1669; string ID 161672749448876033; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She opens a drawer and pulls out a [C1]Book[/C1]. Sheepishly, she hands it to you.

**row 1671; string ID 164253136075280384; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The stone trembles before you. Unknown symbols glow from within.

**row 1673; string ID 164253136108834817; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A sudden chill rushes through the marrow of your bones, surging up your arm, your neck. A [C3]deep vision[/C3] enters your mind.

**row 1675; string ID 164253136108834819; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The stone hums quietly, sending ripples through the air around it. Faint shapes dance upon its surface, vanishing before your eyes can focus on them.

**row 1677; string ID 164253136108834821; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The cold stone does not respond to your touch.

**row 1679; string ID 164253136108834823; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The town of Greater Marrow burns. The lighthouse lies collapsed, its light extinguished.

**row 1680; string ID 164253136108834824; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A red glow fills the sky behind a cyclone that drains the world.

**row 1681; string ID 164253136108834825; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A ship, tossed by waves in the night. Fog unfolds like a blanket from the deck, spilling across the ocean and crashing against the land.

**row 1684; string ID 164253136108834828; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The newborn structures crackle with an unknown energy, crimson lightning arcing between them.

**row 1685; string ID 164253136108834829; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A blustery wind screams through the cliffs, diving and turning. It passes over a large wooden ship at anchor, all flags filling suddenly.

**row 1686; string ID 164253136108834830; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A skinless face appears on the fluttering fabric. A wail of terror rises from the crew.

**row 1687; string ID 164253136108834831; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A heart of stone wrapped in a twisting tail. A serpentine body uncoils, teeth rasping at its earthen shell.

**row 1689; string ID 164253136108834833; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A spore floats in the water, adrift in the current. Fish feast in a frenzy above, devouring its kin. The spore lands in the thick silt of the sea floor.

**row 1691; string ID 164253136108834835; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The green glimmer of an aurora blooms in the night sky above the resort. A distant boom shakes the thatched rooves and rouses dreamers from their sleep.

**row 1692; string ID 164253136108834836; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The waters withdraw, exposing the coral to the warm air. Confused creatures chitter and seek out their hiding places.

**row 1693; string ID 164253136108834837; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A man flees through the trees, bloodshot eyes wide with terror. He runs into a dead end; cornered by the murky waters and his pursuer, he turns.

**row 1695; string ID 164253136108834839; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A parasite burrows into a large snakelike carcass. It feeds, growing fat and multiplying. Tendrils sprout from the head of the body and sense the water around it.

**row 1696; string ID 164253136108834840; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The host corpse shudders reluctantly. With a cry that resounds through the forest, it rises once again.

**row 1697; string ID 164253136108834841; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A leviathan raises its vast grey head, stretching up, up until it sits level with a bright flame cradled at the top of the ancient lighthouse.

**row 1698; string ID 164253136108834842; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> It watches the fire for a while, tilting its head and calling softly to it with a guttural song.

**row 1703; string ID 164253136108834847; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> After the last grotesque figure emerges, a membrane spreads over the fissure.

**row 1706; string ID 165334698715959296; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The earth shifts once more and the stones lie still.

**row 1707; string ID 165334698749513728; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> Moments later the waters return; crashing, seething, sweeping souls out to sea.

**row 1708; string ID 165334698749513729; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A distant groan emanates from the space beyond.

**row 1709; string ID 165664221047214080; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> After a few minutes, he puts down his pen and reads aloud.

**row 1710; string ID 167513943965880320; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The workshop looks as though it hasn't been used in decades.

**row 1711; string ID 167513944016211968; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You've already taken the equipment that was stored here.

**row 1713; string ID 167513944016211970; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You enter, taking a moment to let your eyes adjust to the dim light.

**row 1720; string ID 167852613939941376; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He reaches up to a high shelf and brings down a similar looking stone, albeit one covered with much more dust.

**row 1721; string ID 167852613939941377; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You explain to the Trader that you found a use for the [C0]Fused Tablet[/C0] at the Ancient Lighthouse in Devil's Spine.

**row 1726; string ID 182994176340840448; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You climb the broken steps to the old mansion, the waves swelling and crashing against the basalt columns that surround you. You pause before crossing the threshold.

**row 1727; string ID 190347880794607616; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A small object rolls out from under the generator, covered in dried salt.

**row 1734; string ID 250808444337065987; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You find some [C3]horrifying creations[/C3] amongst the detritus.

**row 1799; string ID 276890758106238976; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A collection of cases have washed up against the shore. Some have already split open and lost their contents to the waves.

**row 1803; string ID 277234494273032192; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You move to the side. A few seconds later, the Photographer sighs in exasperation and mutters to herself.

**row 1807; string ID 277234494273032196; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You leave before she asks you to do anything else.

**row 1809; string ID 277234494273032198; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She sighs again and looks off to the [C0]West[/C0].

**row 1812; string ID 277234494273032201; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She rummages through what few belongings survived the trip.

**row 1815; string ID 277234494273032204; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She takes the case from you and inspects each lens carefully.

**row 1883; string ID 287064896433098763; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He clears scraps of cloth and paper from his workbench, revealing a large pestle & mortar.

**row 1885; string ID 287064896433098765; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He turns back to you, a cunning plan formed in his mind.

**row 1903; string ID 298319190488698881; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Travelling Merchant has crossed over to the nearby iceberg and is inspecting some old pieces of metal. You approach.

**row 1905; string ID 298319190488698883; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She kicks a large sheet of metal, half-embedded in the ice. A thin layer of snow dislodges, revealing lines of rivet holes.

**row 1907; string ID 298319190488698885; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You follow her into a small makeshift shelter, ruined by years of harsh weather. Inside are some schematics nailed to a table, still barely legible.

**row 1915; string ID 298319190488698893; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She trudges outside again and paces out the length of the metal scraps on the ice.

**row 1945; string ID 299009947316678664; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Photographer is hurriedly packing camera equipment into boxes. She seems very on edge.

**row 1949; string ID 299388449014575104; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A cold wind whistles through the doorway of the only cabin still standing. Behind it, a frozen-over dig site is littered with tools and debris.

**row 1950; string ID 299388449018769408; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You enter the small cabin, the door long since fallen from its hinges. A rotting table sits inside.

**row 1952; string ID 299388449018769410; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**
