> A [C0]massive shard of ice[/C0] rises from the floor inside, bursting through the ceiling. It fills the room, engulfing all but an old table in front of you.

**row 1958; string ID 299388449018769416; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You open the book, its frozen spine cracking for the first time in decades. Which entry do you want to read?

**row 1959; string ID 299388449018769417; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With great care, you carefully turn to one of the earlier pages.

**row 1963; string ID 299388449018769421; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A few other entries appear to be legible, so you add them to your cabin to read later.

**row 1964; string ID 299388449018769422; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The table is empty, save for some ancient scratches in the rotten wood.

**row 1970; string ID 299388449022963712; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You return to the shard of ice, a [C3]dark form[/C3] still shimmering deep within.

**row 1985; string ID 299388449022963727; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You strike hard against the center of the shard where the shadowy shape appears darkest. A deep crack chases upwards, cleaving away fragments that fall and shatter.

**row 1986; string ID 299388449022963728; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The structure peels open, split to its core. A glistening stone lies cradled by broken shapes, wispy threads of starlight trailing from it.

**row 1988; string ID 299740934984069120; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You dock against a small platform of ice formed around a gigantic, clawed hand. You are not the first to find this place - remnants of a long-abandoned camp litter the ice.

**row 1989; string ID 299740934984069121; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A pile of icy splinters mark the place where the spire fell.

**row 1990; string ID 299740934984069122; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An unnatural spire of ice stands above scraps of snowed-over equipment. A dark shape twists within.

**row 1997; string ID 299740934988263430; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An anguished voice cries from deep within the ice...

**row 2008; string ID 299740934988263441; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A line describing the dock upon your first arrival.

**row 2009; string ID 299740934988263442; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The site of a final escape, and a greater eye closed shut.

**row 2010; string ID 299740934988263443; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A spire of glassy ice stands in the shadow of the cliffs. Across the water, a monstrous visage peers from an icy tomb.

**row 2025; string ID 299740934988263458; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The words drift in, some carried away on the wind.

**row 2033; string ID 299740934988263466; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A pile of icy splinters marks the place where the spire fell.

**row 2034; string ID 299740934988263467; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An unnatural spire of ice stands above scraps of snowed-over equipment. A dark shape twists within.

**row 2046; string ID 299740934988263479; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The dark figure shudders, sending vibrations through the ice.

**row 2053; string ID 301512130354663424; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A translucent spire of ice stretches down into the darkness. A pulsing glow rises from the deep, illuminating [C0]four round sockets[/C0].

**row 2054; string ID 301512130384023552; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The glowing light intensifies, feeding into the four stones. A web of light grows between them, connecting, rejoining. Uniting.

**row 2070; string ID 302183418150416384; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A crudely drawn map of the area has been charted onto a scrap of paper. [C1]You attach it to your own map[/C1].

**row 2071; string ID 302183418167193600; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A large book sits on the table. It's well-worn from the passage of time, but you can still read the words "Captain's Log" etched into the cover.

**row 2094; string ID 302212623227580416; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You rummage through the small pile of equipment in the snow. Most items are stuck fast in the ice, but inside a small backpack you find an [C0]old journal[/C0].

**row 2111; string ID 302212623227580433; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> There are very few pieces that still remain - whoever made camp here was poorly prepared. Inside a small bag, you find a [C0]leather bound journal[/C0].

**row 2127; string ID 305114571199115264; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> It looks like someone built a feeding station for that [C3]monstrous Narwhal[/C3]. Perhaps its appetite would be [C1]satisfied[/C1] by an [C0]offering of fish[/C0].

**row 2128; string ID 306582182600536064; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You approach a hooded figure. They seem to be frozen to the spot, pale robes frosted over with a heavy rime.

**row 2130; string ID 306582182613118977; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You hear their jaw snap shut again from the cold.

**row 2132; string ID 306582182613118979; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The hooded figure withdraws a scroll from their robes and unfurls it to reveal a drawing of a corrupted fish - an [C3]Aberrated[/C3] [C0]Icefish[/C0].

**row 2133; string ID 306582182613118980; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The hooded figure places the fish on the ice at their feet, kicking loose snow over to cover it.

**row 2136; string ID 306582182613118983; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]Sea Stars[/C0] are arranged in a circle on the ground. Moments later, a sinewy heel stomps down through each of them.

**row 2138; string ID 306582182613118985; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A final scroll, this one stained in patches by some dark liquid. In the center, the shape of an [C3]Aberrated[/C3] [C0]Stargazer[/C0] peers from the page.

**row 2139; string ID 306582182613118986; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The figure places the body of the [C0]Stargazer[/C0] in the center of the crushed [C0]Sea Stars[/C0]. They angle its cratered eye to the sky.

**row 2141; string ID 306582182613118988; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> As you leave, the figure is still focused on the grim fish - adjusting its position, waiting, adjusting, over and over.

**row 2143; string ID 306958049401294848; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> After an age, a victor triumphs, sundering the other into impossibly large remnants that ascend to the surface, slowly, to be claimed by the sky.

**row 2145; string ID 306958049401294850; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> As the remains break the surface, ice begins to form.

**row 2211; string ID 365251946355191810; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The crushing compactor clunks to life. The sound of pneumatic pistons and strained squelching soon gives way to the stuttering sound of distant gears.

**row 2235; string ID 373596185545252864; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You dock at a small metal wharf. Four giant legs stretch down into the deep, lifting a mountain of metal into the sky.

**row 2252; string ID 374399090640658432; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is interrupted by the Foreman outside, shouting through his megaphone again. The Foreman bellows an order.

**row 2271; string ID 374820754201354241; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He walks away, muttering something about modulating voices and waves.

**row 2278; string ID 375044890999812096; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You walk the perimeter of the tower. One of the electrical panels hasn't been securely fastened.

**row 2279; string ID 375044890999812097; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You open the panel. Inside, a bundle of cables travels from a [C0]black box[/C0], up into the heights of the tower.

**row 2280; string ID 375044890999812098; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> After finding the loose panel housing the electrical guts of the tower, you pull out the blueprint for the "[C3]RISE[/C3]" module.

**row 2281; string ID 375044890999812099; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You remove the old module and insert the new one. As it clicks into place, the sound from the tower changes into a deep rumble.

**row 2282; string ID 375044890999812100; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With the last scraps of metal still falling from the destroyed drill tower, the Executive boards his helicopter, rotors already spinning.

**row 2287; string ID 375044890999812105; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Foreman was telling the truth. Something attacked the rig!

**row 2294; string ID 375057878267883520; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> THE DEFENSES HAVE BEEN BUILT, AND YOU'RE BACK.

**row 2315; string ID 376128041820450816; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> An elevator connecting the docking area to the main platform lies ahead. A man waits nearby, a yellow hardhat strapped to a concerned expression.

**row 2320; string ID 376128041841422340; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He glances nervously at the water, large waves rolling underneath the shadow of the platform.

**row 2324; string ID 376128041841422344; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You approach an old man, pacing back and forth near a cordoned off area on the main platform. He's muttering to himself quietly.

**row 2336; string ID 376128041845616641; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is waiting inside the lab. You get the sense that he's not very busy at the moment.

**row 2337; string ID 376128041845616642; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is standing in front of a map of the area. A line is drawn from the location of the rig, down to The Marrows.

**row 2339; string ID 376128041845616644; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is standing in front of a map of the area. A line is drawn from the location of the rig, down to The Marrows and Gale Cliffs.

**row 2341; string ID 376128041845616646; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is standing in front of a map of the area. A line is drawn from the location of the rig, spreading out through the southern regions.

**row 2343; string ID 376128041845616648; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is standing in front of a map of the area. A line is drawn from the location of the rig, spreading out through almost all regions.

**row 2345; string ID 376128041845616650; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is standing in front of a map of the area. A line is drawn from the location of the rig, spreading out through all regions.

**row 2353; string ID 376128041845616658; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A large capsule drops down from a chute labelled "[C2]WASTE[/C2]". Through its clear walls you can see remnants of the fish you deposited.

**row 2374; string ID 376152647188967424; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist walks over, gripping handrails to counteract the shockwaves from the drill.

**row 2379; string ID 376210882889609216; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist looks taken aback. He consults some papers on his desk, then leafs through an old encyclopedia.

**row 2384; string ID 376210882893803524; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist inserts a syringe into each of the creatures, now laying long-dead on the slab. From each, in turn, he draws a vial of fluid.

**row 2389; string ID 376210882893803529; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He places the vials of what should be fish blood in a rack in front of him.

**row 2392; string ID 380189447722737664; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The [C0]black box[/C0] has a number of [C0]empty slots[/C0] on the top, one of which has a module inserted into it. It's labelled "[C2]FLEE[/C2]" in electrical tape.

**row 2397; string ID 380189447722737669; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> THERE MAY BE ANOTHER CUTSCENE HERE WHERE THE EXECUTIVE FLIES AWAY.

**row 2403; string ID 380189447722737675; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He holds it up to the light. For a moment, the fluorescent bulbs in the room flicker and buzz, light recoiling in a pattern against the ceiling.

**row 2405; string ID 380189447722737677; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist arranges the specimens, now laid out on a metal table in front of him. He connects electrodes and wires to the head of each.

**row 2408; string ID 380189447726931969; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The room is pierced with the sharp sound of capacitors charging. The corpses on the bench jolt in unison, slackened muscles arch and tense before lying still again.

**row 2409; string ID 380189447726931970; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist is already looking at the four monitors behind him. The same vague shape scrolls across each screen; ragged circle, writhing cell, lidless eye, yawning pit.

**row 2412; string ID 380189447726931973; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He places the specimens into a large metal chamber before sealing the lid. A motor runs briefly, and a pressure gauge on the chamber rises.

**row 2414; string ID 380189447726931975; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He flips a large switch, and the metal chamber begins to spin. Slowly at first, then accelerating until the whole thing is a blur.

**row 2418; string ID 380189447726931979; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With shocking speed he rushes towards the table, grabbing at the corpses.

**row 2419; string ID 380189447726931980; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The shape that bursts through the blackboard barely resembles the Scientist; no longer meek and cowed, now a frightening visage of stretched skin and sharp teeth.

**row 2420; string ID 380189447726931981; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With a sickening crunch, he bites down into the sea scorpion carcass, [C3]black liquid[/C3] dripping from his mouth.

**row 2422; string ID 380189447726931983; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> With limbs overcome by frantic spasms, he stumbles towards the nearest window, slowly at first, then arriving at considerable speed.

**row 2424; string ID 380189447726931985; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The aberrated form of the Scientist crashes through the glass, diving out, beyond the deck of the platform, down, hitting the water, then disappearing into the deep.

**row 2428; string ID 380189447726931989; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A droning voice begins to sound from the top of the rig, reverberating through the metal, down into the deep.

**row 2435; string ID 380189447726931996; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The drill descends once more into the blackness under the rig.

**row 2438; string ID 380189447726931999; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The executive shoots the foreman a glance, disdain and dismissal lacing the muscles on his face. The Foreman backs down, defeated.

**row 2442; string ID 380189447726932003; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist steps away from the nearby window, knuckles still white from gripping the frame moments before.

**row 2443; string ID 380189447726932004; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He turns back and presses his forehead against the glass.

**row 2451; string ID 380189447726932012; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> As the roar of the [C0]giant creature[/C0] fades, it leaves behind a silence on all that witnessed it. Faces stare from windows and hands grip railings, trying to process the spectacle.

**row 2453; string ID 380189447726932014; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A murmur of panic begins to rise from the onlookers.

**row 2456; string ID 380189447726932017; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Executive turns and walks away towards his office, leaving the Foreman shaking his head.

**row 2467; string ID 380189447726932028; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Ironhaven Corporation has been constructing a drilling platform.

**row 2473; string ID 380189447731126274; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> She turns away and tries to recompose herself with a few deep breaths.

**row 2489; string ID 380189447731126290; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The workers on the rig want to find a way to sabotage them.

**row 2499; string ID 380447089028341760; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A helmeted figure emerges from the shadows by a building site. He lifts his visor.

**row 2500; string ID 380447089028341761; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He gestures to a pile of materials behind him.

**row 2503; string ID 380550693299003392; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A woman is stood just inside the doorway, sketching a design in her notebook.

**row 2531; string ID 380861164547842048; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The defenses are operational, emitting a droning sound that grates at the ears.

**row 2559; string ID 384982415776260098; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> You begin to describe the creatures to the Scientist, but as you grasp at the images in your head, they melt away, replaced with a swirling blackness in your mind's eye.

**row 2560; string ID 384982415776260099; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> After your description trails off, the Scientist looks at you with some concern.

**row 2565; string ID 384982415780454400; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Scientist flinches at something in the air above him. A moment later, the lights flicker. The subtle pop of a blown capacitor can be heard somewhere on the nearby bench.

**row 2569; string ID 384982415780454404; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He turns to look at you. One of his eyes is bloodshot, the white completely overrun with dark red blood.

**row 2572; string ID 384982415780454407; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> He rocks back and forth, hand shading his eyes from the harsh lights above.

**row 2577; string ID 384982415805620227; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> The Executive makes a show of looking around and peering over a nearby railing.

**row 2594; string ID 404067128942358534; inferred: third-/second-person scene description**

> A figure emerges from lower decks and beckons you over into the shadows.

## Travelling Merchant (135 strings)

**row 0251; string ID 56610378876112896; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Ahoy there! You're a new face - a fisherman I take it? Perfect! Well, we should get along like a house on fire, ha ha!

**row 0252; string ID 56610378922250240; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'm a traveller and a merchant, all rolled into one! If you have any fish to sell, I'll take 'em on board and sell 'em at the next town I come across.

**row 0253; string ID 56610378922250241; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Likewise, I haul a small cargo of equipment between places. You're welcome to peruse the stock and see if there's anything that takes your fancy.

**row 0254; string ID 56610378922250242; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Now, what'll it be?

**row 0255; string ID 56610378922250243; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Hey there! What are we doing today? Making repairs? Selling fresh fish? Buying new engines? We can do it all here!

**row 0258; string ID 56610378922250246; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Ok!

**row 0259; string ID 56610378922250247; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> What do you want to talk about?

**row 0260; string ID 56610378922250248; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> What else do you want to talk about?

**row 0265; string ID 56610378922250253; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, these cliffs are always fun to come back to. You never know how much more will have fallen down!

**row 0266; string ID 56610378922250254; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'm not sure what's doing it, but they're falling apart strangely quickly, if you ask me.

**row 0267; string ID 56610378922250255; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> There's one old guy still living there though. He must be nuts! Or he must really love his house. Either way: weird!

**row 0268; string ID 56610378922250256; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Yeah, always! I like to keep moving along. The merchanting is secondary to the travelling, really. I'm more of a merchanting traveller!

**row 0269; string ID 56610378922250257; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I get to see a lot of places, which is nice. I also get to meet a lot of people, which is only sometimes nice.

**row 0270; string ID 56610378922250258; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Strange things? Plenty! But I try not to dwell on them. You could go crazy, you know? Hah!

**row 0271; string ID 56610378922250259; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> But, hey, if you're seeing strange things ... maybe you're not seeing them the right way? Maybe they're not so strange after all.

**row 0272; string ID 56610378922250260; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Or perhaps they are, and they're incredibly dangerous. Better not risk it!

**row 0277; string ID 57673131445260288; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Ahoy there! You're a new face - a fisherman I take it? Perfect! Well, we should get along like a house on fire, ha ha!

**row 0278; string ID 57673131466231808; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'm a traveller and a merchant, all rolled into one! If you have any [C0]fish to sell[/C0], I'll take 'em on board and sell 'em at the next town I come across.

**row 0279; string ID 57673131466231809; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Likewise, I haul a small cargo of [C0]equipment[/C0] between places. You're welcome to peruse the stock and see if there's anything that takes your fancy.

**row 0280; string ID 57673131466231810; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Come and see me when you're ready to trade!

**row 0346; string ID 67751478284062722; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'll still buy fish that aren't totally fresh, but if it's completely turned to [C2]Rot[/C2] I can't pay you anything for it.

**row 0347; string ID 67751478284062723; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Hey again - come by for a chat?

**row 0348; string ID 67751478284062724; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Is there anything else you want to talk about?

**row 0352; string ID 67751478284062733; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Yeah, always! I like to keep moving along. The merchanting is secondary to the travelling, really. I'm more of a merchanting traveller!

**row 0353; string ID 67751478284062734; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I get to see a lot of places, which is nice. I also get to meet a lot of people, which is only sometimes nice.

**row 0354; string ID 67751478284062735; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Strange things? Plenty! But I try not to dwell on them. You could go crazy, you know? Hah!

**row 0355; string ID 67751478284062736; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> But, hey, if you're seeing strange things...maybe you're not seeing them the right way? Maybe they're not so strange after all.

**row 0356; string ID 67751478284062737; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Or perhaps they are, and they're incredibly dangerous. Better not risk it!

**row 0456; string ID 73572393058226177; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Assistance from an angler? Absolutely! I've been trying to collect records of where each species of fish is located.

**row 0457; string ID 73572393058226178; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I've figured out most of them on my travels so far, but there are a few I haven't been able to track down. Reckon you could share your info with me?

**row 0460; string ID 73572393058226181; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I know a little about each species already, so maybe I can point you in the right direction.

**row 0461; string ID 73572393058226182; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> What did you want to know?

**row 0462; string ID 73572393058226183; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> What about the other fish?

**row 0464; string ID 73572393058226185; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Given that the [C0]Oarfish[/C0] has a long and fragile body, I suspect they'll live in reasonably deep, but calm water. Maybe the shelter of [C0]Gale Cliffs[/C0] would suit them?

**row 0466; string ID 73572393058226187; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That's great news! I'll add it to my records. Here, take this - I'm sure you'll find some use for it.

**row 0468; string ID 73572393058226189; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I know that [C0]Gulper Eels[/C0] live at the [C0]deepest depths imaginable[/C0]. You'll have to get some specialist gear to catch one of those.

**row 0470; string ID 73572393058226191; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That's great news! I'll add it to my records. Here, take this - I'm sure you'll find some use for it.

**row 0472; string ID 73572393058226193; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> These guys are legitimately scary! They hunt in [C0]murky, swampy waters[/C0], using the shade of trees as cover.

**row 0474; string ID 73572393058226195; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That's great news! I'll add it to my records. Here, take this - I'm sure you'll find some use for it.

**row 0476; string ID 73572393058226197; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Actually, I have no idea where [C0]Coelacanths[/C0] might be. But they have a [C0]primordial feel[/C0] to them, don't you think?

**row 0478; string ID 73572393058226199; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That's great news! I'll add it to my records. Here, take this - I'm sure you'll find some use for it.

**row 0480; string ID 73572393058226201; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That's...that's all of them, wow! I never thought I'd complete these records.

**row 0481; string ID 73599369877835776; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Great! The fish I have left to track down are: [C0]Oarfish[/C0], [C0]Gulper Eel[/C0], [C0]Goliath Tigerfish[/C0] and [C0]Coelacanth[/C0].

**row 1319; string ID 139135624497254408; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Well there's [C0]Ingfell[/C0] just across the bay, which is a dreary little whaling town. The folks there are always in need of something, which keeps me in business.

**row 1320; string ID 139135624497254409; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> The residents are always talking about the cliffs. Most seem afraid. Frankly, I'm not sure why they stay.

**row 1321; string ID 139135624497254410; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Occasionally I hear deep rumblings from within the rocks. I don't hang around here for long.

**row 1322; string ID 139135624497254411; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> What beautiful, crystalline water! It's a shame I can't take my boat too much further in - I've run aground on the coral a few times.

**row 1323; string ID 139135624497254412; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Have you checked out the abandoned resort to the [C0]East[/C0]? It's pretty impressive. There's probably some decent salvage to be had there.

**row 1324; string ID 139135624497254413; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> It's kind of amazing how quickly it all falls to ruin without people around to maintain it. In a few years, these pontoons will probably go the same way.

**row 1325; string ID 139135624497254414; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That's just the way of things, I guess.

**row 1326; string ID 139135624497254415; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Not much, to be honest. I've gone inside just the once. I sailed into that lagoon behind us and saw I could go left, right, or straight ahead.

**row 1327; string ID 139135624497254416; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Something about the place seemed off. Through the fog, it looked like the trees were...[C3]moving[/C3]. I can't explain it. I turned around and sailed right back out.

**row 1328; string ID 139135624497254417; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I sometimes use this spot to rest up for a few days - the sunrises are particularly striking through the haze. Never do any regular business here of course.

**row 1329; string ID 139135624497254418; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Some kind of ancient civilization must've lived here. Judging by the state of the buildings, things didn't go so well for them.

**row 1330; string ID 139135624497254419; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I can't imagine they built all this around an active volcano. So the volcano must've become active some time after they founded their city.

**row 1331; string ID 139135624497254420; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'd guess that the volcano caused them to leave - or wiped them out, one way or another.

**row 1332; string ID 139135624497254421; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> All in all, it's a pretty inhospitable place. I'm not really sure why I come here.

**row 1616; string ID 161626619872727041; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Woah, you don't look so great, friend. When's the last time you got a good night's [C0]sleep[/C0]?

**row 1617; string ID 161626619872727042; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Are you alright there? You're looking a little...jittery. You're free to [C0]rest[/C0] here anytime.

**row 1618; string ID 161626619872727043; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Your hull's got a few holes that need patching. We can [C0]repair[/C0] those here, remember.

**row 1619; string ID 161626619872727044; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Did you run aground on rocks or something? We should make some [C0]repairs[/C0] - I can see right through your hull!

**row 1620; string ID 161626619872727045; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Your boat's sprung a leak! We should [C0]fix[/C0] that.

**row 1621; string ID 161626619872727046; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You should really be more careful! I don't have a limitless supply of [C0]repair[/C0] materials, you know.

**row 1622; string ID 161626619872727047; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I suppose you're going to say you "couldn't avoid" the rock that "appeared out of nowhere"? Yeah yeah, I've heard it all before. Let's get you [C0]fixed[/C0] up.

**row 1623; string ID 161626619872727048; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'm going to have to start carrying more materials soon aren't I? I've got enough to make [C0]repairs[/C0] for now, at least.

**row 1624; string ID 161626619872727049; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> If you have some worn out [C0]Crab Pots[/C0] or [C0]Trawl Nets[/C0], we can fix them here.

**row 1625; string ID 161626619872727050; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Trawl Net[/C0] fraying? Crabs cutting themselves out of your [C0]Crab Pots[/C0]? Why don't we [C0]fix[/C0] them up here?

**row 1626; string ID 161626619872727051; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Don't forget - [C0]Crab Pots[/C0] and [C0]Trawl Nets[/C0] both need repairing every now and then.

**row 1627; string ID 161626619872727052; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Crab Pots[/C0] and [C0]Trawl Nets[/C0] won't work if they're completely worn out, so best to [C0]repair[/C0] them regularly.

**row 1628; string ID 161626619872727053; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'll buy all sorts of fish here - and I can pay the most for [C1]fresh[/C1] ones.

**row 1629; string ID 161626619872727054; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Not all fish are traded equally! Smaller species can sometimes be more delicious - and more valuable - than the larger ones.

**row 1630; string ID 161626619872727055; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> The most successful captains know how to push their vessels to the limit. Is yours running at peak efficiency?

**row 1631; string ID 161626619872727056; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> There's nothing wrong with combining different engine types - do anything you can to get more speed out of the space you have.

**row 1632; string ID 161626619872727057; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Having a variety of fishing tackle on board means you'll be prepared to catch whatever you come across.

**row 1633; string ID 161626619876921344; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Installing a [C0]Trawl Net[/C0] is a great way to earn some passive income while exploring the islands.

**row 1634; string ID 161626619876921345; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> When [C0]Trawling[/C0], pay attention to how [C0]deep[/C0] the water is. Not every type of fish likes deep water! Check your [C0]Encyclopedia[/C0] if you're not sure.

**row 1635; string ID 161626619876921346; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Crab Pots[/C0] can be quite profitable if you can find a good place to drop them. Just don't forget where you put them!

**row 1636; string ID 161626619876921347; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You'll need to pick up your [C0]Crab Pots[/C0] every now and then and bring them in for repairs.

**row 1637; string ID 161626619876921348; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Not every crab can be found at every [C0]depth[/C0]. When finding a spot to drop your [C0]Crab Pots[/C0], maybe check your [C0]Encyclopedia[/C0]?

**row 1638; string ID 161626619876921349; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I hope you're being careful out there. Seeing your boat sail over puts a smile on my face every time.

**row 1639; string ID 161626619876921350; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You and I, we've got to avoid risks as much as we can. There's no crew to bail us out if things go south.

**row 1640; string ID 161626619876921351; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You don't have to try to grab everything you see out there. That wreck salvage isn't going anywhere fast.

**row 1641; string ID 161626619876921352; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I love the sound of rain against the roof of my cabin. Is there anything better?

**row 1642; string ID 161626619876921353; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You'll need to put a few more engines on that thing if you want it to be as fast as mine, hah!

**row 1661; string ID 161656756005687296; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You look tired. If you don't get some sleep, you'll start seeing things! Why don't you [C0]rest[/C0] up before heading back out?

**row 1662; string ID 161656756039241728; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Everything ok? You've just been standing there for a while...need to have a [C0]rest[/C0]?

**row 1663; string ID 161656756039241729; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I know that look on your face. Too long on the open water, eh? Yeah, you start to feel a little mad after a while. Nothing a good [C0]sleep[/C0] won't fix.

**row 1666; string ID 161672749440487425; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I've got a surprise for you! A gift, for my most loyal customer. Here, I want you to have this.

**row 1716; string ID 167839908856254464; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You might be used to [C0]dry docks[/C0] at other ports, but we can make the same [C1]upgrades to your boat[/C1] here at this [C0]floating dock[/C0]. Check it out!

**row 1717; string ID 167839908885614592; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> On this side of the pontoon I'll [C1]buy any fish[/C1] you want to sell. I also keep a stock of [C0]crab pots[/C0] here for sale!

**row 1718; string ID 167839908885614593; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Most of this gear is super heavy, so I don't offload it at every pontoon. But come aboard and see what [C1]equipment I've got for sale[/C1]!

**row 1722; string ID 169244233709047808; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Thanks to the [C0]Retired Whaler[/C0] at [C0]Ingfell[/C0], I'm now carrying a small stock of [C0]Packed Explosives[/C0] - very carefully!

**row 1723; string ID 169244233738407936; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I met a guy in [C0]Twisted Strand[/C0] who bartered with some buckets of [C0]Mixed Bait[/C0]. I thought they'd be good for your fishing - why don't you take a look?

**row 1846; string ID 279017636403154944; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Say, have you met the [C0]Photographer[/C0]? She stopped by my pontoon a little while back.

**row 1847; string ID 279017636403154945; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> She's loaded up with fancy camera equipment to take pictures of all the wildlife.

**row 1849; string ID 279017636403154947; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I wish I had my own [C1]camera[/C1]. Wouldn't it be nice to be able to document everything you do? You'd never forget anything...

**row 1850; string ID 279017636403154948; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Anyway, she said she was setting up on a small island to the [C0]South[/C0] of [C0]The Marrows[/C0]. If you're in the area, maybe you could check on her.

**row 1900; string ID 298319190455144448; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Down here to investigate the ice field too? It seems to have appeared out of nowhere - my best guess is that it drifted up from the south.

**row 1901; string ID 298319190484504576; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Either way, I thought I should set up a pontoon here - but when I went to stake my claim, I noticed some old equipment on the ice...

**row 1902; string ID 298319190488698880; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Come and check it out when you get a second.

**row 1904; string ID 298319190488698882; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> It's so weird, this stuff seems old. Like, really old.

**row 1906; string ID 298319190488698884; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> And come in here - look!

**row 1908; string ID 298319190488698886; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> See how they angled these sheets...and the lining of the metal around the bow...

**row 1909; string ID 298319190488698887; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Whoever was here, I think they were constructing an [C0]Icebreaker[/C0]!

**row 1911; string ID 298319190488698889; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Probably! But...ah, let me see...

**row 1913; string ID 298319190488698891; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> It's a reinforced structure attached to the front of ships to help them [C1]safely cut a path through ice[/C1].

**row 1914; string ID 298319190488698892; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> With these plans, I could probably make another...hmm...

**row 1916; string ID 298319190488698894; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> We've got most of the basic materials here already, but of course, we're [C2]missing the most crucial pieces[/C2].

**row 1917; string ID 298319190488698895; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> An [C0]Icebreaker[/C0] needs [C0]specially tempered, angled metal[/C0], as well as [C0]components designed to minimize the impact[/C0] to your hull.

**row 1918; string ID 298319190488698896; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I can't see any of those here, but...whoever left all this stuff here, maybe they left other pieces elsewhere?

**row 1919; string ID 298319190488698897; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Or maybe you'll be able to find their old ship and salvage the [C0]Icebreaker[/C0] they already built? That would be convenient! Anyway...

**row 1920; string ID 298319190488698898; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> To build a new [C0]Icebreaker[/C0], these are the parts we'll need.

**row 1921; string ID 298319190488698899; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That's everything! Let me just warm my hands up a little, then I'll get started...

**row 1922; string ID 298319190488698900; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, I think that should do it. [C0]The front of your ship can now break through chunks of ice[/C0]!

**row 1923; string ID 298319190488698901; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Go ahead and test it out! I'll be watching from here.

**row 1924; string ID 298319190492893184; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, I forgot to mention - just let me know if you want to [C0]temporarily remove that Icebreaker[/C0]. I can swap it out at any of my pontoons.

**row 1926; string ID 298616943462764545; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I've been looking through the schematics we found before, and there are plans here for another device too!

**row 1927; string ID 298616943462764546; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> They're for an [C0]Ice Shaper[/C0] - that's different to your [C0]Icebreaker[/C0], by the way - it's for cutting chunks of ice.

**row 1928; string ID 298616943462764547; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> If you find the [C2]missing device[/C2], I'll be able to use the machine to harvest [C0]ice blocks[/C0] for refrigeration!

**row 1929; string ID 298616943462764548; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> The [C0]blocks of ice[/C0] can be packed into your cargo to [C1]slow fish spoilage[/C1]. Pretty handy, right?

**row 1930; string ID 298616943462764549; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> This is what the [C2]missing[/C2] [C0]Ice Shaper[/C0] looks like.

**row 1931; string ID 298616943462764550; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> You really found it! How on earth do you actually locate that stuff down there in the deep? Anyway...

**row 1932; string ID 298616943462764551; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Let me slot this blade in here and...yeah, that seems right...I think it's ready to start cutting!

**row 1933; string ID 298616943466958848; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Blocks of ice, coming right up!

**row 1934; string ID 298616943466958849; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> That works pretty well! Here, these are on the house.

**row 1935; string ID 298616943479541760; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> From now on I'll be able to stock some of these ice blocks at my pontoons. Thanks, partner!

**row 2061; string ID 301863303546564608; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> In the meantime, I've got some [C0]new rods for sale[/C0]! They'll be perfect for [C0]fishing in the ice[/C0] around here.

**row 2062; string ID 301863303546564609; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> I'm not exactly sure yet, but I have a theory...have you heard the legend of [C0]The Pale Reach[/C0]? It's an old sailor's tale.

**row 2063; string ID 301863303546564610; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> The story goes that when the fog grows thick enough, the ice appears, seemingly out of nowhere. Hours later, or sometimes days, it vanishes again without a trace.

**row 2064; string ID 301863303546564611; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Back in the olden days, they sent a survey expedition in to investigate. Apparently, it returned with half the crew - either from mutiny or madness.

**row 2065; string ID 301863303546564612; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Travel on the high seas in those days will do that to you, I guess! Anyway, I'm not sure about this place. It could just be regular old ice.

**row 2066; string ID 301863303546564613; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Sure, give me a moment. I've got it stored back here somewhere...

**row 2067; string ID 301863303546564614; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> All done - you're ready to smash some ice!

**row 2068; string ID 301863303546564615; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Sure, give me a moment. I'll need to find some space for it onboard...

**row 2069; string ID 301863303546564616; source label TRAVELLING_MERCHANT_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, that's sorted! I'll keep it with me for when you need it reattached.


## Scientist (96 strings)

**row 2233; string ID 373588922050912256; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> I was planning on conducting [C0]research into fish species[/C0] in the area. But I can't do that without somewhere to study.

**row 2237; string ID 373675301309095936; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Do you have those samples from [C0]The Marrows[/C0] yet?

**row 2238; string ID 373675301309095937; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Have you had time to gather samples from [C0]Gale Cliffs[/C0]?

**row 2239; string ID 373675301309095938; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Have you collected all of those samples from [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0] yet?

**row 2240; string ID 373675301309095939; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> The fish...from [C0]Twisted Strand[/C0]...do you have them?

**row 2241; string ID 373675301309095940; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Closer...pure...[C0]Devil's Spine[/C0]...the wretches...where are they?!

**row 2242; string ID 373675301309095941; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> I WILL TRANSFORM HERE.

**row 2243; string ID 373675301309095942; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Admittedly, the [C2]fissure[/C2] is larger than my models suggested, but it appears to have stabilized. We should consider [C0]halting the drilling[/C0] for now.

**row 2244; string ID 373675301309095943; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Using that rod, I need you to [C0]collect specimen samples[/C0] from [C0]The Marrows[/C0], just to check that the fish species are unaffected by our operations here.

**row 2245; string ID 373675301309095944; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

**row 2246; string ID 373675301309095945; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> YOU SHOULD GO AND GET SOME SAMPLES FROM THE MARROWS.

**row 2247; string ID 373675301309095946; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> YOU SHOULD MAKE SOME DEFENCES. TALK TO MY STUDENT IN STELLAR BASIN, SHE HAS THE TECH.

**row 2248; string ID 373675301309095947; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> YOU SHOULD GO AND GET SOME SAMPLES FROM STELLAR BASIN.

**row 2249; string ID 373675301309095948; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> YOU SHOULD GO AND GET SOME SAMPLES FROM TWISTED STRAND.

**row 2250; string ID 373675301309095949; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

**row 2251; string ID 374365079113408512; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> NOW THAT YOU HAVE AN ADVANCED ROD, YOU CAN COLLECT SAMPLES.

**row 2262; string ID 374399090644852737; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> ...as I was saying. I'll run some quick tests on these specimens. Though from the sounds of things, you may have some additional samples to gather.

**row 2263; string ID 374399090644852738; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Unfortunately there have been reports of some [C3]contaminatants[/C3] leaking from the fissure in isolated pockets.

**row 2264; string ID 374715283217948672; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Have you collected a canister of that [C3]contaminant[/C3] yet?

**row 2265; string ID 374715283331194880; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Thank you. It's always important to know what we're dealing with in operations such as these.

**row 2266; string ID 374734246345809920; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> There's something gigantic, something incredibly powerful, gripping the rig! So...it's really here.

**row 2267; string ID 374734246345809921; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman. This structure...the people on it...can't stand up to this. I have - well, [C0]I had a student[/C0]. She was developing some [C1]technology[/C1].

**row 2272; string ID 374820754201354242; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Yes...remarkable...the power spikes. Hmm. Fisherman, don't forget about those samples.

**row 2298; string ID 375398316051361792; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm?

**row 2309; string ID 375398316063944708; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> My whole professional career has been dedicated to scientific research at the corporation. So that's...well, north of half a century now.

**row 2310; string ID 375398316068139008; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Well I can't go into specifics, you understand, but the purpose of this platform is to test a crucial hypothesis that I've been working towards for years.

**row 2311; string ID 375398316068139009; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> TODO: FILLER 3

**row 2312; string ID 375398316068139010; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, and don't forget about that other thing I asked you to do.

**row 2313; string ID 375402221523005440; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman, considering you're not on the corp's payroll, why don't you take this; a small token of my appreciation.

**row 2314; string ID 375402221523005441; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Remember, you've still got to collect a canister of that [C3]contaminant[/C3] for me.

**row 2325; string ID 376128041841422345; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> All good things, I promise. Say, you must know a great deal about the fish species around here, yes? What've you encountered in these waters?

**row 2330; string ID 376128041841422350; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm. I have some hypotheses that suggest - ah, we'll see...perhaps.

**row 2332; string ID 376128041841422352; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> You may have certain...preconceived notions about the Ironhaven Corporation's modus operandi, but I assure you, everything we do is with the good of the greater ecosystem in mind.

**row 2334; string ID 376128041841422354; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Is that so? Perhaps you'll find me a little more imaginative than others.

**row 2335; string ID 376128041845616640; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Before we get into all of that though, it seems my laboratory is also a victim of a missing supply shipment.

**row 2338; string ID 376128041845616643; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> How's it going out there?

**row 2340; string ID 376128041845616645; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Can I help you?

**row 2342; string ID 376128041845616647; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> What is it?

**row 2344; string ID 376128041845616649; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Yes?

**row 2346; string ID 376128041845616651; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> ...?

**row 2375; string ID 376152647188967425; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> We've broken through! We're underway at last!

**row 2377; string ID 376152647188967427; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Also, we would be remiss in our duties to the local population if we didn't [C0]survey the area[/C0] and [C0]gather some data[/C0]. In that vein, fisherman, I have something for you.

**row 2378; string ID 376152647188967428; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> The [C0]Factory[/C0] has recently become operational, allowing us to manufacture [C1]equipment infused with the corporation's latest breakthroughs[/C1]. This is a new design.

**row 2380; string ID 376210882893803520; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> This is...remarkable. Some of these species...have you caught these before? They haven't been seen outside the fossil record since...well, ever!

**row 2381; string ID 376210882893803521; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> I suppose it could make sense; the fissure opening caused some kind of disturbance in the deep, possibly spooking some of these fish up the water column.

**row 2382; string ID 376210882893803522; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> I'll run some tests on these -

**row 2383; string ID 376210882893803523; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> We must know...where these creatures entered...where the [C3]blood[/C3]...

**row 2385; string ID 376210882893803525; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> This will only take a moment.

**row 2388; string ID 376210882893803528; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> There was always a chance this might happen. The [C0]Engineer[/C0] at the [C0]Factory[/C0] has been working on a system to siphon such [C3]contaminants[/C3] from the water.

**row 2390; string ID 376210882893803530; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> I'd very much like a [C3]canister[/C3] of it.

**row 2406; string ID 380189447722737678; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> The brains of fish are primitive. In many ways, their simplicity is a kind of purity. No burdensome emotions to confuse the data.

**row 2407; string ID 380189447726931968; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Just as rigor mortis locks the muscles, the final shape of the mind is held by the brain. But it can be...extracted.

**row 2410; string ID 380189447726931971; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> The difficulty is in reading it.

**row 2411; string ID 380189447726931972; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> With the corporation leadership here...I have to focus. There are...pressures acting upon me.

**row 2413; string ID 380189447726931974; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> The [C3]elder[/C3]...communicates in power...

**row 2416; string ID 380189447726931977; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Not yet...pure. We'll need more...down...deeper!

**row 2441; string ID 380189447726932002; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> It seems the reservoir is more unstable than we anticipated. Even with the [C1]drill removed[/C1], it appears the [C2]fissure is continuing to expand[/C2].

**row 2444; string ID 380189447726932005; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> It was a way of...[C3]communicating[/C3], I suppose. We were working on emulating a language that we didn't fully understand. She began to twist the words.

**row 2445; string ID 380189447726932006; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> But she didn't agree with Ironhaven's methods. So, leveraging my role on the board for the Research Society, I pulled some strings and had her posted far away. To [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0].

**row 2446; string ID 380189447726932007; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Please, [C0]go and find her[/C0]. I hope she's progressed her research sufficiently to help us.

**row 2529; string ID 380836352886677504; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Don't forget. The rig still needs some kind of defense system constructed. The people here are...depending on you, oddly enough.

**row 2530; string ID 380836352886677505; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> And...while you're there, try to [C0]catch some of the local specimens[/C0]. I think they hold the key to understanding what we're here for.

**row 2548; string ID 384982415767871488; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Our current executive's father hired me when I was fresh out of college. He must've seen something in me, all those years ago.

**row 2549; string ID 384982415772065792; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> If my hypothesis proves correct, it'll yield a significant asset for the corporation's business diversification strategy.

**row 2550; string ID 384982415772065793; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, yes, I believe the drill has already stopped operating. The Foreman should be making preparations to withdraw it from the seabed.

**row 2551; string ID 384982415772065794; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> It's not something you can just yank out, apparently. The drill team will take their time to try to ensure minimal disturbance.

**row 2552; string ID 384982415772065795; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Mutations are present in almost all species, and fish are certainly no exception. In my years of study I've seen fish with extra fins, eyes, and even heads!

**row 2553; string ID 384982415772065796; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Yes, they're a relatively normal part of the development of a species. Occasionally they can be influenced by external factors, but only in exceptional circumstances.

**row 2554; string ID 384982415772065797; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Now that the drill has withdrawn, it should settle down - provided nothing else disturbs the seabed. I expect that, given enough time, it will be filled in with sediment entirely.

**row 2555; string ID 384982415772065798; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> In the meantime, it appears to have opened up some interesting opportunities for research!

**row 2556; string ID 384982415772065799; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Nothing useful, aside from confirming with some peers that they are indeed species that were previously thought to be extinct.

**row 2557; string ID 384982415776260096; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> There's even a special name for that, you know. A Lazarus taxon - a species seemingly raised from the dead.

**row 2558; string ID 384982415776260097; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Oh? What did they look like?

**row 2561; string ID 384982415776260100; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Long nights at sea, unusual weather conditions, sleep deprivation - they can all lead to auditory and visual hallucinations. Not to worry, best to get some rest.

**row 2562; string ID 384982415776260101; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> My...master? Oh, the Executive, yes. It's...he's...everywhere. Very hard working. Always has been. Always will be...

**row 2563; string ID 384982415776260102; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> He needs me...my work. It all has to be correct. Do you understand? We have to be in the right place. Or else...there's not much time left.

**row 2564; string ID 384982415776260103; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> It's...unlike any substance I've ever seen...touched. It's smooth one moment...silky...and the next...sharp...stabbing.

**row 2567; string ID 384982415780454402; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> It holds...secrets...instructions...whispers...

**row 2568; string ID 384982415780454403; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Concerned...no. Concern implies...

**row 2570; string ID 384982415780454405; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Concern implies...that we didn't know this would happen.

**row 2571; string ID 384982415780454406; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> The inevitable...conclusion...

**row 2573; string ID 384982415780454408; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Our work...almost complete...

**row 2602; string ID 408180644154535936; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> Locals in The Marrows and Gale Cliffs have reported sightings of a [C3]strange ooze[/C3] on the surface of the water. I need you to collect a canister of it for me.

**row 2603; string ID 408180644154535937; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> You'll need to upgrade the [C0]Factory[/C0] and construct a [C0]Siphon Trawler[/C0] before you'll be able to do that, though.

**row 2604; string ID 408180644154535938; source label SCIENTIST_NAME_KEY**

> You'll need to equip the [C0]Siphon Trawler[/C0] and deploy it in [C3]contaminated[C3] areas to extract samples from the water.


## Foreman (93 strings)

**row 2152; string ID 344965151744487424; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> What do you want?

**row 2153; string ID 344965151765458944; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> If you're able to [C1]find any of those crates[C1], I'm sure the corp would let you [C1]use our facilities[/C1]. Once we get the [C0]Generator[/C0] wired up, that is.

**row 2154; string ID 344965151765458945; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> That oughta be enough to get the [C0]Generator[/C0] going! I'll get these materials sent up to the main platform.

**row 2155; string ID 344965151765458946; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> So, that should be in working order, but, I just checked the storeroom, and it looks like we [C2]never got any fuel deliveries[/C2].

**row 2160; string ID 344965151765458951; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> We've got what we need.

**row 2161; string ID 344965151765458952; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Let's get to work.

**row 2162; string ID 344965151765458953; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> We're making good progress on getting the Factory fully operational.

**row 2166; string ID 347566488227311616; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> So, this is where we're going to build some sort of machine to repel those tentacles, right? That's what the Scientist said.

**row 2167; string ID 347566488244088832; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> YOU HAVE RETURNED.

**row 2168; string ID 347566488244088833; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> We'll get started.

**row 2169; string ID 347566488244088834; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Defenses are primed. Turning them on now. This had better work.

**row 2174; string ID 347566488248283137; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> That'll do it for the Factory!

**row 2176; string ID 347566488248283139; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Fleet Services root. Is this unbuilt?

**row 2177; string ID 347566488248283140; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Fleet Services description. This is built, right?

**row 2178; string ID 347566488248283141; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, we'll get to work on Fleet Services.

**row 2179; string ID 347566488248283142; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Everything should be operational here, sir.

**row 2183; string ID 347566488248283146; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> That's all we need; we can get the Foundry built now.

**row 2185; string ID 347566488248283148; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, we'll start upgrading the Foundry.

**row 2193; string ID 347566488248283156; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Everything appears to be in order. I'll get the team onto it.

**row 2195; string ID 347566488248283158; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> That's everything. We'll get to work.

**row 2202; string ID 347566488248283165; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, we've got enough material for the [C0]Science Lab[/C0] now too? We'll get to work.

**row 2208; string ID 365251946330025984; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> So, it turns out the fuel never got dispatched from Ironhaven at all.

**row 2209; string ID 365251946355191808; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> You can add fish or...most other biological material, I suppose, to the hopper here. Once it's filled we ought to be able to start the drill.

**row 2210; string ID 365251946355191809; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> It won't hold any more - I'll fire it up!

**row 2213; string ID 371098043570614272; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Here for some cutscene testing, eh?

**row 2232; string ID 373588922038329344; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> While I go and figure out the [C2]fuel situation[/C2], why don't you introduce yourself to the head [C0]Scientist[/C0]? He's always interested to hear about the local fish species.

**row 2234; string ID 373588922050912257; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I think we have a solution for our [C2]fuel problem[/C2] - come see me at the [C0]Generator[/C0] and I'll give you the rundown.

**row 2253; string ID 374399090640658433; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Engage reverse gear! Get that drill out of there!

**row 2255; string ID 374399090640658435; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> FIRE UP THE DRILL!

**row 2257; string ID 374399090640658437; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> FIRE UP THE DRILL!

**row 2258; string ID 374399090640658438; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> What's that noise?

**row 2259; string ID 374399090640658439; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> FIRE UP THE DRILL!

**row 2261; string ID 374399090644852736; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> FIRE UP THE DRILL!

**row 2268; string ID 374734246345809922; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> IT'S BAD ENOUGH THAT THE FLEET SERVICES BUILDING ISN'T UP AND RUNNING YET. CAN YOU HELP WITH THAT?

**row 2295; string ID 375388728665141248; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> These blueprints are confusing...what even is...okay, I think I follow.

**row 2318; string ID 376128041841422338; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Haven't you heard? This is the [C0]Ironhaven Corporation[/C0]'s new drilling rig. Or, it would be, if we were on schedule. Right now it's just a platform.

**row 2319; string ID 376128041841422339; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> The boss's gonna dock our pay for sure if he shows up and the drill hasn't even started. We've got the foundational stuff laid, but we've been running into some supply issues.

**row 2321; string ID 376128041841422341; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> The ships delivering [C0]crates of materials[/C0] keep [C2]going missing[/C2] - and HQ said they're not able to dispatch any more for a few weeks.

**row 2322; string ID 376128041841422342; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Look, I wouldn't normally ask a civilian for help, but we're already so far behind... Why don't I mark the [C0]last known coordinates of those ships on your map[/C0]?

**row 2323; string ID 376128041841422343; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I did find this though. You can use it to patch things up in a pinch. Keep it, it's the least I can do in return for your help.

**row 2347; string ID 376128041845616652; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> That should be enough space for your tubes and fluids, eh?

**row 2349; string ID 376128041845616654; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I was on the radio to HQ: apparently, this is a [C0]Biofuel Generator[/C0]. It's some new tech the corp wanted to trial out here - and they expected us to supply it ourselves.

**row 2350; string ID 376128041845616655; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Thing is, they didn't think to outfit us with any gear for harvesting biomass, and the [C0]Fleet Services[/C0] construction is way down the project list.

**row 2351; string ID 376128041845616656; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Think you could help us out again?

**row 2352; string ID 376128041845616657; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Haha! We've done it!

**row 2354; string ID 376128041845616659; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Er, well, you'd probably be able to use that for chum, right? It's yours if you want it.

**row 2355; string ID 376128041845616660; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> What can I do for you?

**row 2356; string ID 376128041845616661; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else?

**row 2361; string ID 376128041845616666; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Ever since we got the legs of the platform seated on the seafloor, the supply ships started going missing. HQ told us they'd left port, but they never showed up.

**row 2362; string ID 376128041845616667; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I guess the crews thought they could make some money sailing off with the materials and selling them elsewhere. Doesn't seem worth the risk to me.

**row 2363; string ID 376128041845616668; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> The rig is your standard setup, designed to pierce the seabed and collect from fluid reservoirs. It doesn't have much storage capacity though, which is odd. Maybe they're not expecting huge throughput.

**row 2364; string ID 376128041845616669; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I didn't actually think there was an oil field out here, but I suppose the surveyors must have found something - else I wouldn't be here talking to you.

**row 2365; string ID 376128041845616670; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I've been with the corp for about a decade now; started out as a laborer on projects like this one - not always out to sea, mind you - and I've worked my way up to site foreman.

**row 2366; string ID 376128041845616671; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> The corp itself? It's pretty successful, bit of an institution. It's been around for hundreds of years, in one form or another. They always seem to be diversifying into new areas.

**row 2367; string ID 376128041845616672; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> The whole drilling division is guided by the [C0]Scientist[/C0]. He calls the shots when it comes to deciding on sites. Boss seems to listen to him, too.

**row 2369; string ID 376128041845616674; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Initiating drill deployment in 3...

**row 2370; string ID 376128041845616675; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> 2...

**row 2371; string ID 376128041845616676; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> 1...

**row 2373; string ID 376152647138635776; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I'm the site foreman, and this is the [C0]Ironhaven Corporation[/C0]'s new drilling rig. Or, it would be, if we were on schedule. Right now it's just a platform.

**row 2376; string ID 376152647188967426; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> And what about that [C2]huge crack[/C2] that opened up?! Is the reservoir stable?

**row 2398; string ID 380189447722737670; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Uh, is everyone alright?

**row 2401; string ID 380189447722737673; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I guess we'll wait for our airlifts outta here. There's no way I'm travelling anywhere by boat.

**row 2426; string ID 380189447726931987; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Ah hell...that's enough! Drill is clear - I repeat - drill is clear. Shut it down!

**row 2429; string ID 380189447726931990; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> But sir, the seafloor here...it shouldn't be disturbed; this whole rig is built on some kind of...living fault! You have to trust me!

**row 2437; string ID 380189447726931998; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Sir, I don't think - we really shouldn't...

**row 2439; string ID 380189447726932000; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Setting the drill to full power, sir.

**row 2452; string ID 380189447726932013; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Boss, that must have been the thing that got all our material shipments - and [C0]now it's coming for the rig[/C0]!

**row 2454; string ID 380189447726932015; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Uh, it still has a little way to go - but you can't be serious, surely? Not with that thing in the water here?! [C2]Our lives are in danger and you want to keep drilling?![/C2]

**row 2457; string ID 380189447726932018; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> He's insane. What the hell are we doing here? This drill...whatever's down there. [S1]We shouldn't be disturbing it[/S1].

**row 2458; string ID 380189447726932019; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman. You've got to help us. He won't listen to reason. But perhaps he'll listen to an...[C0]operational setback[/C0].

**row 2461; string ID 380189447726932022; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> We can't shut down the drill ourselves - not without [C2]repercussions from the corporation following us for the rest of our lives[/C2].

**row 2462; string ID 380189447726932023; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> But if it were to be damaged by a force of nature or...[C3]force of the deep[/C3], we'd have no choice but to abandon our positions.

**row 2463; string ID 380189447726932024; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I know it sounds crazy. But if the [C0]defenses were to stop operating[/C0] suddenly, it would leave the [C1]drill vulnerable[/C1].

**row 2464; string ID 380189447726932025; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Please. Have a think, see what you can do.

**row 2502; string ID 380447089032536064; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Between you and me, the corp has a pretty ruthless reputation of tracking down anyone who moves against them. People tend to just make the one mistake, if you get what I mean.

**row 2532; string ID 380861164573007872; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> We've gotta get this place operational, or the boss will have my head!

**row 2533; string ID 380861164573007873; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> We can make [C1]improvements to your vessel here[/C1], including some cutting-edge Ironhaven tech that you [C0]won't find anywhere else[/C0].

**row 2537; string ID 380883836463849472; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Now we can finally get this drill started! [S2]ALL CLEAR![/S2]

**row 2580; string ID 385251768564293632; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> There have been areas of [C3]contamination[/C3] sighted in [C0]The Marrows[/C0].

**row 2581; string ID 385251768564293633; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> There have been areas of [C3]contamination[/C3] sighted in [C0]Gale Cliffs[/C0].

**row 2582; string ID 385251768564293634; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> There have been areas of [C3]contamination[/C3] sighted in [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0].

**row 2583; string ID 385251768564293635; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> There have been areas of [C3]contamination[/C3] sighted in [C0]Twisted Strand[/C0].

**row 2584; string ID 385251768564293636; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> There have been areas of [C3]contamination[/C3] sighted in [C0]Devil's Spine[/C0].

**row 2585; string ID 386361585043349504; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> This hull schematic will add a little [C1]more storage[/C1] to your vessel, but the primary benefit is all the [C1]new slots for fishing equipment[/C1]!

**row 2586; string ID 386361585072709632; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Hope she serves you well, fisherman!

**row 2587; string ID 404067128896221184; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Hey, fisherman - thanks again for your help filling that generator. I've reported our new operational status to HQ.

**row 2588; string ID 404067128942358528; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> In fact, they've given me a budget to [C1]compensate you for any additional biomass[/C1] you bring in. Just see me at the [C0]Generator[/C0] and I'll pay you.

**row 2589; string ID 404067128942358529; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I can now [C1]pay you for any biomass you add to the generator[/C1]. The bigger the better, I assume? This isn't really my area...

**row 2590; string ID 404067128942358530; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Generator's always hungry. Any biomass to throw in?

**row 2591; string ID 404067128942358531; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> Energy levels are good, but I won't say no to a top-up. Add some fish if you've got 'em.

**row 2592; string ID 404067128942358532; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> This BioFuel system is great! More places oughta run on fish. And crabs too, I guess. Got any for the hopper today?

**row 2593; string ID 404067128942358533; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> [C2]I can't pay a premium for your fish[/C2] here, but it's better than nothing, right?

**row 2607; string ID 443613968346742784; source label FOREMAN_NAME_KEY**

> I've been trying to keep a record of where all that [C3]dark liquid[/C3] has been showing up. [C0]Come see me if you need pointing to any contamination patches[/C0].


## Researcher (84 strings)

**row 0599; string ID 86592179702779905; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, hello again. What do you need?

**row 0609; string ID 86592179702779915; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Is there anything else?

**row 0610; string ID 86592179702779916; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, um, hello - I wasn't expecting visitors. Who are you?

**row 0612; string ID 86592179702779918; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Ah yes, I thought you had the look. But a fisherman all the way out here? Strange.

**row 0614; string ID 86592179702779920; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> A sightseer - and a fisherman, I presume? I noted the fishing vessel as you docked.

**row 0616; string ID 86592179702779922; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I struggle to believe that! Your ship looks like a fisherman's, and you - you look as though...

**row 0618; string ID 86592179702779924; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Well, it's a pleasure to meet you, all the same. I've not seen another soul in months. In fact, I could do with your help, if you had the time...

**row 0621; string ID 86592179702779927; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I need help collecting samples to study. I was originally posted at the [C0]Research Outpost[/C0] on the interior of the basin. But it was [C2]attacked[/C2].

**row 0622; string ID 86592179702779928; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> A [C0]large creature[/C0] emerged from the depths and destroyed the outpost. Now it rests in the center of the basin.

**row 0623; string ID 86592179702779929; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I fled here and have been making do as best I can. But I had to leave most of my equipment behind, so now my research has come to a halt.

**row 0624; string ID 86592179702779930; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Take a look at the list of specimens I need - please drop them off here. You might need to [C0]experiment with different fishing strategies[/C0] to collect all of them.

**row 0625; string ID 86592179702779931; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Very well then...

**row 0626; string ID 86592179702779932; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, you're back. Did you have time to discuss that matter?

**row 0627; string ID 86592179702779934; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Thank you, looks like you've caught all of the surface specimens I need.

**row 0628; string ID 86592179702779935; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> But ah, yes, I do also need some from...deeper down. But that's going to be a problem.

**row 0631; string ID 86592179702779938; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I developed a device to retrieve samples from the [C0]Abyssal[/C0] zone. This was over in the other [C0]Research Outpost[C0]. Before the attack.

**row 0632; string ID 86592179702779939; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I have an idea of how to keep the creature at bay long enough to collect these samples. But while I work on that, I need you to retrieve some [C0]Prototype Parts[/C0].

**row 0633; string ID 86592179702779940; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**
