> Carefully. I suggest you go during the day. I wouldn't want to be out on the reef at night.

**row 0634; string ID 86592179702779941; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Excellent. Let me assemble a device for you...

**row 0635; string ID 86592179702779942; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> [C1]This should allow you to catch specimens from much greater depths[C1]. It's a little slow, but [C0]you may be able to make some improvements[/C0].

**row 0636; string ID 86592179702779943; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, no room on board? You can come by for it later.

**row 0637; string ID 86592179702779944; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Now, I've developed a [C0]Repulsion Machine[/C0] that produces a bright light and emits a high pitched noise -- [C0]it should deter the creature from attacking[/C0]. Or enrage it, I'm not sure.

**row 0638; string ID 86592179702779945; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Connect it to the [C0]Research Outpost's Generator[/C0]. You'll have to return and re-engage it every few hours, but other than that, it's completely automated.

**row 0639; string ID 86592179702779946; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Great. Now, take a look at the list of specimens.

**row 0640; string ID 86592179702779947; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, no room on board? You can come by for it later.

**row 0641; string ID 86592179702779948; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Remember, you'll want to [C0]install and activate the Machine[/C0] before attempting to collect these. You might also need to [C0]improve that equipment I gave you to catch some of these[/C0].

**row 0642; string ID 86592179702779949; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I have good news and bad news. Which do you want to hear first?

**row 0645; string ID 86592179702779952; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> The good news is that I've honed in on what's causing the abnormalities in the ecosystem.

**row 0646; string ID 86592179702779953; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> The bad news is that the samples you retrieved from the basin have dangerously high counts of cellular mutations.

**row 0647; string ID 86592179702779954; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> This all points to one thing: that thing in the basin is [C2]harmful to life at a cellular level[/C2]. It damages everything around it, whether passively or...with intent.

**row 0648; string ID 86592179702779955; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I need to write up a report on my findings - sorry, our findings - immediately. Maybe now they'll take me seriously.

**row 0649; string ID 86592179702779956; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Thanks again for your help. I'd recommend that you avoid the area. It doesn't seem safe to be near the basin.

**row 0650; string ID 86592179702779957; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Uh, ok - I don't think there's much to say...I studied Biological Sciences at Ironhaven college. It was a struggle.

**row 0651; string ID 86592179702779958; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> There were no other female students in the department that year. Or the year before, or the year before that.

**row 0652; string ID 86592179702779959; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> It's hard not to feel like an outsider in an environment like that, you know? You probably don't.

**row 0653; string ID 86592179702779960; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> When I graduated, I managed to land a job at the Northern Biological Research Society. And then, well, I was posted here. By myself.

**row 0670; string ID 88389352136298496; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Well, I got the sense that it was attacking the [C0]Research Outpost[/C0] itself, rather than me specifically.

**row 0671; string ID 88389352182435840; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I suspect it was disturbed by some of my monitoring equipment. There were dozens of machines running there, all powered by a noisy generator.

**row 0672; string ID 88389352182435841; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> It was huge - larger than any other marine species I've studied. It attacked with tentacles, so I'd guess at some sort of octopus or squid. Or an anemone.

**row 0673; string ID 88389352182435842; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> One of my research vessels was moored around the back of the station, so I managed to slip away in that while it was tearing apart the front.

**row 0674; string ID 88389352182435843; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> There was a lot of valuable data - and equipment - left there. But I'm not going back. I can't - I won't face it again.

**row 0675; string ID 88389352182435844; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Now that you mention it...I've been so preoccupied with the attack and having to rebuild here, I'd almost forgotten...

**row 0676; string ID 88389352182435845; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I found these letters. From my sister. She'd sent them to me while I was studying, before - before she...

**row 0678; string ID 88389352182435847; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Anyway, I kept finding them amongst my papers. I don't remember packing them, but I must have.

**row 0679; string ID 88389352182435848; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I started finding them more frequently - they turned up in my collection nets. In my sampling tubes.

**row 0680; string ID 88389352182435849; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> They were letters I'd never seen before. But they were in her writing. They were in her name.

**row 0681; string ID 88389352182435850; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> They were saying things...talking about things that happened after she died. I can't explain it.

**row 0685; string ID 89155828191514624; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> A dog?! Oh gosh, let me see -

**row 0687; string ID 89155828191514626; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I could definitely take this one off your hands - and I'd love the company. Are you sure?

**row 0689; string ID 89155828191514628; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Yes! Thank you! Oh, we're going to be such good friends. He can be my assistant!

**row 0691; string ID 89155828191514630; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Aw, that's ok. He can come by and visit anytime.

**row 0737; string ID 89186998581121024; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I thought I would relish the peace and quiet. But I miss having company. I've always had someone, or at least a pet. Out here...it's just machines.

**row 0739; string ID 89448783949139968; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh um, that's...interesting. I suppose that's yours. If you want it.

**row 0740; string ID 89448783949139969; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I can clean the rest up, don't worry about it.

**row 1668; string ID 161672749448876032; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, how rude of me. You've done all of this for me and...I've not got much to say thank you with. Um, you should have this.

**row 1670; string ID 161672749448876034; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> This is something I wrote during my studies. I think it'll be useful to you.

**row 2275; string ID 374820754201354245; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I didn't expect that name to follow me all the way out here. Perhaps that was naive of me.

**row 2276; string ID 374820754201354246; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I couldn't give a damn about the rig, or the corp. But not everyone there deserves to face the consequences of the corp's actions.

**row 2292; string ID 375044890999812110; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh? I hope they worked.

**row 2293; string ID 375044890999812111; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> There's one in particular. I've been referring to it by its closest human analogue: "[C3]RISE[/C3]".

**row 2465; string ID 380189447726932026; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> So. What do you want?

**row 2466; string ID 380189447726932027; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> What else?

**row 2472; string ID 380189447731126273; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, and you're helping them with that, I suppose? Do you have any idea what they're even doing?

**row 2474; string ID 380189447731126275; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Of course you don't. You're just trying to help. But you've seen what's happened to the water here - and elsewhere, I imagine.

**row 2475; string ID 380189447731126276; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> And I know him. He's the reason I was posted all the way out here, so far from home. He might've mentioned that I studied under him.

**row 2476; string ID 380189447731126277; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> But I studied him, too. His theories on nature's cycles were...odd. He never went into much detail, but his work had some curious influences.

**row 2477; string ID 380189447731126278; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I heard whispers through the door of his office, saw confusing diagrams that lingered in half-erased chalk dust and on torn up shreds of paper.

**row 2478; string ID 380189447731126279; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Never could make much sense of it.

**row 2479; string ID 380189447731126280; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Well, it's the same tech used in that Repulsion Machine I gave you.

**row 2480; string ID 380189447731126281; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Strictly speaking, I wouldn't call it a defense system. It doesn't interact with anything on a physical level.

**row 2481; string ID 380189447731126282; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> You see, Ironhaven's scientific communication division have long been working on projects to further their exertion over the natural world.

**row 2482; string ID 380189447731126283; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> It's complicated stuff, but essentially they were trying to emit signals to communicate with...well, anything they wanted to, I suppose.

**row 2483; string ID 380189447731126284; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> The tech I've been working on is along those lines, but instead of a wide spectrum of communication, it attempts to broadcast a single 'word': "[C2]FLEE[/C2]".

**row 2484; string ID 380189447731126285; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> It emits the vocalized panic responses of every animal I was able to gather acoustic data for.

**row 2485; string ID 380189447731126286; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll give you some schematics. I'm sure someone there is smart enough to decipher them and get it built.

**row 2486; string ID 380189447731126287; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Just...don't let them think this means I forgive them. Or that I approve of what they're doing.

**row 2487; string ID 380189447731126288; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else about those defenses?

**row 2491; string ID 380189447731126292; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Good. It's always nice to have additional data points.

**row 2492; string ID 380189447731126293; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Found their conscience, have they? I suppose they're wanting it to look like an accident. They wouldn't want to face the corp's wrath if they were found culpable.

**row 2493; string ID 380189447731126294; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm, I do have a pleasingly elegant idea, actually. We don't have to sabotage the defenses in a traditional sense.

**row 2494; string ID 380189447731126295; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> If you recall, they emit a sort of 'word'. At the moment, they're broadcasting a warning: "[C2]FLEE[/C2]".

**row 2495; string ID 380189447731126296; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> But we can change that. I have other recordings, of other words. Instead of a warning, it could broadcast an...invitation.

**row 2496; string ID 380189447731126297; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> There's one in particular. I've been referring to it by its closest human analogue:

**row 2497; string ID 380189447731126298; source label RESEARCHER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll prepare some blueprints for the module. You'll have to assemble it and slot it into the top of the black box.


## Soldier (72 strings)

**row 0782; string ID 108775052937449472; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> How are things going?

**row 0784; string ID 108775052987781121; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Stranger. From that look on your face I'd say you've encountered the local [C3]wretches[/C3]. Take a seat.

**row 0786; string ID 108775052987781123; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I've been trying to rid them from this accursed jungle ever since we wrecked here.

**row 0787; string ID 108775052987781127; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I'd ask you for safe passage away from here, but not until after I've [C0]avenged my squadron[/C0].

**row 0789; string ID 108775052987781129; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> A number of us went down in the fog, crashing into trees or ditching into the water. Those that survived rallied here, under the boughs of this giant tree.

**row 0790; string ID 108775052987781130; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> But it offered us scant protection. Before help could arrive, our numbers quickly dwindled. Picked off by the fiends and the forest.

**row 0792; string ID 108775052987781132; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I've met a few would-be rescuers over the years. But none would help me in my fight. Are you any different to the rest?

**row 0795; string ID 108775052987781135; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I expected as such. Stay safe, if you can.

**row 0796; string ID 108775052987781136; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman. Have you found your courage?

**row 0799; string ID 108775052987781139; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmmph. Be off with you, then.

**row 0800; string ID 108775052987781140; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I figured you'd be up for it. You...you have the look. Listen up.

**row 0801; string ID 108775052987781141; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> First, we need to [C0]find the lost pieces of my squadron's mortar[/C0]. We'll then use that to bombard the beasts.

**row 0802; string ID 108775052987781142; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Other planes went down to the [C0]North[/C0] and [C0]East[/C0] of where we are now. I'll mark the possible crash sites on your map, you go and see what you can salvage.

**row 0803; string ID 108775052987781143; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm?

**row 0813; string ID 108775052987781153; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Shipwrecks? No. Anything that meets its end here is quickly swallowed by the silt.

**row 0814; string ID 108775052987781154; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Devilish creatures, through and through. They'll stalk you along the waterways and emerge suddenly, [C3]screeching their terrible song[/C3].

**row 0815; string ID 108775052987781155; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> You need to [C0]find the lost pieces of my squadron's mortar[/C0]. I've marked some locations to search on your map. Let me know how you get on.

**row 0816; string ID 108775052987781156; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> You've found one component, but it's still missing a piece. Nearly there; keep looking.

**row 0817; string ID 108775052987781157; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> That's the lot. Now, while I work on assembling the mortar, you can get started on the next phase of the plan.

**row 0818; string ID 108775052987781158; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> You may have noticed some [C0]contraptions[/C0] in the lagoons around here. I've assembled those traps for the [C3]Mind Suckers[/C3]. We just need to [C0]lure them in[/C0] there.

**row 0819; string ID 108775052987781159; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Once they're in, you see, I can [C1]fire on them with our new mortar[/C1]. And that'll be the end of them. No more nightmares.

**row 0820; string ID 108775052987781160; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll mark the locations of the traps on your map. Got it?

**row 0821; string ID 108775052987781161; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you got everything?

**row 0823; string ID 108775052987781163; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> They're attracted to some very specific types of [C1]Bait[/C1]. Your skills will come in handy here yet again.

**row 0825; string ID 108775052987781165; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Of course not.

**row 0827; string ID 108775052987781167; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> With patience. The [C0]creatures only seem to sense movement[/C0]. So whenever one was close, I froze.

**row 0829; string ID 108775052987781169; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> We need to [C0]make three special Baits[/C0] from different combinations of fish.

**row 0830; string ID 108775052987781170; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Did you want to know anything else about the [C0]Bait[/C0]?

**row 0835; string ID 108775052987781175; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> In one of the [C0]traps[/C0] I've constructed in the lagoons. They're marked on your [C0]Map[/C0].

**row 0837; string ID 108775052987781177; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> This one takes [C0]three fish[/C0] from the area to make.

**row 0838; string ID 108775052987781178; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> This one needs [C0]two fish[/C0] from the area to make.

**row 0839; string ID 108775052987781179; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> This one requires [C0]two fish[/C0] from the area to make.

**row 0840; string ID 108775052987781180; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> That should do it. I'll just mix these in...here. [C0]Load this into a trap[/C0], then get well clear from it.

**row 0841; string ID 108775052987781181; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> That should do it. I'll just mix these in...here. [C0]Load this into a trap[/C0], then get well clear from it.

**row 0842; string ID 108775052987781182; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> That should do it. I'll just mix these in...here. [C0]Load this into a trap[/C0], then get well clear from it.

**row 0843; string ID 108775052987781183; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> [C2]I don't have all the fish for that bait yet[/C2]. Come back when you've got the rest.

**row 0853; string ID 108775052987781193; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Let's see it then.

**row 0856; string ID 108775052987781196; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Yes...this is one of the beasts. Progress at last. More to go, though.

**row 0858; string ID 108775052987781198; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Another one taken care of. One remains, by my count. Let's finish this.

**row 0862; string ID 108775052987781202; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Well fisherman, some fortune at last. This ought to fetch a decent price from the right collector. Here, why don't you take it.

**row 0864; string ID 108775052987781204; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> And now...well, that's that, I suppose.

**row 0865; string ID 108775052987781205; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, you have more?

**row 0866; string ID 108775052987781206; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I can't say for sure. I thought I'd be asking you for an escort away from this place, but...this has become somewhat of a home to me.

**row 0867; string ID 108775052987781207; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> It doesn't feel right to leave my brothers here. I think I shall stay a while longer.

**row 0868; string ID 108775052987781208; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> In the meantime, I can make up some special [C0]Bait[/C0] for you. [C1]It ought to help with your fishing[/C1].

**row 0869; string ID 108775052987781209; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll need some fish from you first. Any type will do - but [C1]the more you give me, the more Bait I can make[/C1].

**row 0870; string ID 108775052987781210; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Let me just grind these up...won't be a moment...

**row 0871; string ID 108775052987781211; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> This is what I could make. Take it all; it won't keep here.

**row 0872; string ID 108775052987781212; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> [C2]You didn't give me enough fish[/C2], so I can't make up any [C0]Bait[/C0] for you.

**row 0873; string ID 109781194304049152; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> The boys called them [C3]Mind Suckers[/C3], on account of them, well...affecting the mind. They made us see things...do things...

**row 0874; string ID 109781194354380800; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> The first time it happened to me I saw...shadows of my squadmates. Reaching out to me in a feverish dream.

**row 0875; string ID 109781194354380801; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> When I finally snapped out of it, I was covered in bloodied scratches - they must've sent me running through the foliage.

**row 0876; string ID 109781194354380802; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Since then I've done my best to avoid the fiends. [C0]Their senses seem to be based on movement[/C0], so move carefully.

**row 0877; string ID 109781194354380803; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> They go crazy for that stuff - day, night, it doesn't seem to matter. All sorts of fish usually show up.

**row 0883; string ID 109848088373547010; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I've hung a few of their [C0]Dog Tags[/C0] up on the tree behind me. It seemed fitting enough.

**row 0884; string ID 109848088373547011; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> But I've only got tags from the ones I...you must understand...I never found all of their bodies.

**row 0885; string ID 109848088373547012; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> If you happened to [C0]find any of their Dog Tags[/C0], I could add them to the tree with their brothers.

**row 0886; string ID 109848088373547013; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I have a number of mechanical doodads that you might find useful. Here, take this one - in good faith. [C1]I'll give you more for each tag you bring me[/C1].

**row 0887; string ID 109848088373547014; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Let me see.

**row 0888; string ID 109848088373547015; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I...thank you. I'll hang them on the tree. Here, as promised.

**row 0889; string ID 109848088373547016; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Come back when you've actually got something to show me.

**row 0890; string ID 109848088373547017; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> I want to hang the Dog Tags of my fallen squadmates on the tree behind me. [C1]I'll give you some componentry for each tag you bring me[/C1].

**row 1569; string ID 155146783239098368; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> That's all of them...that I can remember, anyways. I can't picture their faces too clearly any more.

**row 1648; string ID 161626619885309953; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> We're making [C0]special bait[/C0] to lure the [C3]Mind Suckers[/C3] into the [C0]traps[/C0] I've built in the lagoons.

**row 1649; string ID 161626619885309954; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Once we've trapped them, I'll fire on them with the mortar. Then you'll [C0]bring back proof[/C0] that they're dead.

**row 1650; string ID 161626619885309955; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> When the trap springs, I'll fire upon it with the mortar. Make sure you [C0]bring back proof[/C0] that the thing's really dead, you hear?

**row 1651; string ID 161626619885309956; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> When the trap springs, I'll fire upon it with the mortar. Make sure you [C0]bring back proof[/C0] that the thing's really dead, you hear?

**row 1652; string ID 161626619885309957; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> When the trap springs, I'll fire upon it with the mortar. Make sure you [C0]bring back proof[/C0] that the thing's really dead, you hear?

**row 1653; string ID 161626619885309958; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Here, this was left over from the other bait we made.

**row 1654; string ID 161626619885309959; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Did you want that leftover bait now?

**row 1655; string ID 161626619885309960; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Right, now, we can make some fresh stuff if you like.

**row 1656; string ID 161626619885309961; source label SOLDIER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't want it right now? Well, come back if you change your mind.


## Mayor (63 strings)

**row 0013; string ID 5797928922845194; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Welcome to [C0]Greater Marrow[/C0]! I must say, quite the dramatic first impression - I see you've already introduced yourself to the [C2]jagged rocks[/C2] along the bay.

**row 0014; string ID 5797928922845195; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Did you not see the lighthouse? It was shining right at you! Ah well, I'm glad to see our [C0]new fisherman[/C0] upright and breathing.

**row 0015; string ID 5797928922845196; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> [C2]Your boat was hopelessly damaged[/C2], but I've had a few of the locals [C1]move your things to one of our old vessels[/C1]. We'll catch up later to discuss more details.

**row 0016; string ID 5797928922845197; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Finally, I don't suppose I need to say this, but [C2]get back by sundown, before the fog rolls in[/C2]. Keep a close eye on the time. It can really creep up on you.

**row 0017; string ID 5797928922845198; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> No fish? [C0]Get out there and catch something[/C0] for these hungry townsfolk.

**row 0019; string ID 5797928922845200; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, I see you've returned in one piece. Very good. Before you head off to town, we should discuss the matter of [C0]your boat[/C0].

**row 0020; string ID 5797928922845202; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> I understand you may not have the necessary funds on hand, so we'll consider it a [C2]loan[/C2]. But I want to make this easy for you.

**row 0021; string ID 5797928922845203; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> So, to recap, you'll need to sell fish to the local market, paying off your debt and, in turn, keeping the population fed and satisfied. Understand?

**row 0024; string ID 5797928922845206; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Our local [C0]Fishmonger[/C0] will appraise and purchase what you catch. He's open all hours.

**row 0025; string ID 5797928922845207; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Off you go then - sell those fish while they're still fresh!

**row 0027; string ID 5832393564950528; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Excellent work, I have no doubt you'll be able to provide for this town.

**row 0028; string ID 5832393564950529; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, one last thing! Our local [C0]Shipwright[/C0] mentioned she might be able to make some [C1]modifications to your ship[/C1]. You should pay her a visit.

**row 0035; string ID 6872154488508416; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Just the person I was hoping to see! Will you be sailing out [C0]East[/C0], towards [C0]Little Marrow[/C0]? Would you give this package to the [C0]Dockworker[/C0] there please?

**row 0036; string ID 6872154509479936; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, sorry, all full up? [C0]Come back when you have more space[/C0] on board.

**row 0037; string ID 6872154509479937; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Don't forget to check your [C0]Compass[/C0] and [C0]Map[/C0] if you get lost!

**row 0097; string ID 11998932739624960; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Fantastic news - thanks partly to your efforts, [C0]Greater Marrow[/C0] is growing! Our town is sure to be swarming with visitors any day now.

**row 0098; string ID 11998932739624961; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> In fact, I've granted a consent for the [C1]Shipwright to expand her facilities[/C1]. The old [C0]Dry Dock is operational again[/C0]!

**row 0099; string ID 11998932739624963; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Keep up the good work. We're glad you've chosen to be here with us.

**row 0145; string ID 23331031994257408; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> What can I do for you?

**row 0146; string ID 23331032040394752; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Now, what else can I do for you?

**row 0151; string ID 23331032040394768; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm? The last fisherman? Oh, he was very unreliable. He'd go out at night, come back with no fish and proceed to sleep all day.

**row 0152; string ID 23331032040394769; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> He was also a terrible captain. I mean, really terrible. Constantly running aground on the reef - even in broad daylight! The Shipwright almost ran out of wood for repairs.

**row 0153; string ID 23331032040394770; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> No, he was no good. One evening he left and never came back. So long! He shan't be missed by us.

**row 0155; string ID 23520128652087296; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Of course! How much would you like to repay?

**row 0159; string ID 23541337972346880; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Fantastic!

**row 0161; string ID 23541337972346882; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Excellent!

**row 0162; string ID 23541337972346883; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Excellent!

**row 0164; string ID 36290924545904640; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Look here, I found this down by the docks - I'm sure someone like you could make something out of it. Why don't you take it?

**row 0168; string ID 42729481831632896; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Until your debt is repaid, a [C2]tiny portion of your fish sales[/C2] will go towards paying it off. A small amount of interest will go towards [C1]improving the town[/C1].

**row 0275; string ID 57660747100000256; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> I'll let you get out there to [C0]catch some fish[/C0]. See if you can fill your cargo while you get your bearings in the light.

**row 0276; string ID 57660747100000257; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> As I mentioned earlier, your [C2]old boat was too badly damaged to be repaired[/C2]. However, I'm more than happy to sell you that replacement vessel - [C1]yours to own[/C1].

**row 0281; string ID 58722874940379136; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Are you able to make that delivery for me now?

**row 0282; string ID 58722875024265218; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> The [C0]Dockworker[/C0] will pay you upon delivery. Please be quick about it, [C2]I don't want it to spoil[/C2].

**row 0288; string ID 66638564550578176; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Shipwrecks? Unfortunately, there have been quite a few in recent years. The lighthouse helps, but even it struggles to cut through the fog some nights.

**row 0289; string ID 66638564575744000; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> We do our best to salvage and clear any shipwrecks that occur around this side of the island. That should make fishing a bit easier for you!

**row 0290; string ID 66638564575744001; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Well, I heard that my counterpart in Little Marrow met the most unfortunate end.

**row 0291; string ID 66638564575744002; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> They say he went mad quite suddenly! One day he was normal, the next, he was pacing the shoreline hurling people's belongings into the sea!

**row 0292; string ID 66638564575744003; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> After some days like this, he disappeared, never to be seen again! Nowadays, "The Tale of the Old Mayor" is used to scare children.

**row 0293; string ID 66638564575744004; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Not many remember him, myself included. And those that do remember don't seem to like talking about it. A most unpleasant subject!

**row 1176; string ID 128994536054771712; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> I've also allocated some money to [C1]improve the Fishmonger's services[/C1]. He might have something to talk to you about soon.

**row 1285; string ID 139135624493060096; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Certainly. Is there somewhere in particular you wish to know more about?

**row 1286; string ID 139135624493060097; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Is there another area you'd like to discuss?

**row 1293; string ID 139135624493060104; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm, I don't know a great deal. It technically falls under the jurisdiction of Little Marrow. I believe it was sold to a private owner some twenty years ago.

**row 1294; string ID 139135624493060105; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> They must've had a special arrangement - such a sale is quite uncommon. Very complicated paperwork involved, you see.

**row 1295; string ID 139135624493060106; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> I've not met the owner; I suspect they don't live around here. The island seems quite uninhabited.

**row 1296; string ID 139135624493060107; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Well, the main port over there is a place called [C0]Ingfell[/C0]. It's right on the approach to the cliffs, you can't miss it.

**row 1297; string ID 139135624493060108; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> It's a whaling town, but I haven't heard much talk of it these days. I think quite a few people have left, one way or another.

**row 1298; string ID 139135624493060109; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Quite a popular tourist destination. There's a resort on the atoll, built on top of a [C0]beautiful coral reef[C0].

**row 1299; string ID 139135624493060110; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> I'm sure there's a wide variety of fish there. Maybe you fancy getting your hooks into some of those?

**row 1300; string ID 139135624493060111; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm, I don't think there's much to tell. It's just a tangled mass of trees.

**row 1301; string ID 139135624493060112; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> It's often blanketed in a thick fog, even during the day. You'd want some [C0]good lights[/C0] and a [C0]good sense of direction[/C0] if you're to go exploring there.

**row 1302; string ID 139135624493060113; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Ah yes, it's quite exciting really. An active volcano amongst a city of ancient ruins!

**row 1303; string ID 139135624493060114; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> [C2]I must caution against sailing there, however[/C2]. The ruins lie low in the water and would surely tear through your hull.

**row 1304; string ID 139135624493060115; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Not to mention the aforementioned volcano could erupt at any moment! You can occasionally hear it rumbling from here.

**row 1526; string ID 152256381829959680; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Around the back though...it's a little harder to get to. There are still some [C0]wrecks[/C0] towards the [C3]deeper patches[/C3]...you'll want to avoid that area.

**row 1574; string ID 161626619830784003; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Oh my, you look exhausted! You're not forgetting to get some [C0]sleep[/C0], are you?

**row 1575; string ID 161626619830784004; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> As I always say: taking frequent breaks is an important part of being productive!

**row 1576; string ID 161626619830784005; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman! What is it? You look a touch troubled - is the fog starting to get to you?

**row 1577; string ID 161626619830784006; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Completely normal around here, and nothing to worry about! With a good night's [C0]rest[/C0], you'll be right as rain by morning!

**row 1579; string ID 161626619830784008; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Are ... ok? If ... see ... fog ... shapes ... [C0]rest[/C0]. Careful ... [C3]nightmares[/C3].

**row 1843; string ID 279017636340240384; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman! Have you run into a [C0]Photographer[/C0] on your travels? She docked here a few days ago for supplies.

**row 1844; string ID 279017636365406208; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> I suggested that she take some pictures of the town - for promotional purposes - but she didn't seem too interested.

**row 1845; string ID 279017636365406209; source label MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> She mentioned she was heading [C0]South[/C0] - perhaps you should introduce yourself.


## Fishmonger (61 strings)

**row 0003; string ID 5797928922845184; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> You're the new fisherman, eh? Surprised they found a new one so fast.

**row 0005; string ID 5797928922845186; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> He...it takes a certain type of person to last out here. It's not a life for everyone.

**row 0007; string ID 5797928922845188; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> There's no sense getting familiar. You'll be on your way like the rest of them soon, no doubt.

**row 0008; string ID 5797928922845189; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Anyway, to business. I'll pay you for fish. [C1]Bigger and fresher means more money[/C1]. Some species are just worth more, too.

**row 0009; string ID 5797928922845190; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Other towns on other islands might pay you different...but while you've still got a debt outstanding here, I'd suggest you work on paying it off first.

**row 0010; string ID 5797928922845191; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> So, let's see what you've got.

**row 0011; string ID 5797928922845192; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> What've you got for me today?

**row 0042; string ID 7217358294814720; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> What is it today?

**row 0044; string ID 7217358294814722; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Ahh...now, this is the sort of specimen to liven up my day. Hmm, I wonder...

**row 0045; string ID 7217358294814726; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> It's not the first corrupted fish I've seen in these parts, so I'm sure you'll find more before too long. [C1]I'll pay you well if you find any more[/C1].

**row 0100; string ID 14501038075744256; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Lousy weather lately - almost makes you not want to get out of bed. But you and I, we've gotta do it, eh? We're what keeps people from going hungry.

**row 0101; string ID 14501038100910080; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I don't know how you do it every day. These old bones wouldn't cut it on the high seas.

**row 0102; string ID 14501038100910081; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Hope the seagulls didn't get to your catch - those blasted pests.

**row 0103; string ID 14501038100910083; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't over-commit yourself - especially when that sun starts going down. Better to come back with a small catch than to not come back at all.

**row 0104; string ID 14501038100910084; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Keep an eye out for larger fish. They'll feed more mouths, so I can pay you more for them.

**row 0165; string ID 36290924571070464; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I found this [C1]Book[/C1] out back. Could be useful to ya.

**row 0303; string ID 66638564575744014; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Well, I've never seen that before in all my days. I'll give you this thing, and I'll pay you for the fish too, of course.

**row 0359; string ID 71737740131225600; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I've had a special order come through from a customer. If you can fulfil it, I'll pay you above normal prices.

**row 0362; string ID 71737740181557250; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> They asked for [C0]one Gulf Flounder[/C0] and [C0]one Grey Eel[/C0]. Just bring 'em in when you have them.

**row 0363; string ID 71737740181557251; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> You'll need to have a rod that can handle [C0]Shallow[/C0] water fish in order to catch those. The shipwright should be able to sort that for you.

**row 0364; string ID 71737740181557252; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you got any fish for that order?

**row 0368; string ID 71737740181557256; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Good, good. They should be pleased with these.

**row 0369; string ID 71737740181557257; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I've got another order here. This one's a little more curious. They want [C0]a couple of Squid[/C0] and [C0]a whole Black Grouper[/C0].

**row 0370; string ID 71737740181557258; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't see many people fishing squid these days - [C0]they only feed at night[/C0], and well, you know what nights are like 'round here. Good luck.

**row 0371; string ID 71737740181557259; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you got any fish for that order?

**row 0375; string ID 71737740181557263; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I didn't think you had it in you; night fishing is a tall order.

**row 0376; string ID 71737740181557264; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Been meaning to talk to you about a new business venture I've been considering - [C0]Crab Pots[/C0].

**row 0377; string ID 71737740181557265; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Should be pretty straightforward. Just [C0]drop it out at sea anywhere[/C0], then come back after a day or two to check on it.

**row 0379; string ID 71737740181557270; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> These'll do. Glad you got some use out of that old pot. You can always [C0]buy more here[/C0], or [C1]repair damaged ones at the Shipyard[/C1].

**row 0380; string ID 71737740181557272; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I have one final request for you. As you've seen, some fish around here can develop odd...[C3]deformities[/C3].

**row 0381; string ID 71737740181557273; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> What do you think happens to a person that eats the flesh of one of these mutants?

**row 0385; string ID 71737740181557277; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Well...perhaps. But I want to know for sure. Bring me a mutant fish - any will do. Just...keep this between the two of us.

**row 0386; string ID 71737740181557278; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you got any of those mutants with you now?

**row 0390; string ID 71737740181557282; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Yes...this should do nicely. Here's what the fish is worth. And why don't you take these too - they're of no use to me.

**row 0391; string ID 71737740181557283; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm? Do you hear that? It sounds like...the [C3]faintest whispering[/C3]...

**row 0396; string ID 71737740181557288; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Please. Sell what you need, then leave. I must deal with this creature. Quickly now!

**row 0399; string ID 71737740181557291; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Ugh...blinding headache today. Did you come by yesterday? I don't recall... Anyway, what can I do for you?

**row 0400; string ID 71745170072326144; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright.

**row 1148; string ID 128651020623597568; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Not all schools of fish are the same. Some contain...[C3]abnormal specimens[/C3]. Sometimes you can even tell just looking from the surface.

**row 1158; string ID 128695983315013632; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Just leave whatever you have here. I'll keep the order on ice until it's all ready to go.

**row 1159; string ID 128695983340179456; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, come back when you have everything. Now, to regular business, eh?

**row 1161; string ID 128706042413633536; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Just leave whatever you have here. I'll keep the order on ice until it's all ready to go.

**row 1162; string ID 128706042417827840; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, come back when you have everything. Now, to regular business, eh?

**row 1163; string ID 128706042417827841; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I've got one here for you, in fact. It's a bit [C2]banged up[/C2], but you could get it [C1]fixed at the Shipwright[/C1].

**row 1164; string ID 128706042417827842; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Here's that [C0]Crab Pot[/C0] I was going to give you earlier.

**row 1165; string ID 128706042417827843; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> You can come by and grab it later, I guess. To regular business then.

**row 1166; string ID 128706042417827844; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I've got a customer after [C0]a Fiddler Crab[/C0] and [C0]a Common Crab[/C0]. Both species can be found around here.

**row 1167; string ID 128706042417827845; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you got any crabs for that order?

**row 1169; string ID 128706042417827847; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't forget, if your [C0]Crab Pots[/C0] get a bit [C2]worn out[/C2], the [C1]Shipwright should be able to patch 'em up[/C1].

**row 1170; string ID 128706042417827848; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, come back when you have everything. Now, to regular business, eh?

**row 1173; string ID 128989530735955968; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll let you know when I get more orders in. For now, on to business as usual.

**row 1174; string ID 128989530769510400; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Remember, any [C3]aberrant[/C3] will suffice. I suspect they're all the same...underneath.

**row 1175; string ID 128989530769510401; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, come back when you have one for me. Now, to regular business, eh?

**row 1177; string ID 128998044824948736; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> There's something else I wanted to - hmm...

**row 1178; string ID 128998044824948737; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> No. Forget it.

**row 1520; string ID 152256381771239424; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm. Haven't seen that [C3]strange red light[/C3] in a while. Wonder what it was.

**row 1521; string ID 152256381825765376; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you seen that [C3]strange red light[/C3] at night? It seemed to be rising from the [C0]West[/C0].

**row 1522; string ID 152256381825765377; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> If you're ever hunting for specific species, check your [C0]encyclopedia[/C0]. It could have the information you need.

**row 1523; string ID 152256381825765378; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Some fish prefer [C0]deeper[/C0] water, while others stick to the [C0]shallows[/C0]. If you're looking for something specific, it's important to know what [C0]depth[/C0] you're working with.

**row 1570; string ID 161626619805618176; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Make sure you're getting enough [C0]rest[/C0]. Tired eyes won't fare well in these waters.

**row 1571; string ID 161626619830784000; source label FISHMONGER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't let yourself get too worn down. We need you [C0]well-rested[/C0] to provide for this town.


## Retired Whaler (45 strings)

**row 0431; string ID 73248624187985954; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> What is it?

**row 0432; string ID 73248624187985955; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else?

**row 0438; string ID 73248624187985963; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> The real wreck around here is the settlement at Gale Cliffs.

**row 0439; string ID 73248624187985966; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Some time ago, just after the last whale was brought in, there was a sudden exodus from Gale Cliffs.

**row 0440; string ID 73248624187985967; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Dozens of ships sailed past, packed up and hurrying away north as fast they could.

**row 0441; string ID 73248624187985968; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> They were waving and shouting something at us from their boats - but we couldn't make it out.

**row 0442; string ID 73248624187985969; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Never did see or hear of them since.

**row 0443; string ID 73248624187985970; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Ingfell used to be a whaling town - emphasis on the "used to be". We would go out, bring in a carcass and then the rest of the town would get to work.

**row 0444; string ID 73248624187985971; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> But the whales got scarce some twenty years ago - and now look at us. Barely scraping by for a new candle.

**row 0445; string ID 73248624187985975; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> See, I've been trying to [C0]clear some debris[/C0] in the pathways through the cliffs [C1]to make a shortcut[/C1]. With explosives.

**row 0446; string ID 73248624187985976; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> I was able to set a charge, but the cliffs seemed particularly unsteady that day, so I didn't risk detonating it.

**row 0447; string ID 73248624187985977; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> You just need to get in there and set it off. It's not far in - I left some yellow flags nearby, you can't miss it.

**row 0448; string ID 73248624187985979; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Head through the rock arch to the [C0]South-East[/C0]. Follow the channel, and you'll see the yellow flags on the right.

**row 0449; string ID 73248624187985980; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> You've done it - we heard the blast from here! Gods, the echo. I'm surprised the cliffs didn't come down on your head.

**row 0450; string ID 73248624187985981; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> I've got things ready here. [C0]Go and tell my brother[/C0]...that he can come home.

**row 0452; string ID 73255230845931520; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Come for some more [C0]Packed Explosives[/C0]?

**row 0453; string ID 73262937019699201; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Come by the old [C0]Whaling Yards[/C0] anytime.

**row 0540; string ID 81572268593041423; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Little brother. You're home.

**row 0542; string ID 81572268593041425; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> We both were. Come on inside. Let me get your bags.

**row 0544; string ID 81572268593041427; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> My brother ditched us and moved on over to those cliffs, years ago. I suspect he's regretting his decision now, the devil.

**row 0545; string ID 81572268593041428; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Gods...I haven't seen this since...hmmph. So he did take it. I knew it.

**row 0547; string ID 81572268593041430; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm. I don't know if...I was in a right state when he left.

**row 0549; string ID 81572268593041432; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> I bet he is. Maybe I can clear the...hmm.

**row 0550; string ID 81572268593041433; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Ok. Fine. He can stay in my old workshop, at least for the time being. But I'll need some time to clear it out.

**row 0551; string ID 81572268593041434; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> While I'm doing that, perhaps you can do something for me.

**row 0552; string ID 81579320249864192; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, stranger. If you find any more debris that needs clearing...I'm working on making some more [C0]Packed Explosives[/C0].

**row 0555; string ID 81892477604270081; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> There's something I forgot to mention last time we spoke.

**row 0556; string ID 81892477604270082; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> If you find any more debris that needs clearing...I've got more [C0]Packed Explosives[/C0] out the back.

**row 0557; string ID 81892477604270083; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Come by the old [C0]Whaling Yards[/C0] if you need it.

**row 0558; string ID 81892477604270084; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, and any news from my brother? Don't tell me he's hiding below deck. I'm not still mad at him - I promise!

**row 0560; string ID 81892477604270086; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Wh- what? No...how could...

**row 0562; string ID 81892477604270088; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> At least...at least the crest is home. He always loved that thing.

**row 0563; string ID 81892477604270089; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> When he was a kid, he'd take it off the wall and just look at it. Dad gave him a belting every time.

**row 0564; string ID 81892477604270090; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> I was never mad that he took it. I was just upset that he left. And I...I should've made more of an effort to fix things.

**row 0566; string ID 81892477604270092; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh. Well...let him know that he's always welcome to change his mind. I'll keep the room made up for him.

**row 0568; string ID 81892477604270094; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh. Well...I'm glad he moved on, at least. I understand him not wanting to come back here. Too many memories, and all that.

**row 0569; string ID 81892477604270095; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Thanks for what you've done, all the same. It was...some form of help, in the end.

**row 1534; string ID 152256381838348293; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Aye, I did. Sometimes the carcasses needed breaking up. It wasn't the most efficient...or the more pleasant, but it got the job done.

**row 1535; string ID 152256381838348294; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> I've been thinking about getting back into it - I might be able to rustle up something strong enough to [C0]clear those debris piles[/C0] in the cliffs...

**row 1536; string ID 152256381838348295; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Ah...but I'm not sure. I don't have the energy these days.

**row 1538; string ID 152256381838348297; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, I'll think on it some more. But I make no promises.

**row 1657; string ID 167514184756678659; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't want them right now? Well, come back if you change your mind.

**row 1658; string ID 167514184756678658; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> I hope you can put them to good use...wherever you find cause for it.

**row 1659; string ID 167514184756678657; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Did you want those explosives now? I held onto them for you.

**row 1660; string ID 167514184756678656; source label RETIRED_WHALER_NAME_KEY**

> Here, this is on the house - for all your help.


## Lighthouse Keeper (43 strings)

**row 0083; string ID 7657931879288832; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Why are you here?

**row 0087; string ID 7657931879288837; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> There's nothing here for someone like you anymore. Do yourself a favor and move on.

**row 0112; string ID 22896635703005184; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> What is it?

**row 0116; string ID 22923535622262784; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> ...

**row 0119; string ID 22923535664205826; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> There's a spot near the [C0]back of Greater Marrow[/C0] where the current carries in wreckage from the sea.

**row 0294; string ID 66638564575744005; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> So if you're looking for what I think you're looking for, it's best you just move on.

**row 0295; string ID 66638564575744006; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> I see plenty of strange things from atop the lighthouse. Sometimes I don't know whether or not I can trust my own eyes.

**row 0296; string ID 66638564575744007; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Are you really standing here in front of me? Or is this just another of the fog's tricks?

**row 0297; string ID 66638564575744008; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> That's...he's...I try not to think about him. It's a painful memory for us all.

**row 0298; string ID 66638564575744009; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> But I know he's gone - and I don't want to talk about it any more.

**row 0358; string ID 68169881398349824; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Some nights I still think I can hear the hum of his engines sailing past the bay...

**row 0915; string ID 116750453726957569; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> You're really asking me? After all this time? You know it must be returned.

**row 0917; string ID 116750453726957572; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Go and do what's right -- for her. [C3]Throw it back.[/C3]

**row 1309; string ID 139135624493060121; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> I see a lot from up here, you know. I saw that ill-fated boat return. I chose to look the other way.

**row 1310; string ID 139135624493060122; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> I watched an unnatural glow flicker through those windows. Whatever you've got planned, I hope you know what you're doing out there.

**row 1333; string ID 139145582555090944; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Nobody knows. It's changed recently. It lingers in the mornings now, where before...it didn't seem to have the strength.

**row 1334; string ID 139145582584451072; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Even in the midday sun I feel it. A malice concealed in shadows, waiting for night to sweep the island.

**row 1336; string ID 139145582584451074; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> If this light should ever falter...then gods help us.

**row 1337; string ID 139145582584451075; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> The fog...it's behaving...it's as though it's reacting to the lights that cut through it. It swirls and crashes against the edges.

**row 1338; string ID 139145582584451076; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> From the top of the lighthouse I can see [C3]dark figures seething in the thickest tracts[/C3], out beyond [C0]Little Marrow[/C0] where the lights dwindle.

**row 1339; string ID 139145582584451077; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Of all that I've seen, your lights seem to anger them the most.

**row 1340; string ID 139145582584451078; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> [C3]Relics[/C3]? [C3]Collector[/C3]? I'm not sure what you're hoping to achieve with this...character.

**row 1341; string ID 139145582584451079; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Do you even know? Have you stopped to consider it?

**row 1465; string ID 149408775223300099; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> You have to move on. For her sake as well as yours. Take control...be rid of that thing once and for all.

**row 1466; string ID 149408775223300100; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> You've had it this whole time, have you not? It doesn't matter. Make the most of this window of lucidity. Now is the time to act.

**row 1467; string ID 149408775223300101; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Do you still remember where it happened? In that gloomy darkness behind the bay. When you are ready, I'll point the way.

**row 1476; string ID 149408775223300110; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> We must wait until night is thickest. Until the ocean is as it was...all those years ago.

**row 1477; string ID 149408775223300111; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> I understand. Do what you must. But do not delay this any longer than is necessary.

**row 1478; string ID 149408775223300112; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Return to me when you can. I will point the way.

**row 1518; string ID 149802280655441920; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> It's time. I'll point the way.

**row 1519; string ID 149802280680607744; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Follow my light[/C0].

**row 1527; string ID 152256381829959681; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Some nights you can see an...[C3]unnatural glow[/C3] rising from that patch of dark water. I can't imagine what's down there in the deep...

**row 1582; string ID 161626619834978305; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh...it's you.

**row 1585; string ID 161626619834978308; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Your engine, it sounds like...I'm sorry, I mistook it for the vessel of an old friend.

**row 1588; string ID 161626619834978311; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> I saw you sailing out towards the horizon. Crossing that deep expanse. Do you really think that's wise?

**row 1590; string ID 161626619834978313; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Maybe this time. You need to be more careful.

**row 1591; string ID 161626619834978314; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Even you, with all of your experience, cannot truly know the ocean. Nobody can.

**row 1593; string ID 161626619834978316; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> It feels a bit like tempting fate, does it not?

**row 1594; string ID 161626619834978317; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Things are bad enough around here without your provocations.

**row 1597; string ID 161626619834978320; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> You must've been around most of the islands by now. Have you found whatever it is you're looking for?

**row 1600; string ID 161626619834978323; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> How can you be so sure? The last time you thought you knew...and look what happened.

**row 1603; string ID 161626619834978326; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Remember that you are in control. If you're having doubts, it's foolish to blindly persevere. You can always look for alternative paths.

**row 1606; string ID 161626619834978329; source label LIGHTHOUSE_KEEPER_NAME_KEY**

> Why do you mistrust me so? What have I done to warrant such suspicion?


## Engineer (40 strings)

**row 2156; string ID 344965151765458947; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Hey there. Welcome to the Factory. I'm the lead engineer here.

**row 2157; string ID 344965151765458948; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Because of your help getting things running here, I'm permitted to [C1]improve your equipment with our tech[/C1].

**row 2158; string ID 344965151765458949; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> These are some designs I've been tinkering with for passive harvesting methods.

**row 2159; string ID 344965151765458950; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> We've got various off-cuts and scraps here, I can usually turn them into something.

**row 2163; string ID 344965151765458954; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, here's what we'll need to put that together.

**row 2164; string ID 344965151765458955; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> That should do it.

**row 2165; string ID 344965151765458956; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Hey, you forgot to take this with you earlier.

**row 2170; string ID 347566488244088835; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> I reckon we can repurpose some of that slag from the Science Lab. Toss it into the water, see what turns up to investigate.

**row 2171; string ID 347566488244088836; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> The Factory's core facilities should be up and running.

**row 2172; string ID 347566488244088837; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> The Factory's core facilities have been improved. We can make some more items now.

**row 2173; string ID 347566488248283136; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman, I think you're going to like what we can make here now.

**row 2175; string ID 347566488248283138; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> This Factory's running at peak efficiency. We've got all we need.

**row 2190; string ID 347566488248283153; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> When the [C0]Tech Lab[/C0] is operational, we'll be able to work on projects of a more technical nature.

**row 2191; string ID 347566488248283154; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> We're not quite ready for construction yet. Missing materials - can you help?

**row 2192; string ID 347566488248283155; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> This wing is for the [C1]enhancement[/C1] of boat functions; [C0]lights[/C0], [C0]horn[/C0] and [C0]spyglass[/C0].

**row 2194; string ID 347566488248283157; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> At last. I'm excited for you to try some of these out.

**row 2196; string ID 347566488248283159; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> The Tech Lab is fully built now!

**row 2197; string ID 347566488248283160; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> These are a little complex; here's the list of items I'll need.

**row 2198; string ID 347566488248283161; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> That one's done.

**row 2199; string ID 347566488248283162; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, you left this here before. I held onto it for you.

**row 2236; string ID 373675301304901632; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> HERE IS A ROD THAT I PREPARED EARLIER.

**row 2399; string ID 380189447722737671; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> All accounted for in the Factory.

**row 2402; string ID 380189447722737674; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Yeah, I'll sit tight for now. Fisherman, the main buildings are mostly undamaged, so if you still need stuff made, we can sort you out.

**row 2504; string ID 380550693303197696; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Welcome back. Hope things have been steady for you out there.

**row 2505; string ID 380550693303197697; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Those enhanced rods are treating you well? I worked hard on those designs.

**row 2506; string ID 380550693303197698; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> We don't fully understand the properties of all the materials we're working with, but it's something the head Scientist is working on.

**row 2507; string ID 380550693303197699; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Remember that the things we build here all need different ingredients. It's good to keep a stock of various materials on hand.

**row 2508; string ID 380550693303197700; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Most of these projects I'm working on are for the rollout of the corp's new fleet. It'll be cutting edge.

**row 2509; string ID 380550693303197701; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> We're still waiting for a [C0]materials[/C0] shipment before we can [C1]upgrade the factory[/C1]. When it arrives, we'll be able to [C1]make new items[/C1].

**row 2510; string ID 380550693303197702; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> HQ said they're not sending any more delivery ships, so if we want to get this place [C1]upgraded[/C1], we'll need [C0]materials[/C0] from elsewhere.

**row 2511; string ID 380550693303197703; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Almost there. One more delivery of [C0]materials[/C0] and we'll be [C1]fully operational[/C1].

**row 2518; string ID 380550693303197710; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> I have plans for an interchangeable module system. You'll see; I'll show you when we're set up here. But, uh...

**row 2519; string ID 380550693303197711; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> These modules can be attached to your ship. Placement's flexible too, they can be wired up to wherever they need to go.

**row 2520; string ID 380550693303197712; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> One more lot of [C0]materials[/C0], then we'll be able to [C1]upgrade some of your boat's basic functions[/C1].

**row 2521; string ID 380836352853123072; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Can you believe the size of that squid? It's shaking the whole damn rig. Doesn't seem like it should be possible; the legs are anchored pretty deep.

**row 2522; string ID 380836352882483200; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> We're in the planning stages for an [C1]expansion to the Factory[/C1]. Maybe you'd be able to help out?

**row 2523; string ID 380836352882483201; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> I've been drawing up some plans for a [C1]new Factory wing[/C1]. Take a look if you get a chance.

**row 2524; string ID 380836352882483202; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> We're about ready to start construction on the [C1]final section of the Factory[/C1].

**row 2527; string ID 380836352882483205; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> That squid thing keeps shaking the rig. It's making it pretty damn hard to concentrate in here!

**row 2528; string ID 380836352882483206; source label ENGINEER_NAME_KEY**

> Foreman says we're due to start on an [C1]extension[/C1] to the building soon.


## Hermit (40 strings)

**row 0409; string ID 73248624187985924; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Hello again...what can I do for you?

**row 0410; string ID 73248624187985925; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> What else do you want to know?

**row 0415; string ID 73248624187985931; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Someone that used to live here kept their ship moored at the base of the cliff...until it was struck by some falling rocks and sunk to the bottom.

**row 0416; string ID 73248624187985932; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> They were devastated at the loss - we all were - it was a beautiful boat, decorated with all manner of memorabilia from their travels.

**row 0417; string ID 73248624187985933; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> At low tide, I can still see what's left of the masthead, poking through the waves.

**row 0418; string ID 73248624187985934; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> They're being consumed from the core by that [C3]hateful serpent[/C3] - have you seen it?

**row 0419; string ID 73248624187985935; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Nobody believed me. Not until it started devouring our foundations, opening up chasms beneath our homes.

**row 0420; string ID 73248624187985936; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> It was nightmarish - waking in the night to the sound of a creature slithering somewhere underneath.

**row 0421; string ID 73248624187985937; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> It seems to have turned its attention elsewhere...but not before it took everything from me.

**row 0422; string ID 73248624187985938; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Aye...I've been stubbornly holding out here for quite some time. Truth be told, I've been reconsidering my position lately.

**row 0423; string ID 73248624187985939; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> I have been happy here for so long. My wife and I had such a beautiful life. Until the collapse.

**row 0424; string ID 73248624187985940; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Now I'm alone, and cut off by land and sea - left only with these trembling rocks on all sides.

**row 0427; string ID 73248624187985943; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Hmmph.

**row 0428; string ID 73248624187985947; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Right. Well. I'm packed, and ready to go.

**row 0430; string ID 73248624187985949; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Not enough space for me and my things? I can wait for you to make some room.

**row 0519; string ID 81572268588847107; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> It's not that simple. My brother would never...you see, I used to live in Ingfell with my brother. He was a Whaler, back in the day.

**row 0520; string ID 81572268588847108; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Things were bad. We fought a lot. Mostly about how our parents' inheritance should be divided. We were in the prime foolishness of our youth.

**row 0521; string ID 81572268588847109; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> When my wife and I decided to move here, I took the [C0]Family Crest[/C0]. I stole it from him. Now, after all this time...I can't return without it.

**row 0523; string ID 81572268588847111; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> That's where the problem lies.

**row 0525; string ID 81572268593041408; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Again. It's not as simple as that.

**row 0526; string ID 81572268593041409; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> After the collapse...that creature...it dragged debris from the houses down into its wretched burrows. [C2]The crest went down with it[/C2].

**row 0527; string ID 81572268593041410; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> It's a lost cause, I know. But if you were to find it...it's a large, whale-bone crest. It would mean the world to me.

**row 0529; string ID 81572268593041412; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Thank you. But please - be careful.

**row 0531; string ID 81572268593041414; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, I won't.

**row 0532; string ID 81572268593041415; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> You've found it! I never thought I'd see this beauty again.

**row 0533; string ID 81572268593041416; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> But now...I have a further favor to ask. Would you take this to my brother at Ingfell?

**row 0534; string ID 81572268593041417; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Tell him...tell him his brother is sorry, and would like to come home.

**row 0535; string ID 81572268593041418; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> You were going to keep an eye out for my [C0]Family Crest[/C0]. It was dragged into the tunnels by that creature.

**row 0536; string ID 81572268593041419; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> You were going to take the [C0]Family Crest[/C0] to my brother at Ingfell and ask if he'll forgive me.

**row 0537; string ID 81572268593041420; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> He does? That's...a huge weight off my chest. One so old I hardly remembered it was there.

**row 0538; string ID 81572268593041421; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Well, here we are-

**row 0541; string ID 81572268593041424; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> I - I'm so sorry. I was so foolish.

**row 1529; string ID 152256381838348288; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> It patrols the passageways of the cliffs, but it's lair is somewhere towards the center, [C0]by the large waterfall[/C0].

**row 1530; string ID 152256381838348289; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> I've marked the area on your map. Perhaps you could look there for it...but if it's too troublesome, don't worry about it.

**row 1531; string ID 152256381838348290; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm, you'd probably need some explosives to shift those rocks. My brother at [C0]Ingfell[/C0] used to make explosives...

**row 1532; string ID 152256381838348291; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> For the whaling operations, you see. Nasty stuff. But with the whales all gone...I can't imagine he's still in business.

**row 1541; string ID 152281662775021569; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, ah, greetings! Don't get many visitors around here...what is it that you need?

**row 1644; string ID 161626619876921355; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> It's been burrowing tunnels through the base of the cliffs. You can see the [C0]gigantic holes[/C0] it leaves just below the surface.

**row 1645; string ID 161626619876921356; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> As it forces its way out, it often causes [C0]rock falls[/C0]. So if you plan on going in there...keep an ear out.

**row 1646; string ID 161626619876921357; source label HERMIT_NAME_KEY**

> Should it catch sight of you, well, you should be able to outrun it - it's [C0]quite slow[/C0] - provided you can navigate the passageways.


## Photographer (40 strings)

**row 1802; string ID 277234494252060672; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Ahem...Hello. Sorry, would you step out of the way for a moment? You're in my shot.

**row 1804; string ID 277234494273032193; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> No, this simply won't do!

**row 1808; string ID 277234494273032197; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> The angle of this lens is all wrong! I'll never get a close-up shot of anything with this. And the others...I fear they were lost overboard.

**row 1810; string ID 277234494273032199; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I was sailing from [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0] you see, squeezing my boat though a [C0]narrow gap between two islands[/C0] - and then I crashed into some coral.

**row 1811; string ID 277234494273032200; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I thought I heard something in the water behind me, so I dared not stop. If you were able to [C0]retrieve the lenses[/C0] for me, ah, let me see...

**row 1813; string ID 277234494273032202; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I have a spare [C1]camera[/C1] here that I won't need. You're welcome to it - if you can [C0]find those lenses[/C0].

**row 1814; string ID 277234494273032203; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> You have the lenses! Oh, I do hope they've not been damaged by the seawater...

**row 1816; string ID 277234494273032205; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> They seem mostly unharmed - nothing that some careful polishing can't remedy, at least.

**row 1817; string ID 277234494273032206; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> As promised, you should take my spare [C1]camera[/C1]. The lens is nothing special, but it should allow you to capture some good shots.

**row 1818; string ID 277234494273032207; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> If you wanted some advice on locating wildlife to photograph, just say the word!

**row 1819; string ID 277234494273032208; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Good day, fisherman. Are you interested in learning about the local wildlife?

**row 1820; string ID 277234494273032209; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else?

**row 1827; string ID 277234494273032216; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I suspect you're more knowledgeable in this particular area than I am!

**row 1828; string ID 277234494273032217; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> There are a few shark species around, most notably the huge [C0]Whale Shark[/C0]. They tend to prefer the warmer waters around [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0].

**row 1829; string ID 277234494273032218; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> They're completely harmless, as I'm sure you know, but do be careful around them. You wouldn't want your propellors causing them any injury.

**row 1830; string ID 277234494273032219; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> While still relatively scarce, I believe a few Cetaceans - that's Whales and such - can be found in the deeper waters around here.

**row 1831; string ID 277234494273032220; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> You might be lucky enough to spot a pod of [C0]Bottlenose Dolphins[/C0] or [C0]Killer Whales[/C0] - they've been known to swim alongside boats.

**row 1832; string ID 277234494273032221; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> As for the larger Whales, I've heard [C0]Humpbacks[/C0], [C0]Sperm Whales[/C0] and even the odd [C0]Blue Whale[/C0] have been sighted in the area.

**row 1833; string ID 277234494273032222; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> It doesn't surprise me to hear that their numbers have dwindled, what with Ingfell's Whaling endeavours. Hopefully their populations will recover, in time.

**row 1834; string ID 277234494273032223; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> You'll hear quite a wide variety of birdsong from the islands, though I suspect they're all too small to spot from your boat.

**row 1835; string ID 277234494273032224; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> There's sure to be a few larger species - [C0]Gulls[/C0] and [C0]Ravens[/C0] - loitering around the more active docks looking for morsels of food.

**row 1836; string ID 277234494273032225; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> The ruins in [C0]Devil's Spine[/C0] host a few breeding pairs of [C0]Sea Hawks[/C0]. You're bound to hear them calling to each other, though they're quite elusive.

**row 1837; string ID 277234494273032226; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I'm glad you asked! Not many consider reptiles when they think of the ocean.

**row 1838; string ID 277234494273032227; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Loggerhead Turtles[/C0] are a common sight in the temperate waters around [C0]The Marrows[/C0] and [C0]Gale Cliffs[/C0], though they can be hard to spot in the water.

**row 1839; string ID 277234494273032228; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> When it comes to larger reptiles, they don't get any bigger than [C0]Salt Water Crocodiles[/C0]. The mangroves of [C0]Twisted Strand[/C0] are the perfect environment for them.

**row 1840; string ID 277234494273032229; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**
