> You were going to try to [C0]find my case of extra lenses[/C0]. It must've fallen overboard when I hit some coral to the [C0]West[/C0], towards [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0].

**row 1841; string ID 278654577373417472; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll mark on your map where I think I lost them.

**row 1842; string ID 278654577386000384; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Good day, fisherman. How can I help you?

**row 1936; string ID 299009947304095744; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman! I didn't expect to see you this far south. Well, this is a fortunate turn of events.

**row 1937; string ID 299009947316678656; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm, hello, I don't think we've met. A fisherman, I presume? Please, try to keep your engine noise to a minimum. It tends to frighten the wildlife.

**row 1938; string ID 299009947316678657; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> There's a [C0]Narwhal[/C0] somewhere in this ice field. I couldn't get any further in, so I've been trying to take a picture from here with no luck.

**row 1939; string ID 299009947316678658; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I tried placing some scraps of fish on that ice sheet over there to entice it, but I didn't have enough to hold its attention for long enough. I only caught a glimpse.

**row 1940; string ID 299009947316678659; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I don't suppose you have any fish you'd be prepared to donate to the pursuit of wildlife photography?

**row 1941; string ID 299009947316678660; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Fill the space[/C0] entirely with [C0]any fish[/C0] you like, I can't imagine the [C0]Narwhal[/C0] is too picky.

**row 1942; string ID 299009947316678661; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> That's perfect. Now, cut your engines - we need to wait in complete silence.

**row 1943; string ID 299009947316678662; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Ssh! I think I hear something under the ice...

**row 1944; string ID 299009947316678663; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> What on earth was that thing?! I'm not sticking around to find out!

**row 1946; string ID 299009947316678665; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> You know, that was [C3]no regular Narwhal[/C3]. It seemed voracious and hyper-aggressive.

**row 1947; string ID 299009947316678666; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> Animals like that tend to be very unpredictable. These ice fields aren't safe with that thing around...you should consider turning back.

**row 1948; string ID 299009947316678667; source label PHOTOGRAPHER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll be out of here as soon as I've packed my things away.


## Fanatic (38 strings)

**row 1058; string ID 127200836446244869; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> What is it?

**row 1059; string ID 127200836446244870; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else?

**row 1068; string ID 127200836446244879; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> O rapturous day, a fresh disciple carried to me atop the waves! Come ashore, weak thing, and rest your weary soul.

**row 1070; string ID 127200836446244881; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Nonsense!

**row 1072; string ID 127200836446244883; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Your arrival is providence from the deep itself! I have been calling...and now, you are the answer.

**row 1073; string ID 127200836446244884; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> The rite of purification is almost at hand, and you are to be my replacement.

**row 1074; string ID 127200836446244885; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> The rite of purification is...overdue. Are you ready to begin?

**row 1077; string ID 127200836446244888; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> The sea will return you to me in time.

**row 1078; string ID 127200836446244889; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Your trial begins immediately. Tell me, what does your spirit seek?

**row 1080; string ID 127200836446244891; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Hah! Merely an excuse to halt the search for something greater. Happiness is, by definition, a compromise.

**row 1082; string ID 127200836446244893; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Liar. Nobody seeks sadness. Truly, you seek pity.

**row 1084; string ID 127200836446244895; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Yes. A pure vessel. Only those devoid of mortality can ascend past it.

**row 1085; string ID 127200836446244896; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> And your flesh. What does it crave?

**row 1087; string ID 127200836446244898; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> And yet it eludes you. Darkness is cast over the rest of your days.

**row 1089; string ID 127200836446244900; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Pain is a trick of the flesh. Do not surrender to it.

**row 1091; string ID 127200836446244902; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> I sense it in you...a glimmer of what waits below.

**row 1092; string ID 127200836446244903; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Finally. What does the world deserve?

**row 1094; string ID 127200836446244905; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> In a way. It deserves all that is washed ashore - freely given from the deep. But no more than that.

**row 1096; string ID 127200836446244907; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Exactly. And yet it takes, endlessly. Harvesting, draining. But soon it will know that it has taken too much. It is already too late.

**row 1097; string ID 127200836446244908; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> The trial is concluded. You still have much to learn. But that knowledge will be delivered to you in time.

**row 1098; string ID 127200836446244909; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Now. Your role, as my initiate, is to act as [C0]Herald of the Purge[/C0]. Just as I was, for the one before me.

**row 1099; string ID 127200836446244910; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> The nearby spires blaze with eternal fire, yet the stone hearths of these three idols lie barren and void.

**row 1100; string ID 127200836446244911; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Retrieve [C3]three Fathomless Flames[/C3] from the crags behind us and ignite the crucibles.

**row 1101; string ID 127200836446244912; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Visit the [C0]Shrines of the Deep[/C0]. I will mark them. They will offer further guidance - and I must provide no more.

**row 1102; string ID 127200836446244913; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> I can see that. But we cannot proceed in darkness - all three must be lit.

**row 1103; string ID 127200836446244914; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Ahh. I can feel it. The chill of the deep touches us now. It is time. Come.

**row 1106; string ID 127200836446244917; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> It is almost yours. May it mark the start of your journey...and the end.

**row 1127; string ID 127200836446244938; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> You are to prepare the ritual. You must light the stone crucibles around the temple with [C3]three Fathomless Flames[/C3].

**row 1128; string ID 127200836446244939; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> You will receive further illumination at the [C0]three Shrines of the Deep[/C0]. They are scattered about not far from here.

**row 1129; string ID 127200836446244940; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> Relics, you say? Perhaps...one such as this?

**row 1131; string ID 127200836446244942; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> You are drawn to it too, I see. It found me during my rite of proving as I scavenged for creatures of power.

**row 1132; string ID 127200836446244943; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> It sapped at my spirit when I first recovered it. But I came to appreciate its strange nourishment, with time.

**row 1133; string ID 127200836446244944; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> As will you. When the ritual is prepared, it will be yours to carry forth.

**row 1134; string ID 127200836446244945; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> There are pretenders. Failures that make a mockery of the deep. I tried...correcting their ways.

**row 1135; string ID 127200836446244946; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> But I soon saw that they had lost the path. I left them to their ceaseless hunger.

**row 1136; string ID 127200836446244947; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> I found it...at the end of my other life. It washed ashore, as all great gifts do.

**row 1137; string ID 127200836446244948; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> It took some time for me to decipher the runes. It speaks of an incomprehensible door.

**row 1138; string ID 127200836446244949; source label FANATIC_NAME_KEY**

> And those wretched, twisted fish that swam through...their flesh is the closest thing to a key.


## Painter (38 strings)

**row 1740; string ID 250808444337065994; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Which area do you want to paint?

**row 1755; string ID 250808444337066009; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> As you wish...

**row 1756; string ID 250808444337066010; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Which flag do you want to display?

**row 1762; string ID 250808444337066016; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> I can adjust bunting here. I'll use any leftover pigments I have to ensure a cohesive aesthetic.

**row 1797; string ID 251577961164230657; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> What color do you want to paint the [C0]hull[/C0]?

**row 1798; string ID 251577961164230658; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Which flag do you want to display?

**row 1851; string ID 286263959544205312; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> What can I help you with today?

**row 1853; string ID 286358330792808449; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> What color do you want to paint the [C0]roof[/C0]?

**row 1854; string ID 286358330792808450; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Alright, now let me prepare my brushes...

**row 1855; string ID 286358330797002752; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Mmm, much better. That color looks good.

**row 1856; string ID 286358330797002753; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> I don't have the right pigment to make that color. You'll need to bring me these [C3]crabs[/C3]:

**row 1857; string ID 286358330797002754; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> A different color, perhaps?

**row 1860; string ID 286636517166747648; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> As you wish...

**row 1861; string ID 286636517166747649; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> So, you've found a scrap of fabric eh? Let me see...

**row 1862; string ID 286636517166747650; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> A unique design. Do you want to attach it now?

**row 1865; string ID 286636517166747653; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> You don't seem to have any new flags with you. Come back when you find some.

**row 1871; string ID 287064896420515840; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, the fisherman...I had thought you might stop by. Welcome to my humble studio.

**row 1872; string ID 287064896433098752; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> If you're wanting to...experiment with some decorations...yes, I'm the person to talk to.

**row 1874; string ID 287064896433098754; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> I can attach [C0]flags[/C0] and [C0]bunting[/C0] to add some style to your boat. I have a design here already that you might like.

**row 1875; string ID 287064896433098755; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> If you [C0]find any others[/C0] on your travels, [C0]bring them back to me[/C0] and I can rig them up for you.

**row 1876; string ID 287064896433098756; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Of course, I can also add a splash of [C0]colour[/C0] to your hull. I'm sorry to say that [C2]my selection of pigments limited at the moment[/C2], though...

**row 1879; string ID 287064896433098759; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm...

**row 1881; string ID 287064896433098761; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> The other day, down by the shoreline, I saw a [C3]strange crab[/C3] with the most strikingly vibrant coloration.

**row 1882; string ID 287064896433098762; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> My knees aren't suited to clambering over rocks and chasing crustaceans, you see, but if you caught such a creature...

**row 1884; string ID 287064896433098764; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> With the right binding agent...we can grind them together for a new mixture...

**row 1886; string ID 287064896433098766; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman. Bring me some of those [C3]strange crabs[/C3]. We'll turn your vessel into a work of art!

**row 1887; string ID 287064896433098767; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Yes, of course. As I mentioned previously, if you're able to [C3]bring me crabs of the most unusual colors[/C3], we can grind them up for their pigment.

**row 1889; string ID 287064896433098769; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> That should be all we need...let me grind them together...

**row 1890; string ID 287064896433098770; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> That flag's been taken down for you now.

**row 1891; string ID 287064896433098771; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Okay, I've removed the bunting for now.

**row 1892; string ID 287073026999128064; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> But, ah, for now...

**row 1893; string ID 288867811497938944; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you tried searching to the [C0]North[/C0] of [C0]The Marrows[/C0]?

**row 1894; string ID 288867811514716160; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you tried searching to the [C0]South[/C0] of [C0]The Marrows[/C0]?

**row 1895; string ID 288867811514716161; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you tried searching around [C0]Gale Cliffs[/C0]?

**row 1896; string ID 288867811514716162; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you tried searching around [C0]Stellar Basin[/C0]?

**row 1897; string ID 288874556261732352; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you tried searching around [C0]Twisted Strand[/C0]?

**row 1898; string ID 288874556265926656; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you tried searching around [C0]Devil's Spine[/C0]?

**row 1899; string ID 289917227480829952; source label PAINTER_NAME_KEY**

> Although...you already have so many - I'm not sure there are any more out there...


## Shipwright (31 strings)

**row 0001; string ID 3699562844168; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> What'll it be?

**row 0030; string ID 5877743084441600; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> You must be the new fisherman. [C1]I can make improvements to your vessel[/C1] in the yard.

**row 0031; string ID 5877743084441601; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Mind you, I'm not in the business of doing favors 'round here - payment is upfront. And [C2]everything takes some time to install[/C2].

**row 0032; string ID 5877743084441602; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Also, if you take on any [C2]damage[/C2] from the rocks or...any damage at all, [C1]I can patch it up[/C1]. Mostly.

**row 0034; string ID 5877743084441604; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Take a look 'round. Just remember - [C2]the bigger the equipment, the longer it takes to install[/C2]. So plan ahead.

**row 0105; string ID 14501038100910085; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Sorry, sorry, it's a mess in here. I've just been so busy lately, you know how it is. Exhausting.

**row 0106; string ID 14501038100910086; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> I've got a good feeling about these engines. They'll take you far away...reliable, too, provided you take care of 'em.

**row 0107; string ID 14501038100910087; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> You'll want a variety of tackle to catch all the fish out there. Make sure you're prepared for whatever you come across.

**row 0108; string ID 14501038100910088; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Damage to your hull is bound to stop engines and such from functioning right. Be careful near rocks.

**row 0109; string ID 14501038100910089; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> A single unlucky hit is enough to knock out an engine...and you wouldn't want to be without a motor when the fog rolls in.

**row 0110; string ID 14501038100910090; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> If any of your equipment stops working, head back here for repairs.

**row 0111; string ID 14501038100910091; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Make sure you check out the specs before you buy this stuff -- once it's installed, no refunds.

**row 0166; string ID 36290924571070465; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> If you're wanting to get a bit more out of your engines, take a look through this book. On the house.

**row 0176; string ID 48602169095671808; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> This is the Dry Dock. We can make [C1]significant improvements[/C1] to your vessel here.

**row 0177; string ID 48602169158586368; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> We're talking [C1]extra space[/C1], [C1]more attachment points for equipment[/C1], and even [C1]superior hulls[/C1].

**row 0178; string ID 48602169158586369; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> We'll need a decent stock of hard materials for these projects - [C0]Lumber[/C0], [C0]Metal Scraps[/C0], that sort of thing.

**row 0179; string ID 48602169158586370; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Materials assigned towards upgrades here will be stored[/C0]. So don't worry if you only have half of what we need.

**row 0181; string ID 48900075161833472; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> There's plenty of decent materials around the coast, from various shipwrecks and the like. I'm sure you'll find a way to get at them.

**row 0183; string ID 50720212412190720; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Say...where's your fishing gear? Sure hope you have enough money to buy some replacement tackle.

**row 0186; string ID 50720212466716674; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm. That's a worry. I might have something out back...

**row 0187; string ID 50720212466716675; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, here we go. Don't worry about giving it back, it's a piece of junk really.

**row 0188; string ID 50720212466716676; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Just don't tell the Mayor I gave you something for nothing. He'll lecture me about taxes for an hour.

**row 0189; string ID 50721087457251328; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> If you say so.

**row 0594; string ID 84488207162478592; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> You sure you don't need it? Well, come back if you change your mind.

**row 1151; string ID 128651020652957698; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Your boat's looking a bit banged up. I can [C1]repair[/C1] that here if you like.

**row 1152; string ID 128651020652957699; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Looks like some of your [C0]equipment could use some fixing[/C0]. We can [C1]repair[/C1] that here.

**row 1524; string ID 152256381825765379; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> That [C3]crimson glow in the sky[/C3] seems to have gone away. The air feels quieter now...more settled. Have you noticed?

**row 1525; string ID 152256381825765380; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> The skies were lit up with a weird [C3]crimson glow[/C3] the other night...did you see it?

**row 1572; string ID 161626619830784001; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> You're looking a bit worse for wear. When's the last time you had a good night's [C0]sleep[/C0]?

**row 1573; string ID 161626619830784002; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> I'd caution against operating a vessel if you're feeling [C0]overtired[/C0]. Your reaction times are slowed - rocks and things will seem to jump out at you.

**row 1704; string ID 164879788375461888; source label SHIPWRIGHT_NAME_KEY**

> Remember, you don't have to have all the materials at once - you can [C0]store[/C0] whatever you have right here at the [C0]dry dock[/C0].


## Ingfell Resident (27 strings)

**row 0200; string ID 53694989150113792; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> What is it?

**row 0201; string ID 53694989196251136; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else?

**row 0208; string ID 53694989196251143; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Many years ago, the cliffs used to be a haven for smugglers and pirates. Legend tells of two pirate lords who used Gale Cliffs as their hideout.

**row 0209; string ID 53694989196251144; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Supposedly, they got into some disagreement, and a huge battle was fought around the [C0]South[/C0] side of the cliffs. There certainly are a large number of wrecks in that area.

**row 0210; string ID 53694989196251145; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Both sides were completely destroyed - their [C1]treasure hoards[/C1] left abandoned and unclaimed to this day. I dare say they'd be worth a lot, if anyone knew where to find them...

**row 0211; string ID 53694989196251148; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> We catch our fish ourselves here, so we don't have much need for buying any.

**row 0212; string ID 53694989196251149; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> But there's a merchant that travels around, she's usually anchored [C0]across the bay[/C0]. She [C0]buys and sells[/C0] almost anything - and she does [C0]repairs[/C0] too.

**row 0213; string ID 53698726484692992; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> I've been trying to prepare a special meal for my partner, but I've been having some issues.

**row 0214; string ID 53698726484692993; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Around here we consider [C2]aged[/C2] [C0]Conger Eels[/C0] to be a particular delicacy. You may say they're '[C2]rotting[/C2]', but they're delicious if prepared correctly.

**row 0215; string ID 53698726484692994; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Normally, we bury them in the ground and allow them to mature. But our dog keeps digging them up and eating them.

**row 0216; string ID 53698726484692995; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> I can't raise the subject without spoiling the surprise. Can you bring me an [C2]aged[/C2] [C0]Conger Eel[/C0]?

**row 0218; string ID 53698726484692997; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Thank you. You'll be decently compensated.

**row 0220; string ID 53698726484692999; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, ok.

**row 0221; string ID 53698726484693000; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> I still need that [C2]aged[/C2] [C0]Conger Eel[/C0], if you would be so kind.

**row 0222; string ID 53698726484693001; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Perfect! This will do wonderfully. I can offer you a small sum, in thanks.

**row 0223; string ID 53698726484693002; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Safe travels.

**row 0404; string ID 73248624137654272; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Have you heard the tale of Magran? He was an old whaler from these parts.

**row 0405; string ID 73248624187985920; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> As the whales got more scarce, the crews spent longer and longer at sea trying to find them. Magran, it seems, wasn't cut out for this.

**row 0406; string ID 73248624187985921; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> He formed an...unnatural attachment to a scroll he found on one of these trips.

**row 0407; string ID 73248624187985922; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> He says the scroll told him how to become immortal by eating mutated fish. People started calling him "[C0]Magran The Fishman[/C0]".

**row 0408; string ID 73248624187985923; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> One day, the town awoke to find all the boats' nets and lines had been slashed in the night. And old Magran was nowhere to be found - nor has he been seen since.

**row 0512; string ID 81466753196150784; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Ah yes, they can be tricky to find. I'm told they [C0]inhabit the holes and burrows within the cliffs[/C0] themselves.

**row 0513; string ID 81466753196150785; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> I imagine they've been fished out around the edges, so [C0]you may have to venture into the passageways of the cliffs to find them[/C0].

**row 0514; string ID 81466753196150786; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, and did I mention that [C0]they only come out at night[/C0]? Yes, I believe they hide during the day.

**row 0515; string ID 81466753196150787; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Good luck.

**row 1156; string ID 128651020652957703; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Let me see - oh, hmm. That's a [C0]Conger Eel[/C0] alright, but it's not sufficiently [C2]aged[/C2]. If I take it now, the dog is sure to snatch it before it ages fully.

**row 1157; string ID 128651020657152000; source label INGFELL_RESIDENT_NAME_KEY**

> Please, come back after it's had some time to mature.


## Dockworker (26 strings)

**row 0039; string ID 6892242369318912; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm, what - a delivery? Let's see here...

**row 0040; string ID 6892242390290433; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> I was given this old book a few weeks back, but it didn't make much sense to me. You'll surely get more use out of it.

**row 0041; string ID 6892242390290434; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Keep it in your cabin, perhaps you can read it while you're on the water.

**row 0133; string ID 23225168753135616; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> How's the day treating ya?

**row 0134; string ID 23225168761524224; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> What else can I do for ya?

**row 0139; string ID 23225168761524229; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> I know there were a few 'round the Eastern side of the island. Mostly rotted away by now I'd expect. Or dashed apart by the waves.

**row 0140; string ID 23225168761524230; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Poor souls keep hitting rocks in the fog, I guess. There's a blind spot back there where the lighthouse can't be seen. People get turned around.

**row 0141; string ID 23225168761524232; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Supposedly, some twenty-odd years ago, a fisherman from these parts hauled up an old sealed casket from the seafloor.

**row 0142; string ID 23225168761524233; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> They pried it open and took what was inside for themselves.

**row 0143; string ID 23225168761524235; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> As changeable as the weather, hah! Most days it's pretty quiet. The pay's not too great, but the work's straightforward - that suits me to a tee.

**row 0144; string ID 23225168761524236; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> It's good to get that fresh sea breeze without actually being on the water. No offense, but you wouldn't get me out on those seas every day.

**row 0170; string ID 42750363706773504; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Stop by for a chat any time. I know how lonely it can be.

**row 0284; string ID 58722875024265220; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Yeah, nice and fresh. This'll do nicely. I'm to pay you, I suppose?

**row 0286; string ID 58722875024265222; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Ugh, this smells foul. You obviously took your sweet time getting here with this.

**row 0287; string ID 58722875024265223; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> He won't let me pay for a delivery in this state. But, hmm. Maybe I can give you this...

**row 0319; string ID 66638564579938304; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> That very night, the fog rolled in for the first time - and it has cloaked the seas every night since.

**row 0320; string ID 66638564579938305; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> What was in that accursed casket nobody knows. All of the crew that laid eyes on its contents went mad - or worse.

**row 0321; string ID 66638564579938306; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> The casket and crew have since disappeared without a trace. But the fog remains. Crazy stuff, huh?

**row 0323; string ID 66664618359066624; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> I overheard a tale in the tavern some years ago. Sounded pretty strange to me.

**row 1181; string ID 130128175261995008; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> ...

**row 1183; string ID 130128175261995010; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> A delivery, eh? Oh, it's a big one. Let me see...

**row 1185; string ID 130128175261995012; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Much appreciated, friend. Now, if you'll excuse me...wish me luck!

**row 1312; string ID 139135624497254401; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> That old ruin? Doubt it. Uh, let's see.

**row 1314; string ID 139135624497254403; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Thought so. It doesn't even have a shipping address. As far as I know, nobody lives there.

**row 1728; string ID 190976896515772416; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> You look lost, fisherman. Are you after somewhere to sell your catch? [C0]Greater Marrow[/C0] is on the [C0]West[/C0] side of the bay.

**row 1729; string ID 190976896532549632; source label DOCKWORKER_NAME_KEY**

> Keep an eye on the [C0]lighthouse[/C0], eh? Wouldn't want to lose sight of it for too long.


## Builder (24 strings)

**row 0061; string ID 7581114124275712; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> What's it like out there?

**row 0065; string ID 7581114124275716; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> It's got to be a damn sight better than this bloody town. I think I just need to get away for a while -- maybe not come back.

**row 0066; string ID 7581114124275717; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Going backwards is a mistake, don't you think? You should always keep moving forward. Moving along.

**row 0071; string ID 7581114124275722; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Say, if you find me some materials, maybe you can help me move? I mean, really move. There's an island I've heard about, [C0]Steel Point, just to the North[/C0].

**row 0072; string ID 7581114124275723; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Two units of Lumber[/C0] and [C0]Two units of Scrap[/C0] is all I need. I can make a new place for myself out there. You'd be welcome to visit, too.

**row 0073; string ID 7581114124275724; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Just [C0]stack it up at Steel Point[/C0] when you have anything. I'll pay you when the job's done.

**row 0076; string ID 7581114124275730; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> You've done it? Well, no sense in wasting time. Let's get moving.

**row 0077; string ID 7581114124275732; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Good riddance to you, [C0]Greater Marrow[/C0]. I'm onward bound.

**row 0078; string ID 7581114124275733; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> No room on deck? [C0]Come back when you have more space[/C0]. I'll be waiting.

**row 0079; string ID 7581114124275734; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Yes...yes, this'll do. Thank you, fella.

**row 0080; string ID 7581114124275735; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Give me a short while to get things built here. You're welcome back any time.

**row 0081; string ID 7581114124275736; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Take these. Always good to have on hand.

**row 0120; string ID 23200539179290624; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> What is it?

**row 0121; string ID 23200539196067840; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else?

**row 0126; string ID 23200539200262145; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Bring [C0]Two units of Lumber[/C0] and [C0]Two units of Scrap[/C0] to [C0]Steel Point[/C0], which is an island just to the [C0]North[/C0].

**row 0127; string ID 23200539200262146; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> I want to build a place for myself there.

**row 0128; string ID 23200539200262147; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> There are...too many painful memories here - I don't want to be living them any more. It's a bit difficult to talk about - you know how it is.

**row 0129; string ID 23200539200262148; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> It doesn't do you any good to dwell too deeply on the past.

**row 0130; string ID 23200539200262149; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Greater Marrow? Well, it was once a bright and thriving little town, if you can believe it.

**row 0131; string ID 23200539200262150; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> There was plenty of work for me and my crew - new houses and shops every other month. We made a fair bit of money out of it too.

**row 0132; string ID 23200539200262151; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> But-- I don't know, I guess the bubble burst or something. I don't know how these things work. People started leaving. My crew left. I stayed.

**row 1417; string ID 146845812239028224; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> Hey, thanks again for helping me move out here. I've managed to get myself set up now.

**row 1418; string ID 146845812264194048; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> If you ever need to use the dock or [C0]store[/C0] stuff here, be my guest.

**row 1664; string ID 161672749415321600; source label BUILDER_NAME_KEY**

> I found this while I was unpacking. You should take it.


## Executive (22 strings)

**row 2270; string ID 374820754201354240; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> And we're back on track. While he overstepped his corporate responsibility, it appears the Foreman was right to enlist your assistance.

**row 2273; string ID 374820754201354243; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> MAYBE THE EXECUTIVE WILL HAVE SOME DIALOGUE HERE TOO.

**row 2274; string ID 374820754201354244; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Excellent. Remember Fisherman, I'll permit the workers to [C1]upgrade your hull[/C1] with our tech here.

**row 2283; string ID 375044890999812101; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> What do you want?

**row 2284; string ID 375044890999812102; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> What else? I don't have all day.

**row 2288; string ID 375044890999812106; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> The corporation has an interest in every region. Over the next few years, you'll come to understand.

**row 2289; string ID 375044890999812107; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Whatever he likes.

**row 2290; string ID 375044890999812108; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Well, I don't see it any more, do you?

**row 2291; string ID 375044890999812109; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> No. We're close now. And it knows it. How long will it take for the drill to break through to the [C3]next layer[/C3]?

**row 2395; string ID 380189447722737667; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> But I think you know how this works by now. You'll have to gather the materials yourself.

**row 2430; string ID 380189447726931991; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Trust you? You, who couldn't even keep shipments in order, who involved locals, just to save your own hide?

**row 2432; string ID 380189447726931993; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> You, who put this whole operation in jeopardy?!

**row 2433; string ID 380189447726931994; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Enough of this! Your concerns end at steel and scaffolding; the future of this corporation is for me alone to decide!

**row 2434; string ID 380189447726931995; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Fire it up.

**row 2436; string ID 380189447726931997; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> We're almost there. Give it another push. How much power can we give this thing?

**row 2448; string ID 380189447726932009; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> There's one final building that needs to be constructed on this platform - [C0]Fleet Services[/C0]. Without this building, we're unable to scale our efforts efficiently.

**row 2449; string ID 380189447726932010; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> I'll cut you a deal. If you assist in the construction of this building, I'll permit the workers to fit your vessel with a [C1]significant hull upgrade[/C1].

**row 2450; string ID 380189447726932011; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Get on with it.

**row 2455; string ID 380189447726932016; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Get it done.

**row 2536; string ID 380861164581396480; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> After that, I don't want to see you around here any more. Our operation is confidential.

**row 2576; string ID 384982415805620226; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> Honestly, even if I cared to know myself, I'm not sure why you think I'd tell you.

**row 2578; string ID 384982415805620228; source label EXECUTIVE_NAME_KEY**

> My "people"? These are employees.


## Trader (22 strings)

**row 0051; string ID 7258692250570752; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Hello? Is that...no - ah, I don't believe we've met. Forgive me, my eyes aren't what they used to be.

**row 0052; string ID 7258692250570753; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> I specialize in antiques and jewellery. I'll [C1]purchase any special trinkets[/C1] you happen to have.

**row 0053; string ID 7258692250570754; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Have you got anything nice with you today, perhaps?

**row 0054; string ID 7258692250570755; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Welcome! Found anything special lately?

**row 0167; string ID 36290924571070466; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> A returning customer! Please, take this as a symbol of my appreciation. I'm sure you'll find it useful.

**row 1227; string ID 131218274611548160; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, um, hello. What can I do for you today?

**row 1231; string ID 131218274636713987; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Some years ago I...acquired this other piece.

**row 1232; string ID 131218274636713988; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> You found this near those [C0]ruins in Devil's Spine[/C0] didn't you?

**row 1235; string ID 131218274636713991; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Ah, well I suspect there will be [C0]more out there[/C0]. I'll hold onto this for now and study it further while you search for the other fragments.

**row 1237; string ID 131218274636713993; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmm...judging by the age of the stone and the shape of the glyph, these are part of the same set.

**row 1238; string ID 131218274636713994; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll keep this for now until you come across any others. It looks like they join together to form a square, so hopefully there's just [C1]one more to find[/C1].

**row 1241; string ID 131218274636713997; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Mhmm...yes. Go on...

**row 1243; string ID 131218274636714001; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> I only know of one "[C0]guiding light[/C0]", but these tablets predate the lighthouse at Greater Marrow by centuries.

**row 1244; string ID 131218274636714002; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> The fragments are fused together now. Why don't you take this back? In theory, they should open a lock somewhere. But where? Hmm...

**row 1245; string ID 131218274636714003; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> An intriguing mystery - do let me know if you make any progress.

**row 1246; string ID 131218274636714004; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, no room? Come back whenever you like - I'll hold onto it for you.

**row 1247; string ID 131218274636714005; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, you've got another? Wonderful. Please, let me see.

**row 1259; string ID 131224910424100864; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> In the meantime...do you have any trinkets for me to appraise?

**row 1260; string ID 131224910428295168; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Excellent! Lets see what you have.

**row 1261; string ID 131527725935677440; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> It opened a door in that ruined lighthouse? Yes, that makes sense... Well, I hope you found some interesting items for me to take a look at!

**row 1705; string ID 164879788409016320; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> When the sky mistakes the stars,<br>And the air grows thick with night,<br>The deep will open its scars,<br>Protect us, O guiding light.

**row 1719; string ID 167852613935747072; source label TRADER_NAME_KEY**

> Remarkable...yes, I do believe this is a [C0]fragment of a larger arrangement[/C0].


## Founder (19 strings)

**row 2180; string ID 347566488248283143; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Place isn't built yet.

**row 2181; string ID 347566488248283144; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Basic materials can be worked here.

**row 2182; string ID 347566488248283145; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> You find any excess crates, I'm sure we can come to some sort of arrangement.

**row 2184; string ID 347566488248283147; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Good to go.

**row 2186; string ID 347566488248283149; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Should be able to make some better stuff now.

**row 2187; string ID 347566488248283150; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> You want that made, I'll be needing some things from you.

**row 2188; string ID 347566488248283151; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Mmh. That's done.

**row 2189; string ID 347566488248283152; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Take your stuff with you next time.

**row 2400; string ID 380189447722737672; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Mmmh. Yep. Foundry's fine.

**row 2498; string ID 380447089011564544; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Still around, huh?

**row 2501; string ID 380447089028341762; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> We're short on materials. Once they arrive, we can start working. 

**row 2512; string ID 380550693303197704; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't like these waters. But the corp seems to.

**row 2513; string ID 380550693303197705; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Some advice, while you're on corp property: don't go poking around.

**row 2514; string ID 380550693303197706; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> The work's hard, but as long as the pay's good, I'll do it. And it is.

**row 2515; string ID 380550693303197707; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Not much for small talk. Let's get on with it.

**row 2516; string ID 380550693303197708; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Heat and metal. Beats the cold winds out here.

**row 2517; string ID 380550693303197709; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Don't have the materials for this place yet.

**row 2525; string ID 380836352882483203; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> Those tentacles seem angry. Not surprised.

**row 2526; string ID 380836352882483204; source label FOUNDER_NAME_KEY**

> [C1]Foundry's about to get bigger soon[/C1]. Still waiting on a delivery though.


## Grieving Father (18 strings)

**row 0056; string ID 7275844126969860; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> You - oh. Well, maybe that wasn't his... I'd - I'd better go and check before he melts it down!

**row 0059; string ID 7275844126969863; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> This is...this is his! Oh, thank you, bless you. You've returned him to me...

**row 0175; string ID 42755149529272324; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Please, take this. I've not any money to pay you, but this might hold some value to you.

**row 0324; string ID 66696833407193088; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Hello. Is everything ok?

**row 0325; string ID 66696833423970304; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Anything else I can help you with?

**row 0330; string ID 66696833423970309; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> I've told you all I know.

**row 0331; string ID 66696833423970310; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Well, yes, you see...my only son was lost at sea some years ago.

**row 0332; string ID 66696833423970311; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> He's...still out there, somewhere. In one of those wrecks. All alone in the cold dark water.

**row 0334; string ID 66696833423970313; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> I was wondering...I notice you have some dredging equipment installed on your vessel...

**row 0335; string ID 66696833423970314; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> If you're able to retrieve any of his belongings from the wreck...I think he'd feel more at peace if his mementos were with me.

**row 0336; string ID 66696833423970315; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> He wore a [C0]Bronze Belt Buckle[/C0], specially engraved. I'd recognize it anywhere. If there's a chance you could find it, I'd be in your debt.

**row 0337; string ID 66696833423970316; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> I don't get out a lot anymore, so I don't see much of what goes on.

**row 0338; string ID 66696833423970317; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> But I do remember the Old Mayor in his final days. What an awful sight it was.

**row 0339; string ID 66696833423970318; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Screaming and throwing things into the sea. "[C3]Throw it back,[/C3]" he would cry, over and over.

**row 0340; string ID 66696833423970319; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Begging anyone who would listen. Howling it to the wind. These days, I almost feel like I understand.

**row 0342; string ID 66696833423970321; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> You - oh. Well...perhaps that's his way of saying he wishes to remain at sea.

**row 0343; string ID 66696833423970322; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Thank you for your efforts. Truly.

**row 0345; string ID 66705580196171777; source label GRIEVING_FATHER_NAME_KEY**

> Please, bring it to me. I need to know if it's his.


## Captain's Log (17 strings)

**row 1960; string ID 299388449018769418; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> The vessel was pursued by a particularly [C2]aggressive Narwhal[/C2]. Some men [C0]placed fish on the ice nearby[/C0] in an effort to distract it.

**row 1961; string ID 299388449018769419; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> It appeared to work, though only for a time. It resurfaced and needed satiating repeatedly.

**row 1962; string ID 299388449018769420; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> If we can't find a more permanent solution to the beast, we'll have to make sure we keep [C0]enough fish on hand to placate it[/C0].

**row 2080; string ID 302183418167193609; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> Arrived at the floating sea-ice amidst a particularly thick fog. A few channels could be seen leading into the ice, though sizeable chunks float in our way, posing a hazard.

**row 2081; string ID 302183418167193610; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> I have ordered us to moor against the outer ice wall while the engineers make adjustments to better prepare the vessel.

**row 2082; string ID 302183418167193611; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> Adjustments were completed and we began maneuvering through the closest channel.

**row 2083; string ID 302183418167193612; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> After a time, the narrow channel opened up into a large lagoon. During the night, some of the men reported seeing lights coming from under the ice.

**row 2084; string ID 302183418167193613; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> We shall establish a temporary encampment on an island in the center of the lagoon to see if we can excavate the source.

**row 2085; string ID 302183418167193614; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> Setup of the encampment is complete, and some surface samples were gathered. No trace of the glowing light could be found.

**row 2086; string ID 302183418167193615; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> Perhaps we will dig deeper tomorrow, though I hesitate to spend any more time on this endeavour. Our purview is geographical charting.

**row 2087; string ID 302183418167193616; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> As I lay falling asleep, the most almighty voice spoke unto me. It resounded from the thick ice below, at once both powerful and ethereal.

**row 2088; string ID 302183418167193617; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> There was no mistaking this as a trick of the wind, nor a jest by the crew. It did not pause for response or acknowledgement, but whispered a [C3]cryptic prophesy[/C3].

**row 2089; string ID 302183418167193618; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> At first, I could not make sense of it - "[C3]Three listeners shall hear the heart[/C3]", and that I should indulge these "[C3]listeners[/C3]".

**row 2090; string ID 302183418167193619; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> It spoke of "[C1]boundless wealth from the stars[/C1]" and of "[C0]eyes opening unto the world[/C0]".

**row 2091; string ID 302183418167193620; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> The rest of my sleep was feverish, as my mind was plagued with the sound of the voice. It spun in my head, echoing ever louder.

**row 2092; string ID 302183418167193621; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> In the morning, the [C0]Boatswain[/C0] and the [C0]Navigator[/C0] came to me separately, claiming to have heard a [C3]sound like a heartbeat[/C3] as the men were digging yesterday.

**row 2093; string ID 302183418167193622; source label CAPTAINS_LOG_NAME_KEY**

> They indicated a desire to explore the [C0]Eastern[/C0] and [C0]Western[/C0] reaches of the ice field. I shall indulge them.


## Dog (16 strings)

**row 0695; string ID 89155828191514634; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You jump off the boat into thigh-high water. The dog stops chasing fish and focuses on you.

**row 0700; string ID 89155828191514639; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> The dog wags its tail briefly, but doesn't move.

**row 0702; string ID 89155828191514641; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You throw some scraps into the water near the dog. It pounces at the splash, and swallows sea water.

**row 0704; string ID 89155828191514643; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You wade towards the shore. As you get nearer, the dog backs up a little ways. It sits.

**row 0708; string ID 89155828191514647; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You start to walk closer to the dog, but it runs away nervously and hides in some bushes. After a few moments, it emerges again.

**row 0711; string ID 89155828191514650; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You throw some scraps towards the dog, which land in the sand. The dog eats them, along with a mouthful of sand.

**row 0713; string ID 89155828191514652; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You make soft, encouraging noises, and the dog cautiously approaches. It stops just out of your reach.

**row 0715; string ID 89155828191514654; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You extend your arm out towards the dog. It shies away and retreats up the shoreline. It looks at you expectantly, and licks its lips.

**row 0717; string ID 89155828191514656; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You move closer to the dog. It seems startled, and runs back up the shoreline.

**row 0719; string ID 89155828191514658; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You call out to the dog softly, but it doesn't seem to want to come any closer.

**row 0722; string ID 89155828191514661; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You hold out some fish scraps in the palm of your hand. The dog approaches slowly, but soon begins licking at them.

**row 0725; string ID 89155828191514664; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You reach out and gently pat the dog. Its eyes follow your hand, and it seems to understand you're not a threat.

**row 0726; string ID 89155828191514665; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You pat the dog again. It seems to be enjoying it.

**row 0728; string ID 89155828191514667; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> The dog is standing a few paces behind you. It tilts its head questioningly.

**row 0735; string ID 89155828191514674; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> You lift the dog onto the deck. It walks around briefly, before curling into a ball and looking up at you. Maybe you could find this dog a more permanent home.

**row 0738; string ID 89448783928168448; source label DOG_NAME_KEY**

> Suddenly, the dog stands upright and begins to retch, its stomach heaving. Seconds later, it vomits a wet glob onto the floor. [C1]Something glistens through the mucus[/C1].


## Courier (15 strings)

**row 1202; string ID 130128175266189315; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> [S1]Who goes there?[/S1]

**row 1205; string ID 130128175266189318; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> Very funny.

**row 1208; string ID 130128175266189321; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> What do you want?

**row 1210; string ID 130128175266189323; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> Do I seem ok?! No, I am very much NOT ok. I'm supposed to be [C0]delivering this package to Little Marrow[/C0]. But I'm being stalked!

**row 1211; string ID 130128175266189324; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> A [C3]huge leviathan[/C3] lurks in these deep waters - I've seen it! It hung in the water, its gaping mouth just below the boat!

**row 1212; string ID 130128175266189325; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> It would've swallowed this vessel whole if I hadn't bolted for the shallows. I'm not going any further into this accursed island chain!

**row 1215; string ID 130128175266189328; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> You would? Well, that would work, I guess.

**row 1216; string ID 130128175266189329; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, you won't take it after all? Just my luck.

**row 1218; string ID 130128175266189331; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> Thank you, stranger. This is bound for the [C0]Dockworker at Little Marrow[/C0].

**row 1219; string ID 130128175266189332; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> I'm not sure what's inside, but...listen, I know this sounds crazy...sometimes I think I can hear the [C3]faintest of whispers[/C3] coming from it.

**row 1220; string ID 130128175266189333; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> Now, I'm not supposed to be transporting live goods - don't have the equipment for it. So just...be careful with this thing, whatever it is.

**row 1221; string ID 130128175266189334; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> Here, why don't you take this, too.

**row 1223; string ID 130128175266189336; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> I'll be on my way whenever the coast is clear. Safe travels.

**row 1224; string ID 130128175266189337; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> Hmmph.

**row 1226; string ID 130131995954171904; source label COURIER_NAME_KEY**

> You again. What is it?


## Old Mayor (15 strings)

**row 0995; string ID 116750453731151873; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> [S1]Did you...did you throw it back?![/S1]

**row 0999; string ID 116750453731151877; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> The book!

**row 1003; string ID 116750453731151881; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> I'm not seeing things! It's really you, isn't it?

**row 1008; string ID 116750453731151886; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> It was him. Him and his damned wife. They dredged up that old book. But something...something cloaked in fog...followed it out. Right through the boat. [S3][C2]BOOM![/C2][/S3]

**row 1010; string ID 116750453731151888; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> It spoke. It said it was coming for our breath...that we wouldn't be needing it soon. And then darkness crashed down.

**row 1011; string ID 116750453731151889; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> An age later, some of us washed up on the shore. And him. Still clutching that book.

**row 1012; string ID 116750453731151890; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> "[C3]THROW IT BACK![/C3]" We cried, as the fog rolled ashore. We thought...surely...it can still be undone.

**row 1015; string ID 116750453731151893; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Silver and crimson! The book - covered in her blood!

**row 1018; string ID 116750453731151896; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> [S2][C3]THROW IT BACK![/C3][/S2]

**row 1020; string ID 116750453731151898; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Please. The [C0]Lighthouse Keeper[/C0]. She saw. She knows where it came from. Where it...where it needs to go.

**row 1021; string ID 116750453731151899; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> ...

**row 1026; string ID 116750453731151904; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Silver and crimson...silver and crimson...

**row 1028; string ID 116750453731151906; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> It cloaks that calamitous thing...it's evil, haven't you felt it?

**row 1029; string ID 116750453731151907; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> She saw it all. That ruinous day.

**row 1030; string ID 116750453731151908; source label OLD_MAYOR_NAME_KEY**

> Ask her. Beg her. Tell her I...


## Castaway (13 strings)

**row 0224; string ID 53986564572893184; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> I'm saved! Saved! Oh, you are a sight for these sore eyes.

**row 0227; string ID 53986564623224834; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> I bet I do! I've certainly felt better. I've been chewing on seaweed and starfish for longer than I'd like.

**row 0228; string ID 53986564623224835; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> I washed up here...oh, it must be some two weeks ago now. We were in rough seas, and when a wave hit us broadside I was jostled overboard and swept away.

**row 0229; string ID 53986564623224836; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> I thought my crew would've come to find me by now, but yours is the first ship I've seen pass by here.

**row 0230; string ID 53986564623224837; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> Did the boys send you looking for me?

**row 0232; string ID 53986564623224839; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> Oh, well, I'm sure they're out there lookin' anyways.

**row 0234; string ID 53986564623224841; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> That's a relief to hear, I'd thought for sure those sorry sacks would be halfway across the sea with my boat by now.

**row 0235; string ID 53986564623224842; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> Can you get me to [C0]Little Marrow[/C0], stranger? I should be able to catch them there when they next stop by.

**row 0238; string ID 53986564623224846; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> What, you're just going to leave me here to starve to death?! Curse you!

**row 0240; string ID 53986564623224848; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> Have you come back for me?

**row 0241; string ID 53986564623224849; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> The sweet, rank smell of civilization. I thought I could never miss it.

**row 0243; string ID 53986564623224851; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> I've not got much on me, as you can imagine. And I've a feeling this isn't worth much anymore -- for me, at least. But perhaps it'll be worth something for you.

**row 0245; string ID 53986564623224853; source label CASTAWAY_NAME_KEY**

> On to better horizons, friend. Farewell.


## Leather Journal (13 strings)

**row 2117; string ID 302212623227580439; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> The men spent the whole day digging. They passed up chunks of ice, some as brilliant as crystal, others of a deepest blue more beautiful than sapphires.

**row 2118; string ID 302212623227580440; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> But none held the glow we had seen in the night. However, I did note a disturbance in the ice to the West.

**row 2119; string ID 302212623227580441; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> It began as a periodic, distant rumble. As the men dug deeper, the pulsing quickened, strengthening until I could see the ice shifting on the surface.

**row 2120; string ID 302212623231774720; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> I shall have to inform the Captain. It may well be that Narwhal creature we saw earlier, though this did seem much larger.

**row 2121; string ID 302212623231774721; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> As I write this, I can already feel my eyes closing. It will have to wait until the morning.

**row 2122; string ID 302212623231774722; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> This morning, the Captain appeared out of the fog, rowing alone. Apparently, the Boatswain remains at a camp in the East, waiting for nightfall.

**row 2123; string ID 302212623231774723; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> They were unsuccessful in their search, but it does make sense that the lights would be more visible at night.

**row 2124; string ID 302212623231774724; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> Tomorrow, we're to journey together to the West, towards the shifting ice that I reported.

**row 2125; string ID 302212623231774725; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> He is on edge, his head turning this way and that, listening for the slightest sound. He is convinced something will happen after dark.

**row 2126; string ID 302212623231774726; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> As the last light from the cold sun fades, I have the sense that it will be a long night.

**row 2149; string ID 309823512426762240; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> We are alone on a small island of ice.

**row 2150; string ID 309823512456122368; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> After hours of rowing through narrow channels, it rose through the fog. A gargantuan, monstrous claw, reaching from the deep.

**row 2151; string ID 309823512456122369; source label LEATHER_JOURNAL_NAME_KEY**

> Whatever creature it once belonged to must be long dead, as the claw is frozen solid. I wanted to turn back immediately, but the Captain insisted we investigate.


## Soul (Middle) (12 strings)

**row 1969; string ID 299388449018769427; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> Don't... disturb... the ice...

**row 1971; string ID 299388449022963713; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> ...

**row 1976; string ID 299388449022963718; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Captain[/C0]... the expedition... chart the ice field... appeared suddenly... through the ice...

**row 1977; string ID 299388449022963719; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> It whispered... promised... stones from the stars...

**row 1978; string ID 299388449022963720; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> It knew... three would hear... the heart... beneath the ice...

**row 1980; string ID 299388449022963722; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> It commanded... three listeners... three sacrifices... for three eyes...

**row 1981; string ID 299388449022963723; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> I obeyed... but... a fourth... now sees... forever... futile...

**row 1983; string ID 299388449022963725; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> Slumbering... still... should leave... it sees you... before it whispers...

**row 1984; string ID 299388449022963726; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> Do not... risk breaking... it senses... through the ice... through the shadows... no hope...

**row 2050; string ID 300100449071951872; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> The [C0]Boatswain[/C0]... gone... fearful boy... the skies... we remain... the consequences... inherited...

**row 2051; string ID 300100449088729088; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> The [C0]Navigator[/C0]... my eyes... for the shadow... closing... you risk... annihilation... annihilation...

**row 2052; string ID 300100449088729089; source label SOUL_MIDDLE_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Officer[/C0]... unworthy... tried to take... turn the men... my expedition... my discovery... his failure...


## Soul (South) (11 strings)

**row 2017; string ID 299740934988263450; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]Find the axe[/C0]... undo this... the betrayer...

**row 2019; string ID 299740934988263452; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> Murderer... betray your men... left us here... your sleepless god...

**row 2021; string ID 299740934988263454; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> Wait... your shadow... it speaks... another shape... please... save us all...

**row 2022; string ID 299740934988263455; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> [C0]First Officer[/C0]... safety of the crew... failed them... should have... cursed place...

**row 2023; string ID 299740934988263456; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> The men... saw lights... southern cliffs... at night... Captain and I... would look into... we rowed alone...

**row 2024; string ID 299740934988263457; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> He was... restless... sleepless... we passed... walls of ice... finally arrived... the great horn... into view...

**row 2026; string ID 299740934988263459; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> Pulled ashore... quickly... the size of it... the light... somehow... he knew... eyes were wild... he was... whispering... answering...

**row 2027; string ID 299740934988263460; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> He said... it chose him... alone... avert my eyes... could not help... look upon... we fought... cold hands... an iron grip... breathless...

**row 2029; string ID 299740934988263462; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> I know not... the eyes open... watching... shadow of shadow... through the ice... yet more... closed still...

**row 2030; string ID 299740934988263463; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> It cannot... arise... break the ice... cull the heart...

**row 2031; string ID 299740934988263464; source label SOUL_SOUTH_NAME_KEY**

> Must not... fully awaken... the eyes... close them... strike the heart...


## Tattered Diary (11 strings)

**row 2100; string ID 302212623227580422; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> Captain ordered us to make camp on the ice in the center of the lagoon. After we saw those lights in the ice, everyone's got [C1]treasure[/C1] on their minds.

**row 2101; string ID 302212623227580423; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> Treasure hunting isn't exactly what the expedition was commissioned for, but given the Captain gets the lion's share of whatever we find...

**row 2102; string ID 302212623227580424; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> It doesn't surprise me that he's willing to bend the rules a bit. We'll start digging after the camp's done.

**row 2103; string ID 302212623227580425; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> Spent the day digging down in the ice. Shovels and ice picks. Can't remember the last time I was this tired.

**row 2104; string ID 302212623227580426; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> We were only allowed two short breaks all day. Down in the pit, as I rested with my back against the ice, I started hearing something.

**row 2105; string ID 302212623227580427; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> It was different to the sounds of the picks. They were short and sharp. This was low, a rumble from deeper down.

**row 2106; string ID 302212623227580428; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> On my first break, it was occasional. Only a few each minute. By my second break, it had quickened, like a heartbeat.

**row 2107; string ID 302212623227580429; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> The Captain overheard me telling some others about the sound I heard in the pit. Before I knew it, I was in front of his desk, sure I was about to have my pay docked.

**row 2108; string ID 302212623227580430; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> To my great surprise, he seemed to know something of it already. He was keen on hearing it described, over and over.

**row 2109; string ID 302212623227580431; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> He wants me to help locate the source of it. When he pressed me on it further, I said that it did seem to be coming from the East.

**row 2110; string ID 302212623227580432; source label TATTERED_DIARY_NAME_KEY**

> Tomorrow, we are setting off to see if we can find it. Perhaps I shall get a bonus after this is done!


## Soul (East) (8 strings)

**row 1996; string ID 299740934988263429; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> Please... break the ice...

**row 1998; string ID 299740934988263431; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> Help me... frozen... so many years... free me...

**row 1999; string ID 299740934988263432; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> I was... I am... the [C0]Boatswain[/C0]... the expedition... the Terror...

**row 2001; string ID 299740934988263434; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> We saw... lights... dancing in ice... dug deep... didn't know... I heard it...

**row 2002; string ID 299740934988263435; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> The heart... beating... under ice... the cracking... waking... the noises... in the east...

**row 2003; string ID 299740934988263436; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> I went... the Captain... brought him here... to find it... the hand... in the night... shadow of claws...

**row 2005; string ID 299740934988263438; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> I turned away... just a moment... the pain... the claws... my back... so cold... so dark... under ice...

**row 2006; string ID 299740934988263439; source label SOUL_EAST_NAME_KEY**

> Silence it... you must... shatter...


## Soul (West) (8 strings)

**row 2040; string ID 299740934988263473; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> Break it... somehow... free me...

**row 2042; string ID 299740934988263475; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> Trapped... forever... this prison... get me out...

**row 2043; string ID 299740934988263476; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> The [C0]Navigator[/C0]... find safe passage... chart the ice... it came from... beyond...

**row 2044; string ID 299740934988263477; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> The digging... the cracked ice... like a web... awoke... the ice heart...

**row 2045; string ID 299740934988263478; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> I told him... whispers in the west... took the Boatswain... east... returned alone...

**row 2047; string ID 299740934988263480; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> The next day... rowed here... the Captain... saw the claws... grip my soul... he insisted... made camp... shadow of the beast...

**row 2048; string ID 299740934988263481; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> Waiting for the stars... in the night... I saw... his shape... suddenly... swallowed... by the cold... in the dark... he left me...

**row 2049; string ID 299740934988263482; source label SOUL_WEST_NAME_KEY**

> This cold sun... warmth long forgot... no more... break me...

## Founder (Undermarket) (6 strings)

**row 2595; string ID 404067128942358535; source label FOUNDER_MARKET_NAME_KEY**

> If you're going to be hanging around...maybe we can do business. Come see me at the [C0]Undermarket[/C0] - alone.

**row 2596; string ID 404067128942358536; source label FOUNDER_MARKET_NAME_KEY**

> Got some [C0]items you might be interested in[/C0]. Don't ask where they come from - better off not knowing. Can make some [C0]basic repairs[/C0] too, but it'll cost ya.

**row 2597; string ID 404067128942358537; source label FOUNDER_MARKET_NAME_KEY**

> Don't tell anyone from the corp about this place. I'm doing you a favour, you know.

**row 2598; string ID 404067128942358538; source label FOUNDER_MARKET_NAME_KEY**

> Nobody ever does a thorough stocktake at big corporations. Lucky for us.

**row 2599; string ID 404067128942358539; source label FOUNDER_MARKET_NAME_KEY**

> Things go missing all the time. Who knows where they end up? Doesn't matter in a place like this.

**row 2600; string ID 404067128942358540; source label FOUNDER_MARKET_NAME_KEY**

> The bigwigs don't bother with the lower decks and the folks down here. Why would they mingle with people like us? I'm not complaining.


## Hooded Figure 1 (5 strings)

**row 0249; string ID 55076055871983617; source label HOODED_FIGURE_1_NAME_KEY**

> Fisherman...please. Help.

**row 1345; string ID 144664106987868160; source label HOODED_FIGURE_1_NAME_KEY**

> Fulfill...the hunger.

**row 1348; string ID 144664106987868163; source label HOODED_FIGURE_1_NAME_KEY**

> More. Still...empty.

**row 1351; string ID 144664106987868166; source label HOODED_FIGURE_1_NAME_KEY**

> Almost. Fulfilled.

**row 1355; string ID 144664106987868170; source label HOODED_FIGURE_1_NAME_KEY**

> Hunger...sated. Now...I wait.

**row 1361; string ID 144958400751652866; source label HOODED_FIGURE_2_NAME_KEY**

> An answer...

**row 1364; string ID 144958400751652869; source label HOODED_FIGURE_2_NAME_KEY**

> Another. A sacrifice.

**row 1367; string ID 144958400751652872; source label HOODED_FIGURE_2_NAME_KEY**

> Sense...an opening.

**row 1371; string ID 144958400751652876; source label HOODED_FIGURE_2_NAME_KEY**

**row 1378; string ID 144958400751652883; source label HOODED_FIGURE_3_NAME_KEY**

**row 1382; string ID 144958400751652887; source label HOODED_FIGURE_3_NAME_KEY**
